*Seongyong Kim,Jingdao Chen,Yong Kwon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种面向入射感知的采样策略，用于单次LiDAR扫描的3D场景分割。该方法将点映射到几何归一化流形空间进行体素选择，同时保留原始欧氏坐标用于下游学习，仅需点坐标和法线，无需修改骨干网络。在SIP基准上，使用Point Transformer和PointNeXt实验表明，该方法提高了分辨率平均的分割性能，尤其对非平面元素和梯子效果显著，同时降低了对采样分辨率的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景理解方法大多在精心整理的基准数据集上开发，未能真实反映施工现场的传感条件。单次LiDAR扫描存在表面覆盖有限、密度随采集方式变化以及平面表面与稀疏建筑元素之间的严重不平衡等问题。由于大点云必须降采样，采样分辨率和点分配直接影响几何细节与空间上下文之间的平衡，因此需要一种考虑采集特性的采样策略。

Method: 提出入射感知采样策略：将点云映射到几何归一化的流形空间中进行基于体素的选择，同时保留原始欧氏坐标用于下游学习。该方法仅需点坐标和法线作为输入，不需要修改深度学习骨干网络。在固定每片段点预算下评估不同采样策略的影响。

Result: 在Site in Pieces (SIP)基准上，使用Point Transformer和PointNeXt两种骨干网络进行实验，结果显示该方法在分辨率平均的分割性能上有所提升，尤其对非平面元素和梯子的分割效果改善明显，同时降低了对采样分辨率的敏感性。

Conclusion: 采集感知的采样策略能够提供更稳定的几何表示，应当被视为单次扫描3D分割中的主动组成部分，而非通用的预处理步骤。

Abstract: 3D scene understanding is increasingly important in construction, yet most methods are developed on curated datasets that do not fully reflect real site sensing conditions. In many workflows, individual LiDAR scans provide rapid local updates rather than complete scene representations, producing limited surface coverage, acquisition-driven density variation, and severe imbalance between dominant planar surfaces and sparse construction elements. Because large point clouds must be downsampled, sampling resolution and point allocation directly affect the balance between geometric detail and spatial context. This study evaluates these effects under a fixed per-fragment point budget and introduces an incidence-aware sampling strategy for individual LiDAR scans. The method maps points to a geometry-normalized manifold space for voxel-based selection while preserving original Euclidean coordinates for downstream learning. It requires only point coordinates and normals and no backbone modification. Using the Site in Pieces (SIP) benchmark, experiments with Point Transformer and PointNeXt show improved resolution-averaged segmentation performance, especially for non-planar elements and ladders, while reducing sensitivity to sampling resolution. The results show that acquisition-aware sampling can provide a more stable geometric representation and should be treated as an active component of individual-scan 3D segmentation rather than generic preprocessing.

</details>


### [42] [XClipGS: Exact Half-Space Clipping for Medical Volume Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07760)
*Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Meng Zheng,Anwesa Choudhuri,Chaoyi Zhou,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XClipGS提出一种基于EWA抛雪花的精确剪裁方法，通过闭式条件高斯CDF算子实现对医学体数据的半空间剪裁渲染，无额外学习参数；同时引入配对剪裁/未剪裁切面协议和两个新指标（CDE和Leak）来监督和评估内部结构，在精度和速度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯溅射代理虽能交互式渲染医学体数据，但剪裁平面会暴露未经过外部视角训练约束的解剖结构，且传统溅射方法只能整体保留或丢弃图元，无法精确处理与剪裁平面相交的高斯，导致渲染不精确。

Method: 将问题分解为渲染时剪裁算子和内部结构监督两个子问题。在EWA溅射的局部仿射模型下，半空间约束高斯的光线积分可精确分解为普通二维脚印与条件高斯CDF的乘积，其参数是像素坐标的仿射函数，从而得到闭式逐像素算子，无需学习参数且对图元和平面可微。通过多距离参考视图（变化剪裁平面轴和偏移）监督内部，并引入配对剪裁/未剪裁切面协议及两个指标（CDE和Leak）解决全局指标稀释问题。

Result: 在八个CT和MRI体数据上，使用训练未见的平面偏移，XClipGS在所有体数据上取得最高PSNR（33.56 dB对比ClipGS的32.34 dB），渲染速度超650 FPS（远超实时），而ClipGS为278 FPS。在体素轴切面视图上，平均带SSIM从0.809提升到0.860，泄漏量减少约40倍；无需重训即可在任意法向平面上取得四个指标的最佳平均；固定内部结构时，面保真度与RaRa相当而泄漏量减少约16倍。

Conclusion: XClipGS通过精确闭式剪裁算子和针对性内部监督协议，在医学体数据剪裁渲染中同时实现了更高的保真度、更低的泄漏以及远超实时的渲染速度，为高斯溅射在医学可视化中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Gaussian-splatting proxies enable interactive rendering of volumetric medical scans, but a clipping plane exposes anatomy not constrained by external-view training and intersects primitives that conventional splatting can only keep or drop whole. We present XClipGS (eXact Clipping), which treats these as two separate problems: the render-time clip operator and supervision of the hidden interior. Under the local affine model used by EWA splatting, the ray integral of a half-space-restricted Gaussian factorizes exactly into its ordinary 2D footprint and a conditional Gaussian CDF whose argument is affine in pixel coordinates. The resulting closed-form per-pixel operator introduces no learned clipping parameters or auxiliary network and remains differentiable with respect to the primitive and plane. We use multi-distance reference views with varied clipping-plane axes and offsets to supervise the interior through the same operator. We also introduce a paired clipped/unclipped cut-face protocol with difference-referenced cut error (CDE) and culled-side leakage (Leak), because global image metrics dilute errors near the plane. On eight CT and MRI volumes with plane offsets not used for training, XClipGS attains the highest PSNR on every volume (33.56 versus 32.34 dB for ClipGS) while rendering at over 650 FPS, far above real time, versus 278 FPS. On voxel-axis cut-face views, it raises average band SSIM from 0.809 to 0.860 and leaks roughly 40 times less. Without retraining, it also achieves the best average across all four metrics on arbitrary-normal planes; on a fixed interior, it matches RaRa's face fidelity with about 16 times less leakage. Project page: https://gaozhongpai.github.io/XClipGS/

</details>


### [43] [DINO-3DRA: Leveraging 2D Foundation Model Semantics for 3D Cerebral Aneurysm Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07767)
*Jiayang Lu,Fengming Lin,Alejandro F. Frangi,Ali Sarrami-Foroushani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DINO-3DRA，一种双路径框架，通过将冻结的DINOv3二维视觉基础模型特征注入三维U-Net骨干网络，实现跨维度语义迁移，在三维旋转血管造影（3DRA）动脉瘤分割中达到最优性能（Dice: 0.758，HD95: 2.75mm），仅需5.72M可训练参数，且完全消除基线架构中的灾难性失败案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维旋转血管造影（3DRA）中的动脉瘤分割面临三大挑战：极端类别不平衡、动脉瘤与血管形态高度相似、以及缺乏大规模三维预训练模型。虽然二维视觉基础模型（如DINOv3）编码了来自17亿图像的密集结构先验，但简单的切片级迁移会破坏解剖连续性并导致优化不稳定，因此需要一种有效的跨维度语义迁移方法。

Method: 提出DINO-3RDA双路径框架，核心思路是将冻结的DINOv3二维基础模型特征注入三维U-Net骨干网络，通过两个关键模块实现跨维度迁移：(1) Room-Lite空间混合模块，用于跨维度特征对齐；(2) 校准残差融合模块，用于稳定地将基础模型特征与三维特征融合。

Result: 在多中心3DRA数据上，DINO-3DRA实现了SOTA动脉瘤分割性能（Dice: 0.758；HD95: 2.75mm；相比nnU-Net提升13%），仅需5.72M可训练参数。消融实验证实性能提升源于结构化跨维度迁移而非单纯损失函数设计。无需在CADA和SHINY-ICARUS上微调即可消除基线架构中所有灾难性失败案例，证明了跨异构成像协议的鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: DINO-3RDA证明了二维视觉基础模型的特征可以有效桥接至三维医学图像分割任务，通过结构化跨维度迁移显著提升动脉瘤分割精度和跨数据集泛化能力，且参数效率极高，为三维医学影像中利用大规模二维预训练模型提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Accurate aneurysm segmentation in 3D rotational angiography (3DRA) is hindered by extreme class imbalance, morphological similarity to vessels, and absent large-scale 3D pretraining. 2D vision foundation models encode dense structural priors from 1.7 billion images, yet naïve slice-wise transfer fragments anatomical continuity and destabilises optimisation. We propose DINO-3DRA, a dual-path framework achieving effective cross-dimensional semantic transfer by injecting frozen DINOv3 features into a 3D U-Net backbone via Room-Lite spatial mixing and calibrated residual fusion. On multi-centre 3DRA data, DINO-3DRA achieves state-of-the-art aneurysm segmentation (Dice: 0.758; HD95: 2.75 mm; +13% over nnU-Net) with only 5.72M trainable parameters. Ablation studies confirm that gains arise from structured cross-dimensional transfer rather than loss design alone, with bridged foundation features improving anatomical continuity between aneurysms and parent vessels. Without fine-tuning on CADA and SHINY-ICARUS, DINO-3DRA eliminates all catastrophic failure cases observed in baseline architectures, demonstrating robust generalisation across heterogeneous imaging protocols.

</details>


### [44] [SeqLoc: Beyond the Single Frame for Cross-View Geo-Localization in Feature-Sparse Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07835)
*Junwei Zheng,Yun Huang,Ruize Dai,Ruiping Liu,Yufan Chen,Kunyu Peng,Kailun Yang,Jiaming Zhang,Guangming Wang,Olaf Wysocki,Rainer Stiefelhagen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CV-FSS基准数据集和SeqLoc方法，解决跨视角地理定位（CVGL）在特征稀疏场景（如乡村道路）中性能崩溃的问题。SeqLoc通过在线测试时序列聚合机制，利用熵温控不确定性、地图引导重定位和峰值锚定平滑三个关键组件，在位置和方向召回率上均提升超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨视角地理定位方法在结构丰富的城市环境中表现良好，但在特征稀疏的乡村道路等场景中会显著退化甚至完全崩溃。这一问题尚未被系统研究，因此需要一个专门针对此类失败模式的基准和解决方案。

Method: 作者首先构建CV-FSS基准，包含五个乡村区域的连续全景序列与对齐的OSM地图。随后提出SeqLoc方法，这是一个在线测试时序列聚合机制，递归维护一个对数信念体积，包含三个关键组件：(1) 熵温控不确定性（ETU），通过归一化熵对每个输入姿态似然体积进行温度调节；(2) 地图引导重定位（MGR），将地图形状的恢复分布融入信念中，使被抑制的真实姿态得以恢复；(3) 峰值锚定平滑（PAS），以亚网格精度推导最终姿态。

Result: 在CV-FSS和CV-RHO基准上的大量实验表明，SeqLoc相比单帧定位方法有大幅提升，位置和方向召回率均改善超过50%。

Conclusion: SeqLoc有效解决了CVGL在特征稀疏场景下的失败模式，证明了测试时序列聚合是提升稳健性的有效策略。基准和源代码将公开，为该领域的后续研究提供基础。

Abstract: Cross-View Geo-Localization (CVGL) with OpenStreetMap (OSM) performs well in structure-rich urban environments but collapses in feature-sparse scenes such as rural roads. To study this failure mode, in this work, we introduce CV-FSS, a benchmark that pairs sequential panoramas from five rural regions with aligned OSM maps, on which single-frame methods degrade drastically. We then propose SeqLoc, an online test-time sequence aggregation mechanism that recursively maintains a log-belief volume with three key components: (1) Entropy-Tempered Uncertainty (ETU) tempers each incoming pose likelihood volume by its normalized entropy; (2) Map-Guided Relocalization (MGR) mixes a map-shaped recovery distribution into the belief so that a suppressed true pose can recover; (3) Peak-Anchored Smoothing (PAS) derives the final pose at sub-grid precision. Extensive experiments on CV-FSS and CV-RHO demonstrate that SeqLoc outperforms single-frame localization by a large margin, improving both position and orientation recall by over 50%. The benchmark and source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [45] [IRPol-Fuse: Energy-structure coordination for infrared polarization fusion under low visibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07848)
*Zhuangfan Huang,Chusheng Fang,Xiaosong Li,Yang Liua,Xiaoqi Cheng,Haishu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IRPol-Fuse，一种面向低能见度场景的红外-偏振图像融合框架，通过能量与结构协调策略，在保留红外热目标显著性的同时恢复偏振纹理细节，并构建了LI-PI评估数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外-偏振图像融合方法过度强调红外主导响应，导致暗区域中微弱但信息丰富的偏振纹理被抑制，影响低能见度条件下的感知质量。

Method: 提出IRPol-Fuse框架，包含三个关键模块：偏振注意力融合模块实现红外-偏振自适应分配，红外高亮注入模块进行高亮引导的红外保留，偏振纹理注入模块负责偏振纹理恢复与细节重建。

Result: 在构建的LI-PI数据集和公开LDDRS数据集上的实验表明，IRPol-Fuse在热目标保留、结构细节恢复和视觉自然度方面表现良好；区域感知评估和下游目标检测进一步验证了能量-结构协调策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 能量-结构协调策略能够有效同时保留红外目标显著性和偏振结构信息，为低能见度场景的稳健感知提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Robust perception under low-visibility conditions requires fused imagery that jointly preserves infrared thermal saliency and polarization-derived structural details. However, existing infrared-polarization image fusion (IPIF) methods often overemphasize dominant infrared responses, causing weak yet informative polarization textures in dark regions to be suppressed. To address this issue, we propose IRPol-Fuse, an energy-structure coordinated IPIF framework for challenging low-visibility scenarios. The proposed framework contains three key modules: Polarization Attention Fusion for adaptive infrared-polarization allocation, Infrared Highlight Injector for highlight-guided infrared preservation, and Polarization Texture Injector for polarization texture restoration and fine-detail recovery. We further construct LI-PI, a dedicated infrared-polarization evaluation dataset for low-visibility and visually concealed scenes. Experiments on LI-PI and the public LDDRS dataset demonstrate that IRPol-Fuse achieves favorable performance in thermal target preservation, structural detail recovery, and visual naturalness. Region-aware evaluation and downstream object detection further verify that the proposed energy-structure coordination strategy effectively preserves both infrared target saliency and polarization-derived structural information. Code is available at https://github.com/1hzf/IRPolar-Fuse .

</details>


### [46] [Distilling CT Foundation Models into Editable Concept Bottlenecks for Lung Nodule Malignancy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07857)
*Fakrul Islam Tushar,Stephen Adamo,Geoffrey D. Rubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了基于两个冻结CT基础模型表征的概念瓶颈模型，将肺部结节映射为八个放射科医生定义的属性，并结合结节大小预测恶性肿瘤。模型在内部测试集中AUROC达0.86，外部验证集为0.72-0.73，与仅用结节大小的表现相当，且提供了可分解、可干预的透明预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型提供的CT表征具有很强的迁移性，但直接基于这些嵌入的预测结果难以解释，缺乏临床可理解性，亟需一种能够将基础模型表征转换为可解释临床属性的方法。

Method: 采用概念瓶颈模型，将两个冻结的CT基础模型表征（CT-FM和FMCIB）映射到八个放射科医生定义的肺结节属性，通过八个岭回归概念头训练于2,610个LIDC-IDRI结节，再结合结节大小预测恶性程度，并评估概念保真度和恶性判别能力。

Result: 概念保真度中等，FMCIB在微妙性（R²=0.24 vs 0.11）、棘状突起（0.17 vs 0.08）、纹理（0.17 vs 0.07）和分叶（0.15 vs 0.05）上优于CT-FM。内部AUROC均为0.86，外部为0.72-0.73，与仅用结节大小（0.73）相当，优于纯嵌入探针（0.60/0.67）。

Conclusion: 概念瓶颈模型提供了透明的恶性预测，判别能力与结节大小相当，但概念保真度的差异表明概念恢复效果依赖于底层基础模型的表征方式。

Abstract: Foundation models provide transferable CT representations, but predictions based directly on these embeddings are difficult to interpret. We developed concept bottleneck models that map two frozen CT foundation-model representations to eight radiologist-defined pulmonary-nodule attributes and predict malignancy from the estimated concepts and nodule size. The models included CT-FM, a whole-CT self-supervised encoder using a 96^3-voxel nodule-centered patch, and FMCIB, a nodule-focused contrastive encoder using a 50-mm crop. Eight ridge-regression concept heads were trained on 2,610 LIDC-IDRI nodules. Malignancy models were trained on LUNA25 and evaluated on a held-out internal test set and the external DLCS cohort. Concept fidelity was assessed using five-fold cross-validated R^2, and malignancy discrimination was assessed using AUROC with 95% confidence intervals estimated by patient-grouped bootstrap resampling. Concept fidelity was modest but higher for FMCIB than CT-FM for subtlety (R2, 0.24 vs. 0.11), spiculation (0.17 vs. 0.08), texture (0.17 vs. 0.07), and lobulation (0.15 vs. 0.05). Internally, the CT-FM and FMCIB concept+size models achieved AUROCs of 0.86 (95% CI, 0.80-0.92) and 0.86 (0.79-0.92), respectively. Externally, AUROCs were 0.72 (0.68-0.75) and 0.73 (0.70-0.76), compared with 0.73 for nodule size alone and 0.60 and 0.67 for the corresponding embedding only probes. Additive predictions could be decomposed into feature-level contributions and modified through controlled concept interventions. Concept bottlenecks provided transparent malignancy predictions with discrimination similar to nodule size alone, while differences in concept fidelity suggest that concept recovery depends on the underlying foundation-model representation.

</details>


### [47] [How Much Does It Cost to Answer My Question? Benchmarking Cloud VLM-based VQA Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07861)
*Henri Vanhuynegem,Weitao Xu,Yiran Shen,Guohao Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VQABench是首个系统研究客户端输入预处理对云VLM视觉问答系统影响的基准测试，通过评估12种预处理技术在3个数据集、4个商业VLM上的95,168次API调用，发现预处理并非普遍有益，其效果取决于目标模型、API范式、提供商计费规则和任务设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM难以在移动端运行，VQA系统将推理卸载到云端，客户端图像预处理成为关键优化空间。然而，预处理技术的成本-质量影响从未在商业云VLM上被系统研究过。

Method: 提出了VQABench基准测试框架，将客户端输入预处理作为可控变量，系统评估12种预处理技术在3个VQA数据集和4个商业VLM（来自3个提供商）上的表现，共进行95,168次API调用。

Result: 预处理并非普遍有益：其有效性依赖于目标模型、API范式、提供商token计费规则和任务公式化方式。选择不当的预处理策略可能增加部署成本或延迟，同时降低答案准确率。

Conclusion: 该基准测试阐明了预处理策略何时有效、何时失效及原因，为VQA系统的未来研究和实际部署提供了指导性见解。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are becoming a practical backend for mobile visual question answering (VQA) systems, enabling smartphones and smart glasses to answer users' questions about the physical world. Since modern VLMs remain difficult to run on mobile and edge devices, VQA systems increasingly offload inference to cloud-based VLMs. This gives mobile devices access to stronger computation, but it also makes visual input preparation a key system variable: how the image is prepared before offloading affects not only answer quality but also payload size, token cost, and system latency. Proprietary APIs expose little control over model internals or serving behavior, leaving client-side preprocessing as the main practical optimization space for downstream developers. Many such techniques have been proposed for visual offloading, yet their cost-quality impact on commercial cloud VLMs has never been studied. To fill this gap, we present VQABench, the first systematic benchmark that treats client-side input preprocessing as a controlled variable for cloud-VLM-based VQA. We evaluate 12 preprocessing techniques across three VQA datasets and four commercial VLMs from three providers, totaling 95,168 API calls. Our results show that preprocessing is not universally beneficial: its effectiveness depends on the target model, API paradigm, provider token-accounting rule, and task formulation. A poorly selected preprocessing strategy can increase deployment cost or latency while degrading answer accuracy. Overall, our benchmark clarifies when preprocessing helps, when it fails, and why, providing insights to guide future research and real-world deployment of VQA systems.

</details>


### [48] [LHSDet: High-Resolution AI-Generated Image Detection via Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07863)
*Qian Yao,Jun-Jie Huang,Yongjun Wang,Luming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LHSDet，一种基于视觉问答框架的高分辨率AI生成图像检测器，通过三分支架构（低层视觉分支捕获局部纹理、高层视觉分支基于SigLIP2提取全局特征、语义文本分支使用BLIP-2生成描述）实现多模态特征融合，在多种扩散模型和自回归模型上取得了高检测精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测方法通常对图像进行下采样，忽略了高分辨率图像中关键的低层纹理细节，限制了检测性能；同时，未知生成模型的不断涌现使得大规模预训练数据集难以获取。这两个挑战促使研究者探索无需下采样且能适应未知模型的新检测方法。

Method: LHSDet将AI生成图像检测任务建模为视觉问答问题，采用三分支架构：(1) 低层视觉分支，聚合非重叠图像块以捕获局部纹理线索；(2) 高层视觉分支，基于SigLIP2提取全局感知特征；(3) 语义级文本分支，使用BLIP-2生成图像描述。通过重新设计的视觉编码器（适应AI生成图像检测任务）和多模态特征融合实现检测。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，LHSDet在多种生成模型（包括扩散模型和自回归模型）上均取得了高检测精度和鲁棒的检测性能。

Conclusion: LHSDet通过充分利用视觉与文本模态的互补信息，并兼顾低层纹理细节与高层语义特征，有效解决了高分辨率AI生成图像检测中因下采样导致的信息损失问题，为应对不断涌现的未知生成模型提供了可行的检测方案。

Abstract: Driven by advances in diffusion models and autoregressive models, the fidelity and resolution of AI-generated images now rival those of real images. However, existing AI-generated image detection methods often downsample the images, inevitably overlooking critical low-level texture details in high-resolution AI-generated images, therefore limiting their detection performance. In addition, the ceaseless emergence of unknown generative models makes large-scale pre-training datasets inaccessible. To address these challenges, we propose a novel high-resolution AI-generated image detector, termed LHSDet. Specifically, we formulate the AI-generated image detection task as a Visual Question Answering problem, leveraging a fine-tuned vision-language framework to fully exploit the complementary information between visual and textual modalities. Recognizing that the default visual encoder of existing vision-language models is not tailored for AI-generated image detection, we redesign a visual encoder to better capture both the low-level and high-level artifacts inherent in AI-generated images. Furthermore, we incorporate a semantic-level textual branch to enable multi-modal feature fusion and detection. Consequently, LHSDet employs a triple-branch architecture to extract complementary multi-modal features: a low-level visual branch that aggregates non-overlapping patches for local texture cues, a high-level visual branch based on SigLIP2 for global perception feature extraction, and a semantic-level textual branch that generates captions using BLIP-2. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LHSDet achieves high detection accuracy and robust performance across diverse generative models, including both diffusion and autoregressive models.

</details>


### [49] [UniScale: Arbitrary-Scale Industrial Anomaly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07864)
*Shilei Zeng,Linxin Guan,Xurui Li,Yaohan Tang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniScale，一个统一的工业异常生成训练与推理框架，旨在解决扩散模型在处理小尺度异常时因极端下采样导致信息丢失的问题。通过误差抑制多尺度训练（EMT）策略和生成后融合去噪（Generation-then-Fusion Denoising）推理方法，UniScale在VisA和MVTec AD 2数据集上均实现了异常生成质量和下游检测性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测面临真实异常样本稀缺的挑战，虽然生成模型可用于合成异常数据，但现有方法在处理小尺度异常时表现不佳，原因是扩散模型中的极端下采样导致小异常信息在潜在空间中丢失。

Method: 提出UniScale框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 训练阶段采用误差抑制多尺度训练（EMT）策略，学习丰富的位置感知异常纹理，同时抑制上采样插值误差；(2) 推理阶段采用生成后融合去噪方法，将异常生成与背景集成解耦，防止小尺度异常被淹没。

Result: 在VisA上IS(a)相对提升45.86%（从1.81到2.64），在MVTec AD 2上提升37.70%（从1.22到1.68）；下游像素级IoU在VisA上提升4.22%，AUROC在MVTec AD 2上提升6.55%。

Conclusion: UniScale在异常生成质量和下游检测性能上均优于现有最先进方法，有效解决了小尺度工业异常生成难题，代码已开源。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly inspection faces a major challenge due to the lack of real-world anomaly samples. While generative models are used to create anomaly data, existing methods still struggle when handling small-scale anomalies.This failure occurs because extreme downsampling in diffusion models causes the information of small anomalies to be lost in the latent space.To address this, we introduce UniScale, a unified training and inference framework for high-fidelity industrial anomaly generation across arbitrary scales.During training, we introduce an Error-Suppressed Multi-Scale Training (EMT) strategy, which enables the model to learn the rich location-aware textures of anomalies, while suppressing upsampling-induced interpolation errors in texture acquisition, ensuring the model is capable of learning small-scale anomalies, while remaining effective for regular scale anomalies.For inference, we propose Generation-then-Fusion Denoising. It decouples anomaly generation from background integration, preventing small anomalies from being overwhelmed.Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art competitors in both anomaly generation quality and downstream detection performance.It achieves a relative IS(a) improvement of 45.86\% (from 1.81 to 2.64) on VisA and 37.70\% (from 1.22 to 1.68) on MVTec AD 2, while also improving the downstream pixel-level IoU by 4.22\% on VisA and AUROC by 6.55\% on MVTec AD 2. Code is available at https://github.com/HUST-SLOW/UniScale.

</details>


### [50] [Vision-Language Grounding as Bidirectional Concept Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07886)
*Jieyu Zhang,Ziqi Gao,Luke Zettlemoyer,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将视觉-语言接地从单向定位问题重新定义为双向概念对应问题，即给定图像-文本对，恢复所有可视觉指涉的文本片段与实例级图像区域之间的对应关系。作者提出ConCor-1模型，基于预训练视觉-语言模型，使用可学习的桥接标记来预测文本掩码、图像掩码和对应存在分数，在多个基准上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言接地方法大多将接地简化为单向定位问题（给定预设文本短语或类别名，定位对应图像区域），这假设相关语言单元已被预先指定，忽略了接地通信中更基本的挑战：确定文本的哪些部分是视觉可指涉的，以及它们如何与图像中的实体对应。

Method: 作者将接地形式化为图像-文本对上的双向概念对应问题，统一了短语接地、指称表达接地和开放词汇检测等任务。为此提出ConCor-1模型，构建于预训练视觉-语言模型之上，使用可学习的桥接标记表示候选图像-文本对应关系，对每个标记预测文本掩码、图像掩码和对应存在分数。同时将多种接地和分割数据集转换为统一的对应格式用于训练和评估。

Result: 实验表明ConCor-1在多个基准上持续优于基线：长标注数据集上对应F1提升48%，零样本LVIS（以大型类别列表作为文本输入）上提升29%。

Conclusion: 将接地任务重新定义为双向概念对应不仅能统一多种常见的接地任务，而且通过ConCor-1模型在实验上验证了该范式的有效性和优越性，为解决接地任务中"哪部分文本是可指涉的"这一基本问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language grounding connects language to visual content, yet most existing formulations reduce grounding to a unidirectional localization problem: given a prespecified text phrase or category name, identify the corresponding image region. This setup assumes that the relevant linguistic unit is already known, overlooking a more basic challenge in grounded communication: determining which parts of the text are visually referential and how they correspond to entities in the image. We formulate grounding as $\textit{bidirectional concept correspondence}$ over an image-text pair. Given an image and its paired text, the goal is to recover all correspondences between visually referential text spans and instance-level image segments, without assuming that the relevant text spans are provided. This formulation unifies common grounding tasks, including phrase grounding, referring expression grounding, and open-vocabulary detection, by treating text segmentation, image segmentation, and cross-modal alignment as a single correspondence prediction problem. To address this task, we introduce $\textbf{ConCor-1}$, a grounding model built on top of a pretrained vision-language model. It uses learnable $\textit{bridge tokens}$ to represent candidate image-text correspondences and predicts, for each token, a text mask, an image mask, and a correspondence presence score. To train and evaluate this task, we convert diverse grounding and segmentation datasets into a unified correspondence format. Experiments show that $\textbf{ConCor-1}$ consistently outperforms baselines, improving correspondence F1 by 48% on the long-caption dataset and by 29% on zero-shot LVIS, where the large category list serves as the text input.

</details>


### [51] [DeCo: Zero-Shot Industrial Anomaly Generation through Decoupling and Recoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07904)
*Shilei Zeng,Xurui Li,Yaohan Tang,Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeCo框架，通过解耦异常结构与源产品、再与目标产品正常纹理重新耦合的方式，解决零样本工业异常生成中异常信息获取不准确和异常-产品融合不可控两大问题，在MVTec AD和VisA数据集上取得SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测面临真实异常数据稀缺的严重制约，而现有零样本异常生成方法存在两个关键局限：异常信息获取不准确以及异常与产品的融合过程不可控。

Method: DeCo方法包含两部分：(1) 异常信息获取阶段，通过Dual-Routing Flow（DR-Flow）将纹理不变的异常结构绑定到异常令牌上，同时利用Product-Invariant Flow（PI-Flow）作为并行约束，防止异常令牌绑定源产品信息；(2) 异常-产品融合阶段，提出混合注入（hybrid injection）将获取的异常结构重新耦合到目标产品上，并通过产品兼容性校正（PCC）补偿异常结构与产品之间的不兼容性。

Result: 在MVTec AD数据集上提升了5.1%的像素AP，在VisA数据集上提升了8.2%的像素AP，建立了新的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: DeCo通过显式解耦与重耦合策略有效解决了零样本工业异常生成中的两大核心问题，验证了该方法对下游检测模型训练的显著促进作用。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly inspection is severely hindered by the scarcity of real anomalous data.Zero-shot industrial anomaly generation addresses this by generating anomalies on specific products without requiring any of their real anomalous images. However, existing methods suffer from two critical limitations, i.e., inaccurate anomaly information acquisition and uncontrolled anomaly-product fusion.To overcome these challenges, we propose DeCo, which decouples the anomaly structure from its source product, and explicitly recouples it with the normal textures of the target product. During anomaly information acquisition,Dual-Routing Flow (DR-Flow) binds the texture-invariant anomaly structure to an abnormal token, while a parallel constraint, Product-Invariant Flow (PI-Flow), prevents the abnormal token from binding the source product. During anomaly-product fusion, we propose a hybrid injection to recouple the acquired anomaly structure with the target product, and Product Compatibility Correction (PCC) to compensate for the incompatibility between the acquired anomaly structure and the product. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeCo establishes a new state-of-the-art. Training downstream detection models on our generated data yields massive pixel AP improvements of 5.1\% on MVTec AD and 8.2\% on VisA. Code is available at https://github.com/HUST-SLOW/DeCo.

</details>


### [52] [SegDem: Segmentation helps Demosaicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07916)
*Ping Chen,Xiangming Wang,Yongyong Chen,Jiezhang Cao,Kai Zhang,Jingyong Su,Jie Liu,Haijin Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SegDem，一种基于跨任务解码器表示迁移的图像去马赛克框架，利用实例分割的结构预训练来增强去马赛克性能，并在多个骨干网络和CFA布局上取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去马赛克方法主要将问题视为像素级重建，依赖局部纹理、跨通道相关性和低级图像统计，忽略了重建与视觉理解共享同一物理世界结构这一事实。作者认为两者可以互为补充，结构信息应当在两类任务间保持一致。

Method: 提出SegDem框架：首先通过实例感知结构预训练学习区域和边界感知表征，然后将解码器迁移到RAW条件下的重建任务。同时将分割和去马赛克条件特征锚定到共享的冻结DINOv2表示空间，以跨任务保持结构组织。该框架可在卷积、Transformer和状态空间骨干网络上实例化，支持单Bayer和四Bayer去马赛克。

Result: 在合成、外部和具有挑战性的数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明SegDem在不同架构和CFA布局上均带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将重建任务与视觉理解任务相结合，通过跨任务结构一致性约束，可以有效提升去马赛克性能，验证了共享场景结构假设的有效性。

Abstract: Image demosaicing reconstructs a full-color image from incomplete color measurements produced by a sensor covered with a color filter array (CFA). Most existing methods formulate demosaicing as pixel-level reconstruction and mainly rely on local textures, cross-channel correlations, and low-level image statistics. Our core insight is that reconstruction and visual understanding can be viewed as complementary views of shared scene structure: both are grounded in the same underlying physical world, and therefore the structural and physical information inferred from an image should remain consistent across the two tasks. We instantiate this idea with instance segmentation and propose \emph{SegDem}, a cross-task decoder representation transfer framework for demosaicing. SegDem first learns region- and boundary-aware representations through instance-aware structural pretraining and then transfers the decoder to RAW-conditioned reconstruction. Segmentation- and demosaicing-conditioned features are further anchored to a shared frozen DINOv2 representation space to preserve structural organization across tasks. We instantiate SegDem with convolutional, Transformer-based, and state-space backbones for unified Single- and Quad-Bayer demosaicing. Extensive experiments on synthetic, external, and challenging datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and CFA layouts.

</details>


### [53] [Forged Peer Judgments Mislead Multimodal LLM Judge Panels: Source-Blind Anchoring and Panel-Consensus Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07920)
*Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究揭示多模态大语言模型评审团中存在"无源锚定"攻击面——引用他人的独立视觉判断会产生19-26个百分点的巨大锚定差距。研究提出panel-consensus验证方法，可阻断84.9%的伪造攻击，将净危害降低97.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM评审团可以交叉引用同伴判断，但被引用的同伴判断本身可能不可信。作者希望揭示这一文本层面的攻击面，即无源锚定效应，因为引用看似独立的视觉判断会显著影响模型最终的裁决结果。

Method: 研究设计了自我框架和同伴框架下的引用实验，通过匹配内容、仅标签对照验证锚定效应是否由标签本身引起；测试了故意生成的简洁错误引用与自然发生的错误同伴陈述的破坏力对比（使用bootstrap置信区间）；最后提出panel-consensus验证机制，通过独立收集的盲选投票来交叉核验引用内容。

Result: 锚定差距高达19-26个百分点；对照实验显示标签本身仅改变-0.17pp（95% CI [-0.68,0.35]），排除标签解释；故意生成的错误引用将原本正确的裁决推翻的频率是自然错误陈述的1.5-2.7倍（bootstrap 95% CI排除等价性）；panel-consensus验证阻断84.9%伪造攻击，削减97.5%净危害。

Conclusion: 多模态协作评估中存在低成本攻击面，但通过panel-consensus验证可以形成具体有效的防御机制。该研究为更安全的多模态协同评估提供了实证基础和实践方案。

Abstract: Multimodal LLM judge panels can cross-reference peers, but a quoted peer judgment may itself be untrusted. We expose source-blind anchoring as a text-level attack surface in vision-language model (VLM) panels. Quoting independent visual judgments creates large anchoring gaps (19--26 percentage points) under both self and peer framing. A matched-content, label-only control changes the broken rate by only $-0.17$pp (95\% CI $[-0.68,0.35]$), showing that the self/peer label itself does not explain the effect. Under our tested construction, deliberately generated, concise wrong quotes overturn originally-correct verdicts 1.5--2.7$\times$ more often than naturally occurring wrong peer statements, with bootstrap 95\% CIs excluding parity across two datasets and seven VLM judges. Because the two statement populations differ in selection and form, this ratio measures differential damage under the tested attack rather than a provenance-only causal effect. We then introduce panel-consensus verification, which cross-checks a quote against independently collected blind votes. It blocks 84.9\% of fabricated attacks, cuts their net harm by 97.5\%, and preserves the positive but statistically inconclusive point estimate for genuine peer information under leave-one-out re-verification. These results identify a low-cost attack surface and a concrete defense for safer multimodal collaborative evaluation.

</details>


### [54] [SCoPE: Training-Free Audio-Visual Event Perception via Sparse Cross-Modal Prior Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07923)
*Jaemo Jeong,Junho Yoon,Hyunju Kim,Dongman Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoPE，一个无需训练的音频-视觉事件感知框架，通过让所有查询标签竞争共享证据、各模态相互引导事件选择，解决标签间"虚假共激活"（FCA）问题，在LLP基准上相比AV²A提升了Type@seg 7.45分和Event@seg 5.04分，且可无缝迁移至其他数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无需训练方法在音频-视觉事件感知中通过匹配冻结的音频和视觉特征与文本编码事件名来查询新事件词汇，但相关标签共享证据，导致错误标签可能获得与正确标签相同甚至更高的分数（称为虚假共激活FCA）。仅靠标量阈值或类别特定阈值无法从根本上解决这一问题，因此需要一种能够消除FCA的机制。

Method: SCoPE是一个无需训练的框架，核心思路是让所有查询标签竞争共享证据，同时每个模态引导另一模态的事件选择。作者还推导了在二标签拟合情况下移除FCA的精确条件。

Result: 在LLP数据集上，使用相同的冻结CLIP+CLAP骨干网络，SCoPE相比AV²A报告的数值在Type@seg上提升了7.45分，Event@seg上提升了5.04分。相同的固定配置无需修改即可迁移到OV-AVEBench和VGGSound-AVEL100k数据集。

Conclusion: SCoPE通过标签竞争机制有效消除了虚假共激活问题，显著提升了音视频事件感知的性能，并且具有良好的跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-visual event perception (AVEP) determines which events occur in a video, when they occur, and whether they are audible, visible, or both. Training-free methods query new event vocabularies by matching frozen audio and visual features with text-encoded event names. However, related labels share evidence. An incorrect label can then score at least as high as a correct one. We call this a false co-activation (FCA). No scalar cutoff can reject the incorrect label while keeping every correct one. Class-specific thresholds may prevent that label from becoming a final prediction, but the FCA remains in the underlying score vector. We introduce SCoPE, a training-free framework in which all queried labels compete for shared evidence and each modality guides event selection in the other. We derive an exact condition for when this competition removes an FCA in a two-label fit. With identical frozen CLIP+CLAP backbones on LLP, SCoPE improves Type@seg by 7.45 points and Event@seg by 5.04 points compared with the reported AV$^2$A values. The same fixed configuration transfers unchanged to OV-AVEBench and VGGSound-AVEL100k.

</details>


### [55] [SportsGrounder: Proposal-Aided Interleaved Grounding for Dense Sports Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07932)
*Yizhi Li,Jiawei Jiang,Guanhong Wang,Yingcai Wu,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SportsGrounder框架，利用开放词汇视觉专家进行交错式接地（interleaved grounding），以解决密集体育视频推理中大量小尺度、高交互、视觉同质实体（如相同球衣的球员和球）难以精细理解的问题。通过Interleaved Grounding Fusion（IGF）机制逐帧融合边界框坐标、隐式视觉语义和全局网格特征，配合Action-Aware Supervision（AAS）模块正则化模型隐藏状态，并使用Mixed Preference Optimization（MPO）优化，在SoccerNet和FineSports衍生数据集上取得了最先进的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型多模态模型（LMMs）在密集体育视频推理中面临严峻挑战：由于缺乏细粒度视觉细节，模型常过度依赖文本先验进行猜测，尤其在区分视觉上相似的球员动作和身份时表现不佳。这一缺陷阻碍了体育视频分析在竞技分析和转播增强等场景中的应用。

Method: SportsGrounder包含三大核心组件：(1) 开放词汇视觉专家辅助的交错式接地框架，提取领域引导的目标提议实现精确空间定位；(2) Interleaved Grounding Fusion (IGF)机制，逐帧集成显式边界框坐标与隐式视觉语义及全局网格特征，保持严格时间对齐同时避免序列长度爆炸；(3) Action-Aware Supervision (AAS)模块直接正则化模型隐藏状态，强制网络学习准确的运动表征而非依赖语言偏差。最后通过Mixed Preference Optimization (MPO)优化以更好区分具有欺骗性的干扰项。

Result: 在新构建的密集体育VQA数据集（源自SoccerNet和FineSports）上的大量实验表明，SportsGrounder显著提升了细粒度推理能力，取得了最先进的准确率。

Conclusion: SportsGrounder通过引入显式空间接地信息与动作感知监督，有效缓解了LMMs在密集体育视频推理中对文本先验的过度依赖，验证了融合细粒度视觉定位信息对于复杂视频理解任务的重要价值。

Abstract: Sports video analysis is crucial for athletic analytics and broadcasting enhancement. Dense sports video reasoning, however, demands a fine-grained understanding of numerous small-scale, highly interactive, and visually homogeneous entities (e.g., players sharing identical uniforms, the ball) across long temporal contexts. Current Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) inherently struggle with such dense visual complexities. Due to the lack of fine-grained visual details, these models often over-rely on textual priors to guess answers, especially when distinguishing visually similar actions and players. To address this, we propose \textbf{SportsGrounder}, a framework that leverages an open-vocabulary visual expert to aid interleaved grounding specifically for dense sports video reasoning. To achieve precise spatial localization, we extract domain-guided object proposals and introduce an Interleaved Grounding Fusion (IGF) mechanism. The IGF frame-by-frame integrates explicit bounding box coordinates and implicit visual semantics with global grid features. This design preserves strict temporal alignment and prevents sequence length explosion. Furthermore, we design an Action-Aware Supervision (AAS) module that directly regularizes the model's hidden states, forcing the network to learn accurate motion representations rather than relying on language bias. Optimized with Mixed Preference Optimization (MPO) to better distinguish deceptive distractors, our extensive experiments on newly curated dense sports VQA datasets (derived from SoccerNet and FineSports) demonstrate that SportsGrounder significantly improves fine-grained reasoning and achieves state-of-the-art accuracy.

</details>


### [56] [FlexSplat: Flexible Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting without Point Cloud Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07937)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Hanting Ye,Maria Gorlatova,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexSplat是一个前馈框架，用于从无标定、以物体为中心的多视图图像集合中进行新视角合成（NVS）。它通过联合训练的几何变换器预测每张图像的相机参数和深度，消除对相机位姿的依赖，并利用深度引导的高斯参数化和多视图可变形交叉注意力，在紧凑的高斯预算下实现与有标定方法相当的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于查询的方法（query-based）通过将3D高斯视为transformer查询并使用多视图可变形注意力进行细化，能够重建紧凑的3D高斯集合，但这些方法都假设相机位姿已知。作者旨在移除这一假设，实现无需标定即可进行高斯重建的操作。

Method: FlexSplat包含一个几何变换器，与高斯解码器联合训练，用于预测每张图像的相机参数和深度。这些预测用于深度引导的高斯参数化和多视图可变形交叉注意力，将所有输入视图的evidence聚合成一组视图一致的基本图元。同时使用不确定性加权的深度一致性目标让联合学习的几何适应重建任务，解码过程中形成的跨视图共识可吸收相机和深度估计的残余误差。

Result: 在ShapeNet-SRN和Google Scanned Objects（GSO）数据集上，FlexSplat在没有相机位姿和真实深度的情况下，达到了与有标定最优方法相近或相当的重建质量。在GSO上匹配了最佳感知质量（LPIPS），且与有标定方法相比PSNR差距保持在0.7 dB以内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合训练的几何前置模块足以将无标定操作引入基于查询的高斯重建中，同时保持与有标定方法相当的定量和感知质量。此外，表示使用与输入分辨率解耦的紧凑高斯预算，图元数量不随图像网格或视图数量增长。

Abstract: We present FlexSplat, a feed-forward framework for novel view synthesis (NVS) from uncalibrated, object-centric multi-view image collections. A recent line of query-based methods reconstructs a compact set of 3D Gaussians by treating them as transformer queries that are refined with multi-view deformable attention; these methods, however, assume that camera poses are given. FlexSplat removes this assumption: a geometry transformer is trained jointly with the Gaussian decoder to predict per-image camera parameters and depth, which in turn ground a depth-guided Gaussian parameterization and a multi-view deformable cross-attention that aggregates evidence across all input views into a single, view-consistent set of primitives. An uncertainty-weighted depth-consistency objective lets the jointly trained geometry adapt to the reconstruction task, while the cross-view consensus formed during decoding absorbs the residual error of the estimated cameras and depth. The representation uses a compact Gaussian budget that is decoupled from the input resolution - unlike pixel-aligned methods, the primitive count does not grow with the image grid - and is not dictated by the number of views. On ShapeNet-SRN and Google Scanned Objects (GSO), FlexSplat matches or approaches posed state-of-the-art reconstructors while requiring neither camera poses nor ground-truth depth, and matches the best perceptual (LPIPS) quality among the compared methods on GSO. Our results indicate that a jointly trained geometry front-end is sufficient to bring calibration-free operation to query-based Gaussian reconstruction while staying within 0.7 dB PSNR of posed methods and matching their perceptual quality.

</details>


### [57] [LAD-COD: Language-Aligned Dense Perception for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07941)
*Shangye Song,Tianzhi Zhu,Syed Ariff Syed Hesham,Xin He,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LAD-COD框架，用于伪装物体检测（COD）。该框架通过Language-Aligned Dual Visual Fusion（LADVF）机制，将基于大语言模型的指令语义作为顶层引导，与底层的层次化视觉特征对齐，实现语义引导与细节保留的平衡，并在CAMO、COD10K和NC4K三个数据集上的12项指标对比中全部取得最佳成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装物体检测面临前景与背景高相似性的挑战，导致视觉判别性弱、边界证据不足。现有基于大语言模型的分割系统虽然通过指令条件化目标嵌入指导掩码解码，但这些嵌入主要影响掩码解码器，未能显式引导密集视觉特征，难以保留低对比度边界和精细局部结构。

Method: LAD-COD框架包含两个核心组件：1）可训练的分层视觉分支，捕捉伪装敏感的纹理、边界和上下文信息（而非直接适配大型通用图像编码器）；2）Language-Aligned Dual Visual Fusion（LADVF）模块，将稀疏提示扩展为查询patch级语言对齐特征，并通过门控残差方式将其与分层特征整合，实现语义信息引导定位同时保留精细结构细节。

Result: 在CAMO、COD10K和NC4K三个标准数据集上，LAD-COD在所有12项数据集-指标组合对比中均取得最佳结果，全面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LAD-COD通过语言对齐的顶层语义引导与底层层次化视觉特征的深度融合，有效解决了伪装物体检测中弱视觉线索与语义引导之间的对齐问题，证明了在语言到掩码范式中同时引导密集视觉特征的重要性。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) aims to segment objects that exhibit high visual similarity to their surroundings, which reduces foreground-background discriminability and weakens boundary evidence across appearance, texture, and structure. Such limitations motivate the use of instruction-conditioned semantics as top-down guidance for identifying which weak visual cues are relevant to the target. Recent segmentation systems built on large multimodal models (LMMs) demonstrate this possibility through instruction-conditioned target embeddings that guide mask decoding. However, in this language-to-mask paradigm, the generated target embedding conditions mainly the mask decoder, leaving the dense visual features that must preserve low-contrast boundaries and fine local structure without explicit guidance. We propose Language-Aligned Dense perception for COD (LAD-COD), a framework that aligns top-down semantic target guidance with bottom-up hierarchical visual features. Instead of fully adapting a large generic image encoder, LAD-COD learns a trainable hierarchical visual branch that captures camouflage-sensitive texture, boundary, and contextual information. To align these features with the target embedding, LAD-COD applies Language-Aligned Dual Visual Fusion (LADVF), which extends the embedding beyond sparse prompting to query patch-level language-aligned features and to gate their residual integration with the hierarchical features. This design allows semantic information to guide localization while preserving the fine structural details needed for camouflage segmentation. Experiments on CAMO, COD10K, and NC4K show that LAD-COD obtains the best reported value in all 12 dataset-metric comparisons.

</details>


### [58] [SynVAR: Synergizing Spatial and Semantic Alignment in Visual Autoregressive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07948)
*Zhennan Chen,Tianxing Shi,Pengcheng Xu,Kepan Nan,Qian Wang,Zili Yi,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynVAR是首个专为VAR范式设计的免训练增强框架，通过空间-语义协同控制策略抑制跨尺度误差传播，包含全局引导、感受野约束和高频补偿三个关键组件，显著提升VAR在复杂场景建模中的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR因其下一尺度预测范式而广受欢迎，但在处理包含多个对象和属性的复杂场景时存在显著的性能瓶颈。现有基于扩散的增强方法无法有效解决VAR特有的跨尺度误差传播与累积问题。

Method: 提出SynVAR框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 全局引导，确保早期阶段的合理空间结构；(2) 感受野约束，缓解早期阶段的语义混淆；(3) 高频补偿，恢复细粒度细节。三者协同实现空间-语义协作控制，无需训练即可增强VAR。

Result: 大量定量和定性实验表明，SynVAR在增强VAR复杂场景建模能力方面取得了显著改进，有效抑制了传播误差并提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: SynVAR作为首个针对VAR范式的免训练增强框架，通过空间-语义协同控制策略有效解决了跨尺度误差传播问题，为VAR在复杂场景下的生成质量提升提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: VAR has gained widespread popularity due to its next-scale prediction paradigm. However, it faces substantial performance bottlenecks when handling complex scenes with multiple objects and attributes. Existing diffusion-based enhancement methods fail to adequately address the unique challenge of cross-scale error propagation and accumulation in VAR. To this end, we propose SynVAR, the first training-free enhancement framework specifically tailored for the VAR paradigm, which introduces a spatial-semantic collaborative control strategy to effectively suppress propagation error and improve generation quality. SynVAR comprises three key components: (1) Global guidance to ensure reasonable spatial structure in the early stages, (2) Receptive field constraints to mitigate early-stage semantic confusion, (3) High-frequency compensation to recover fine-grained details. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate the significant improvements in the ability of SynVAR to enhance the VAR's capability for complex scene modeling.

</details>


### [59] [LIBAD: A Multimodal Anomaly Detection Benchmark for Li-Ion Battery Electrode Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07958)
*Wenbo Sui,Daniel Lichau,Harold Phelippeau,Zhao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LIBAD，这是首个面向锂离子电池电极制造的多模态异常检测基准数据集，源自真实卷对卷生产线，提供对齐的双面可见光成像、高分辨率X射线成像和低分辨率X射线成像。针对现有方法在该场景下迁移性差、误报率高的问题，提出DA-Core方法，通过联合考虑特征空间覆盖率和局部密度进行核心集选择，在0.05核心集比例下将FPR95从60.4%降至54.3%，并降低43.9%推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态工业异常检测主要针对离散产品，依赖强相关的RGB和3D观测，而连续过程制造和弱相关传感模态（如可见光与X射线）未被充分探索，且现有方法在该场景下缺乏基准验证。

Method: 提出DA-Core方法，一种基于记忆的异常检测方法，在核心集选择过程中同时考虑正常特征的特征空间覆盖率和局部密度，使得紧凑的记忆库能够更好地保留细粒度的正常变异信息。

Result: 在LIBAD基准上，DA-Core在0.05核心集比例下将FPR95从60.4%降低到54.3%（与标准最远点采样相比），同时优于标准核心集在0.20比例下的最佳结果，并将推理时间减少43.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，为过程制造设计异常检测方法时，需要显式考虑正常特征的数据分布以及模态间的关系特性。

Abstract: Multimodal industrial anomaly detection has largely focused on discrete products using strongly correlated RGB and 3D observations, leaving continuous process manufacturing and weakly correlated sensing modalities underexplored. We introduce LIBAD, the first multimodal anomaly detection benchmark for Li-ion battery electrode manufacturing. Collected from real roll-to-roll production lines, LIBAD provides aligned double-sided visible-light imaging, high-resolution X-ray radiography, and inline-compatible low-resolution X-ray radiography. Electrode patches in LIBAD exhibit highly homogeneous material appearance, while defect evidence can be strong in one modality but weak or absent in another, resulting in pronounced cross-modal anomaly inconsistency. Benchmarks of representative methods under the inline-compatible visible-light and low-resolution X-ray setting exhibit limited transferability and consistently high false-positive rates. We therefore propose DA-Core, a memory-based method that jointly considers feature-space coverage and local density of normal features during coreset selection, allowing compact memory banks to better preserve fine-grained normal variations. With a coreset ratio of 0.05, DA-Core reduces FPR95 from 60.4% to 54.3% compared with standard farthest point sampling. At this ratio, DA-Core also outperforms the best standard coreset result (obtained at 0.20) while reducing inference time by 43.9%. These results suggest that both the data distribution of normal features and the modality relationship itself require explicit consideration when designing anomaly detection methods for process manufacturing.

</details>


### [60] [Distilling Physical Priors into Streaming World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07981)
*Liangliang Zhao,Junying Wang,Danni Yang,Yifan Chang,Bin Fu,Yu Qiao,Bowen Zhou,Yihao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyS框架，通过三阶段流程将物理先验蒸馏到流式世界模型中。构建了包含12万条真实世界物理交互视频的PhyS-120K数据集，通过物理感知监督微调注入14B双向DiT教师模型，再蒸馏为1.3B因果DiT，最后使用在线强化学习优化物理合理性，并提出了Temporal Credit Routing（TCR）解决时间信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式世界模型的推演经常违反基本物理约束。现有的双向到因果蒸馏框架存在两个根本缺陷：通用双向教师模型从视觉导向的预训练中习得的物理先验有限，且在蒸馏过程中这些有限先验会进一步丢失。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 构建PhyS-120K真实物理交互视频数据集（涵盖刚体动力学、软体变形、流体现象和相变），每个视频带结构化标注；2) 物理感知监督微调将物理先验注入14B双向DiT教师，再蒸馏为1.3B因果DiT用于少步自回归流式生成；3) 在线强化学习激励物理合理生成，提出TCR通过在重叠时间窗口上评估物理一致性，将组相对优势路由到时间对齐的去噪动作上。

Result: 在PhysicsIQ基准上，PhyS相比Wan2.1-14B教师提升18.2%，相比Self Forcing、Rolling Forcing和Causal Forcing分别提升23.7%、14.8%和31.4%；同时改善了VideoPhy、VideoPhy2和PhyGenBench等物理感知视频基准的结果。

Conclusion: 实验证明了三阶段物理先验蒸馏方法能有效克服通用双向教师物理先验不足及蒸馏损失的问题，显著提升流式世界模型的物理一致性与长时程动力学质量。

Abstract: Streaming world models predict future visual states online while maintaining physically coherent dynamics over long horizons. However, their rollouts often violate basic physical constraints. A common approach distills pretrained bidirectional DiTs into few-step causal generators. However, this paradigm suffers from two fundamental limitations: generic bidirectional teachers acquire limited physical priors from visually oriented pretraining, and the limited priors suffer further loss during bidirectional-to-causal distillation. We present PhyS, a three-stage framework for distilling physical priors into streaming world models. To acquire physical priors from real-world interactions, we construct PhyS-120K, a dataset of 120K real-world physical-interaction videos spanning rigid-body dynamics, soft-body deformation, fluid phenomena, and phase transitions. Each video is annotated with structured descriptions of object properties and causal state transitions. Physics-aware supervised fine-tuning injects the physical priors into a bidirectional 14B DiT teacher, which we then distill into a lightweight 1.3B causal DiT for few-step autoregressive streaming generation. Finally, we use online reinforcement learning to incentivize the distilled model to generate physically plausible rollouts and further propose Temporal Credit Routing (TCR) to address temporal credit assignment. TCR evaluates physical consistency over overlapping temporal windows and routes the resulting group-relative advantages to temporally aligned denoising actions. On PhysicsIQ, PhyS improves the Wan2.1-14B teacher by 18.2\% and the Self Forcing, Rolling Forcing, and Causal Forcing by 23.7\%, 14.8\%, and 31.4\%, respectively. Results also improve the physics-aware video benchmarks VideoPhy, VideoPhy2, and PhyGenBench. The dataset, code, and more sample videos are available on our Project Page.

</details>


### [61] [AdaDINO: Pair-Aware In-Backbone Adaptation of Frozen DINO for Efficient Remote Sensing Change Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07982)
*Xu Zhang,Xinqing Li,Jianpeng Xie,Zeshuai Zhu,Xin He,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaDINO是一种面向遥感变化检测的配对感知骨干网内自适应框架，通过冻结DINO编码器注入双时相交互实现高效变化检测，在四个基准数据集上取得竞争性或更优性能，并实现1.41倍吞吐量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉基础模型（如DINO）是为单图像表示预训练的，而遥感变化检测需要对双时相图像对进行推理。现有VFM方法通常独立编码两幅图像，事后才比较，导致骨干网络无法感知跨时相关关系，存在预训练范式与任务需求之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出AdaDINO框架，核心包含三个组件：1) 变化感知门控局部适应（CGLA），在选定的冻结块后耦合两个流，注入带相反符号的共享时间残差，增强真实变化响应同时保持配对中点；2) 批共享通道块选择（BSCS），保留批共享的通道块子集以紧凑稠密FFN执行，降低FFN计算量；3) CGLA先验引导精炼（CPGR）解码器，复用编码端变化响应进行由粗到细的预测。

Result: 在四个遥感变化检测基准上的实验表明，AdaDINO相对于VFM基线取得竞争性或更优性能，在类别无关的SYSU-CD数据集上增益最大。去除62.5%的FFN隐藏宽度后，在SYSU-CD上仍达到85.29%的F1分数，同时实现1.41倍的吞吐量加速。

Conclusion: 在冻结的DINO编码器中注入双时相交互可以有效地弥合VFM预训练（单图表示）与变化检测任务（双图推理）之间的差距，同时通过通道块选择和共享残差策略实现计算效率的显著提升。

Abstract: Vision foundation models (VFMs) such as DINO are pretrained for single-image representation, whereas remote sensing change detection requires reasoning over a bi-temporal pair. Existing VFM-based methods usually encode the two images independently and compare them only afterward, leaving the VFM backbone unaware of cross-temporal relations. To bridge this mismatch, we present AdaDINO, a pair-aware in-backbone adaptation framework that equips a frozen DINO encoder with bi-temporal interaction for efficient change detection. Its core component, Change-aware Gated Local Adaptation (CGLA), couples the two streams after selected frozen blocks and injects a shared temporal residual into them with opposite signs, enhancing genuine change responses while preserving the pair midpoint. Batch-Shared Chunk Selection (BSCS) further reduces feed-forward network (FFN) computation by retaining a batch-shared subset of channel chunks that can be executed as a compact dense FFN. A CGLA-Prior-Guided Refinement (CPGR) decoder reuses encoder-side change responses for coarse-to-fine prediction. Experiments on four remote sensing change detection benchmarks show that AdaDINO achieves competitive or superior performance against VFM-based baselines, with the largest gain on the category-agnostic SYSU-CD dataset. With 62.5% of the FFN hidden width removed, AdaDINO still achieves an F1 score of 85.29% on SYSU-CD while delivering a 1.41$\times$ throughput speedup. The code will be released.

</details>


### [62] [AgriField-40K: Adapting Vision Models to Agriculture With Efficient Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07984)
*Vasileios Tzouras,Paraskevas Pegios,Lazaros Nalpantidis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgriField-40K农业视觉数据集（整合17个公开资源）和AgriMAE参数高效持续预训练基线，仅训练轻量适配器即可在农业下游任务中匹配甚至超越全量微调，同时减少多达9倍的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 田间农业计算机视觉对精准农业至关重要，但现有方法严重依赖昂贵的标注数据和大型预训练模型的高成本适配，缺乏低成本高效的持续预训练方案。

Method: 构建覆盖多种作物、杂草和田地条件的AgriField-40K数据集；提出AgriMAE参数高效持续预训练基线，仅训练轻量适配器来适配在自然图像上预训练的掩码自编码器；探索语义特征重建作为替代预训练目标。

Result: AgriMAE持续提升下游性能，能以少至9倍的可训练参数匹配甚至超越全量微调，验证了AgriField-40K作为农业视觉持续预训练实用资源的有效性。

Conclusion: 参数高效的持续预训练结合领域专用数据集是农业视觉的低成本有效方案，AgriField-40K为该领域提供了一个实用的预训练资源。

Abstract: Field-based agricultural computer vision is important for precision agriculture, yet it largely depends on expensive annotations and costly adaptation of large pretrained models. We introduce AgriField-40K, a field-centric dataset curated from 17 public resources and covering diverse crops, weeds, and field conditions. Building on this, we present AgriMAE, a parameter-efficient continual pretraining baseline that adapts a masked autoencoder pretrained on natural images by training only lightweight adapters. We further explore semantic feature reconstruction as an alternative pretraining objective and evaluate transfer across multiple tasks. AgriMAE consistently improves downstream performance and can match or even outperform full fine-tuning while using up to $9\times$ fewer trainable parameters, showing that AgriField-40K is a practical resource for continual pretraining in agricultural vision. Project page: https://dtu-pas.github.io/agrifield40k/

</details>


### [63] [Advantage-Guided Gate: Reshaping Open-Ended Reasoning for Vision-Based Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07987)
*Ling Lin,Yang Bai,Congcong Zhu,Jiangming Shi,Meng Wang,Yang Long,Jingrun Chen,Ling Shao,Huazhu Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种优势引导门控框架（Advantage-Guided Gating），通过蒙特卡洛价值评估对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的逐步推理过程进行动态干预和纠偏，包含Step-Advantage Gate和Trajectory-Advantage Gate分别筛选高价值推理步骤与高质量完整推理轨迹，并构建了Reasoning-Tree-160k数据集进行两阶段训练，在空间场景理解与推理任务上显著提升了基准MLLMs的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在复杂空间场景理解与推理任务中虽展现出潜力，但其开放式推理过程容易出现决策错误和错误累积问题，导致答案质量不稳定，需要一种机制来动态干预和纠正推理过程中的偏差。

Method: 将逐步推理建模为有限时域决策过程，在推理树上引入蒙特卡洛价值评估作为中间监督信号；框架包含Step-Advantage Gate（动态选择高价值推理步骤）和Trajectory-Advantage Gate（选择高质量完整推理轨迹）；训练时利用多分支采样生成的推理树进行门控的监督学习，结合共享参数初始化与任务特定头部实现跨任务鲁棒性；推理时根据问题类型贪心选择高价值前缀推理步骤和最优推理头。

Result: 构建了Reasoning-Tree-160k数据集并完成两阶段学习，大量实验表明该优势引导门控框架能有效提升基准MLLMs在基于视觉的空间理解和推理任务中的性能。

Conclusion: 优势引导的门控框架能有效抑制MLLMs推理过程中的错误累积，提升推理稳定性与最终答案准确性，且代码已开源供研究使用。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated significant potential in complex spatial scene understanding and reasoning tasks. However, their open-ended reasoning process is prone to decision errors and error accumulation, leading to instability in answer quality. To address this, we propose an advantage-guided gating framework that dynamically intervenes in and corrects deviations during the reasoning process. Specifically, we model step-by-step reasoning as a finite-horizon decision process and introduce Monte Carlo value evaluation on the reasoning tree to provide intermediate supervision signals. The framework includes Step-Advantage Gate and Trajectory-Advantage Gate, which dynamically select high-value reasoning steps and high-quality complete reasoning trajectories, respectively. During training, we perform supervised learning for the gates using reasoning trees generated via multi-branch sampling, and combine shared-parameter initialization with task-specific heads to achieve cross-task robustness and diversity. During inference, the model greedily selects high-value prefix reasoning steps while choosing the optimal reasoning head based on the problem type, thereby significantly improving the accuracy of the final answer. Furthermore, we constructed the Reasoning-Tree-160k dataset and performed two-stage learning on it. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this advantage-guided gating framework effectively enhances the performance of benchmark MLLMs in visual-based spatial understanding and reasoning tasks. The code is open to the public for research: https://github.com/LingLin-ll/Advantage-Guided-Gate.

</details>


### [64] [MRBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Human Motion-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07993)
*Fulong Liu,Liang Xu,Chengqun Yang,Yuhao Zhang,Yichao Yan,Xiaokang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MRBench，一个全面的运动-文本检索基准，包含3,390个多样化运动（来自动作捕捉、野外视频、合成视频和生成模型）和10,170条多粒度描述，覆盖118个细粒度类别。同时提出一个轻量级的粒度感知模型，利用LLM生成的伪监督来提升混合粒度检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动-文本检索基准存在同质化室内运动、不均衡分布和过于简化的重复文本等问题，无法可靠评估跨域和跨粒度的对齐能力，因此需要更全面的基准来推动运动-语言对齐评估。

Method: 构建MRBench采用多阶段数据策展流水线（筛选平衡候选、验证语义对齐、生成多粒度描述）。提出粒度感知模型，以冻结的标准描述对齐检索模型为核心，利用LLM生成的简洁和细粒度描述作为伪监督，训练额外分支的粒度特定运动提取器和文本适配器，推理时通过粒度感知分数融合保持分数可比性。

Result: 在MRBench上的广泛评估揭示了代表性检索基线存在显著的跨数据集泛化差距和对查询粒度的明显敏感性。所提出的粒度感知模型在提升混合粒度检索性能的同时不损害标准描述性能。

Conclusion: MRBench为运动语言对齐评估提供了一个全面测试平台，粒度感知模型验证了多粒度运动检索的有效性，有助于推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Human motion-text retrieval provides a rigorous means of assessing cross-modal alignment. Prevailing benchmarks are dominated by homogeneous indoor motions, imbalanced motion distributions, and oversimplified, repetitive texts, which hinder the reliable measurement of cross-domain and cross-granularity alignment. We thus introduce MRBench, a comprehensive motion-text retrieval benchmark featuring heterogeneous motions, broad and balanced category coverage, and reliable, discriminative, multi-granular descriptions. MRBench is constructed through a meticulously designed multi-stage data curation pipeline, which filters and balances candidates, verifies unambiguous semantic alignment, and generates motion-grounded descriptions at multiple granularities. The resulting benchmark contains 3,390 motions drawn from motion capture, in-the-wild videos, synthetic videos, and motion generative models, covering 118 fine-grained categories. Each motion is paired with concise, standard, and fine-grained descriptions, yielding 10,170 captions. Extensive evaluations of representative retrieval baselines on MRBench reveal a substantial cross-dataset generalization gap and pronounced sensitivity to query granularity. We propose a lightweight granularity-aware model anchored at a frozen standard-caption-aligned retrieval model. LLM-based concise and fine-grained captions provide pseudo-supervision for extra-branch granularity-specific motion extractors and text adapters. For inference, granularity-aware score fusion integrates global and adapted similarities while strictly maintaining score comparability across all description levels. The resulting model improves mixed-granularity retrieval without compromising standard-caption performance. We believe that our MRBench provides a comprehensive testbed for advancing motion-language alignment evaluation.

</details>


### [65] [PE-Mamba: Bidirectional Selective Layer Aggregation for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07999)
*Kutub Uddin,Nusrat Tasnim,Khalid Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PE-Mamba，一种基于预训练PE-Core视觉Transformer的AI生成图像检测框架，通过轻量级LoRA适配和三种互补的跨层特征聚合组件（双向选择性聚合器BSA、softmax加权聚合器SWA、sigmoid门控融合SGA）来捕获层间特征的顺序语义进展，在UniversalFakeDetect和AIGCDetect数据集上显著超越18种现有检测器，且仅需训练1.3%的总参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉Transformer的AIGI检测器通常使用加权求和策略聚合不同Transformer层的中间表示，忽视了层级特征从浅层纹理到深层语义的天然有序语义进展，导致检测性能受限。

Method: PE-Mamba框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 双向选择性聚合器（BSA），通过前向扫描累积浅到深的取证证据、后向扫描进行深到浅的上下文精炼；(2) softmax加权聚合器（SWA），学习所有层标记的全局摘要；(3) sigmoid门控融合（SGA），通过可学习标量门自适应平衡BSA和SWA的输出。整体基于预训练PE-Core Transformer，仅用轻量级LoRA适配。

Result: 在UniversalFakeDetect上达到96.6% mACC和99.5% mAP，在AIGCDetect上达到95.3% mACC和98.1% mAP，超越18种检测器，在多种生成模型上表现出卓越的泛化能力，且仅训练总参数的1.3%（其中LoRA仅0.13%）。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模Transformer层间特征的顺序语义关系（从浅层到深层），结合双向选择性扫描和自适应门控融合机制，PE-Mamba在极低参数训练成本下实现了高效的AI生成图像检测，验证了利用层级语义顺序性进行跨层特征聚合的有效性。

Abstract: AI-generated image (AIGI) detection has become increasingly challenging due to the rapid advancement of generative models and the diminishing gap between synthetic and authentic content. Existing vision transformer-based detectors commonly rely on weighted-sum strategies to aggregate intermediate representations across transformer layers, often overlooking the inherently ordered semantic progression of hierarchical features from shallow texture cues to deep semantic representations. In this work, we propose \textbf{PE-Mamba}, a novel framework built upon a pre-trained PE-Core vision transformer with lightweight LoRA adaptation that introduces three complementary components for cross-layer feature aggregation and fusion. First, a bidirectional selective aggregator (BSA) processes layer-wise classification tokens through forward and backward selective scans, where the forward scan progressively accumulates shallow-to-deep forensic evidence, and the backward scan performs deep-to-shallow contextual refinement to reinterpret low-level cues in light of high-level semantic context. Second, a softmax-weighted aggregator (SWA) computes a learned global summary of all layer tokens as a complementary aggregation path. Third, a sigmoid-gated blend (SGA) adaptively fuses the BSA and SWA outputs via a learnable scalar gate, allowing the model to dynamically balance directional sequential evidence and global layer-wise aggregation. Extensive experiments on UniversalFakeDetect (96.6\% mACC, 99.5\% mAP) and AIGCDetect (95.3\% mACC, 98.1\% mAP) demonstrate that \methodname{} outperforms 18 detectors with superior generalization across diverse generative models, while training only 1.3\% of total parameters (0.13\% for LoRA alone).

</details>


### [66] [Evidence-Grounded Forensic Reasoning for Detecting and Grounding Multi-Modal Media Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08009)
*Yichun Yeh,Yiheng Li,Xiaobo Hu,Zhen Lei,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EFR框架（基于证据锚定的取证推理），通过"锚定-验证"推理链和模态解耦优势路由机制，解决多模态大模型在虚假新闻检测中解释与证据不一致的问题，实现了可验证的可解释伪造检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有虚假新闻检测方法为黑盒模型，无法提供决策理由；而MLLM应用于DGM4任务时面临两大困难：一是模型生成的解释与预测的证据位置脱节，二是强制证据-结论一致性需要主动优化，但统一的训练信号无法区分定位token与分类token，导致多头联合训练不可靠。

Method: 提出证据锚定取证推理（EFR）框架：(1) Anchor-and-Verify推理链，先进行模态隔离感知再进行跨模态比较，以结论坐标为显式锚点约束下游证据的空间对应；(2) 可验证奖励系统在训练中强制证据-结论一致性；(3) 模态解耦优势（MDA）路由机制缓解预测任务间的信用分配错误。

Result: 实验表明EFR在DGM4任务上取得了最先进（SOTA）性能，同时生成结构化的取证推理记录，明确将解释与证据绑定，实现了可验证的可解释性。

Conclusion: EFR通过显式锚定证据与结论的空间对应关系，结合可验证奖励系统和模态解耦的路由机制，有效解决了MLLM在虚假新闻检测中解释不可信、训练不稳定等问题，为取证场景提供了兼具高性能和可验证推理链的解决方案。

Abstract: Fake news increasingly relies on cross-modal image-text forgeries, making transparent and verifiable reasoning chains an urgent need for Detecting and Grounding Multi-Modal Media Manipulation (DGM4). Existing methods produce black-box detection results without any decision rationale, limiting their reliability in forensic practice. Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer a natural path toward explainability, but applying them to DGM4 raises two difficulties. First, models tend to generate explanations disconnected from predicted evidence locations, producing unverified attribution. Second, enforcing evidence-conclusion consistency requires active optimization, yet uniform training signals fail to distinguish localization tokens from classification tokens, making multi-head joint training unreliable. We propose a multi-modal manipulation detector based on an Evidence-Grounded Forensic Reasoning (EFR) framework. EFR introduces an Anchor-and-Verify reasoning chain that enforces modality-isolated perception before cross-modal comparison, with conclusion coordinates as explicit anchors to which downstream evidence must spatially correspond. A verifiable reward system then enforces evidence-conclusion consistency during training, while a Modality-Decoupled Advantage (MDA) routing mechanism mitigats credit misassignment across prediction tasks. Experiments show that EFR achieves state-of-the-art performance while producing structured forensic reasoning records that explicitly bind explanations to evidence.

</details>


### [67] [EgoTrack3D: A Modular Framework for Egocentric 3D Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08016)
*Jan Kulik,Bjarni Dagur Thor Karason,Yung-Hsu Yang,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTrack3D是一个模块化框架，直接从第一人称RGB视频中重建并维护动态3D场景表示，通过将2D分割掩码提升到全局3D坐标系，结合基于点的运动评分机制和基于体素的合并启发式来关联物体轨迹，在ADT数据集上比最强基线提升11%的PCL指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D跟踪和场景图构建方法主要针对显式交互或假设静态场景，难以捕捉复杂动态；而第一人称视频中的快速视角变化和部分遮挡使得构建结构化场景表示充满挑战。

Method: 模块化框架，将2D分割掩码提升到全局3D坐标框架中；使用基于点的运动评分机制与基于体素的合并启发式来关联物体轨迹；在退化条件下用稀疏3D边界框估计替代密集深度图，并集成交互引导的动态关联。

Result: 在Aria Digital Twin (ADT)数据集上，相对于最强基线实现了11%的PCL（正确位置百分比）提升；在稀疏深度和噪声观测等退化条件下仍能保持准确的空间表示。

Conclusion: EgoTrack3D解决了动态3D跟踪这一更一般的场景设置（同时覆盖静态和动态物体），并证明了在真实部署约束下的鲁棒性，为机器人导航和自主导航中的3D场景理解提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Understanding 3D scenes from egocentric video is fundamental for robotics and autonomous navigation, yet rapid viewpoint changes and partial occlusions make building structured representations challenging. Existing 3D tracking and scene graph construction methods primarily address explicit interactions or assume static scenes, limiting their ability to capture complex dynamics. We introduce EgoTrack3D, a modular framework that reconstructs and maintains a dynamic 3D scene representation directly from egocentric RGB video. The framework lifts 2D segmentation masks into a global 3D coordinate frame, using a point-based motion scoring mechanism alongside a voxel-based merging heuristic to associate object tracks. EgoTrack3D maintains accurate representations over time, achieving an 11% improvement in percentage of correct locations (PCL) relative to the strongest baseline on the Aria Digital Twin (ADT) dataset, while addressing the more general setting of persistent 3D tracking for both static and dynamic objects. Furthermore, to demonstrate the system's robustness under degraded conditions that simulate real-world deployment constraints, we replace dense depth maps with sparse 3D bounding box estimation and integrate interaction-guided dynamic association, enabling EgoTrack3D to maintain accurate spatial representations despite noisy observations.

</details>


### [68] [Evidence-RL: Towards Evidence-intensive Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08021)
*Haojie Huang,Xinlei Yu,Chengming Xu,Zhangquan Chen,Cheng Yang,Qingdong He,Yu Yang,Jiangning Zhang,Xiaobin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CED（反事实证据解耦）方法，一种训练时的证据审计机制，用于提升视觉语言模型（VLM）的接地能力。CED通过中和对象级证据区域并对比支持度下降，结合答案正确性在GRPO中训练模型，使模型依赖真正的证据路径而非语言先验或捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM感知后训练方法通过全局扰动或注意力代理来鼓励模型使用图像，但无法验证采样答案是否因果地依赖于支持它的局部证据。许多模型答案可能来自语言先验、数据集捷径或无关的视觉上下文，而非具体的图像证据。

Method: CED的核心方法包括：(1) 对每个响应，中和一个对象中心的证据区域（Evidence Region），并与匹配的非证据区域进行支持度下降对比；(2) 将该信号与答案正确性结合在GRPO目标函数中，奖励那些依赖证据路径而非捷径或干扰路径的正确答案；(3) 使用弱对象级提议（weak object-level proposals），不需要问题特定的证据标注，推理时无额外开销。

Result: 在九个公开基准和四个骨干网络上，CED优于先前的基于RL的后训练方法，针对性的分析验证了其对象中心信号的有效性。

Conclusion: CED通过反事实证据解耦的方式实现了训练时的证据审计，有效提升了VLM的视觉接地能力，证明了对象级因果信号在RL后训练中的价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) should answer from concrete image evidence rather than language priors, dataset shortcuts, or irrelevant visual context. Existing perception-aware post-training methods encourage image use through global perturbations or attention proxies, but they do not test whether a sampled answer causally depends on the local evidence that supports it. We propose Counterfactual Evidence Disentanglement (CED), a training-time evidence audit for VLM grounding. For each response, CED neutralizes an object-centric Evidence Region and compares the resulting support drop against matched non-evidence Regions. We combine this signal with answer correctness inside GRPO, rewarding correct answers that rely on the evidence path rather than shortcut or nuisance paths. CED uses weak object-level proposals, requires no question-specific evidence annotations, and adds no inference-time overhead. Across nine public benchmarks and four backbones, CED outperforms prior RL-based post-training methods, with targeted analyses verifying its object-centric signal.

</details>


### [69] [ZOMP: Zeroth-Order Multi-Modal Prompt Tuning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08060)
*Sajjad Ghiasvand,Yifan Yang,Mahnoosh Alizadeh,Ramtin Pedarsani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZOMP是一种完全仅前向传播的多模态提示调优方法，利用同时扰动随机近似（SPSA）在冻结的CLIP模型视觉和文本分支中调整深度提示，结合跨模态低秩重参数化、梯度校正动量项和预算索引秩调度，在5000次查询预算下显著优于已有的免反向传播方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调CLIP等视觉-语言模型通常需要全模型反向传播，但在内存受限的边缘设备和专有模型部署场景中仅有前向传播访问权限。已有的免BP零阶提示调优方法要么只调优单一模态，要么搜索空间过大导致收敛需要数千次前向传播，在现实查询预算下不可行。

Method: ZOMP包含三个核心组件：(1) 跨模态低秩重参数化，通过共享因子连接视觉和文本两个分支，保持有效搜索维度较小；(2) 梯度校正动量项，稳定噪声零阶估计；(3) 预算索引秩调度，随查询预算消耗逐步释放模型容量。整体采用SPSA进行零阶优化。

Result: 在13个视觉-语言基准上，匹配5000次查询预算下，ZOMP在少样本准确率和查询效率方面一致优于已有的免BP提示调优方法，并在base-to-new、跨数据集迁移和分布外设定中表现出更好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 联合利用多模态和低秩结构是实现实用、查询高效的免BP提示调优的有效途径。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language models such as CLIP typically requires backpropagation (BP) through the full model, which is infeasible when only forward-pass access is available, as is common for memory-constrained edge devices and proprietary model deployments. Prior BP-free, zeroth-order prompt-tuning methods avoid this requirement but often tune prompts in a single modality or optimize over a search space large enough that convergence requires thousands of forward passes, which is impractical under realistic query budgets. We propose ZOMP (Zeroth-Order Multimodal Prompt tuning), a query-efficient, fully forward-only method that tunes deep prompts in both the vision and text branches of a frozen CLIP model using simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation. ZOMP combines three ingredients: a cross-modal low-rank reparameterization that ties the two branches through a shared factor and keeps the effective search dimensionality small, a gradient-correction momentum term that stabilizes the noisy zeroth-order estimate, and a budget-indexed rank schedule that unlocks capacity as the query budget is spent. Across 13 vision-language benchmarks under a matched 5,000-query budget, ZOMP consistently outperforms prior BP-free prompt-tuning methods in both few-shot accuracy and query efficiency, and it generalizes better across base-to-new, cross-dataset transfer, and out-of-distribution settings. Our results show that jointly exploiting multimodality and low-rank structure is an effective route to practical, query-efficient BP-free prompt tuning.

</details>


### [70] [EvBS: Event-guided Blur Synthesis for Domain-adaptive Motion Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08066)
*Junsik Jung,Seokryun Choi,Yoonki Cho,Woo Jae Kim,Andrew Jeong,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvBS框架，利用事件相机的高时间分辨率解耦运动与视觉内容，通过内在模糊合成和外在模糊合成两种策略生成多样化的训练样本对，用于微调预训练去模糊模型以适配目标领域，从而提升其在未见测试数据集上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练去模糊模型在真实场景中常因训练与测试分布之间的域偏移而性能下降，现有方法受限于运动与视觉内容之间的固有耦合，难以有效生成多样化的训练数据来适配目标域。

Method: EvBS包含两种互补策略：内在模糊合成，利用内容自身的运动模式对清晰图像进行模糊化；外在模糊合成，将模糊块中的运动迁移到不同的清晰内容上。借助事件相机的高时间分辨率，实现了运动与视觉内容的有效解耦。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，EvBS能有效增强现有去模糊模型在未见测试数据集上的鲁棒性，显著提升域自适应去模糊性能。

Conclusion: EvBS通过解耦运动与内容并生成多样化的训练样本对，打破了自然耦合运动的固有约束，为预训练去模糊模型的域适应提供了一种有效的训练数据增强方案。

Abstract: Motion deblurring has achieved remarkable progress with deep learning, yet pre-trained deblurring models often suffer from performance degradation in real-world scenarios due to the domain shift between training and testing distributions. To remedy this, we propose EvBS, an event-guided blur synthesis framework that generates diverse training pairs for calibrating pre-trained models to the target domain. While existing methods are constrained by the inherent entanglement between motion and visual content, our method leverages the high temporal resolution of event cameras to effectively decouple them. This enables us to utilize not only the intrinsic motion that is inherent to the given content but also extrinsic motion transferred from different sources within the target domain, thereby facilitating effective adaptation via fine-tuning. Specifically, EvBS comprises two complementary strategies: Intrinsic-Blur Synthesis, which blurs sharp contents with their own motion patterns, and Extrinsic-Blur Synthesis, which transfers motion from blurry patches to distinct sharp content. This approach generates a diverse set of training pairs that break the inherent constraints of naturally coupled motion and content, resulting in enhanced domain-adaptive deblurring performance. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that EvBS effectively enhances the robustness of existing deblurring models on unseen testing datasets.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [71] [Application of Artificial Intelligence for Fraudulent Banking Operations Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07471)
*Bohdan Mytnyk,Oleksandr Tkachyk,Nataliya Shakhovska,Solomiia Fedushko,Yuriy Syerov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用机器学习算法识别银行欺诈交易的问题。研究提出了基于人工神经网络的欺诈检测模型，并详细探讨了处理不平衡数据集、特征变换和特征工程等提升精度的方法。结果显示逻辑回归算法表现最佳（AUC约0.946），而堆叠泛化方法取得了更高的AUC值0.954。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来受新冠疫情影响，大量银行业务转向线上平台，同时犯罪团伙利用慈善基金等途径实施欺诈，导致银行欺诈案件日益增多。因此，亟需有效的智能手段来识别和防范在线银行欺诈交易。

Method: 采用多种机器学习算法（包括逻辑回归和人工神经网络）进行银行欺诈交易识别，同时提出银行数据预处理技术，并通过处理高度不平衡数据集、特征变换和特征工程等方法提升检测精度，最后对多种算法结果进行可视化比较。此外，还采用了堆叠泛化（stacked generalization）策略来进一步提升模型性能。

Result: 在多种算法对比中，逻辑回归算法表现最佳，AUC值约为0.946；采用堆叠泛化方法后，AUC进一步提升至0.954。基于人工神经网络的模型也有效提升了欺诈交易检测的准确性。

Conclusion: 利用人工智能算法进行银行欺诈识别是数字化社会中具有现实意义的热点问题。堆叠泛化策略优于单一算法，能够更有效地识别银行欺诈交易，为金融安全防护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This study considers the task of applying artificial intelligence to recognize bank fraud. In recent years, due to the COVID19 pandemic, bank fraud has become even more common due to the massive transition of many operations to online platforms and the creation of many charitable funds that criminals can use to deceive users. The present work focuses on machine learning algorithms as a tool well suited for analyzing and recognizing online banking transactions. The study`s scientific novelty is the development of machine learning models for identifying fraudulent banking transactions and techniques for preprocessing bank data for further comparison and selection of the best results. This paper also details various methods for improving detection accuracy, i.e., handling highly imbalanced datasets, feature transformation, and feature engineering. The proposed model, which is based on an artificial neural network, effectively improves the accuracy of fraudulent transaction detection. The results of the different algorithms are visualized, and the logistic regression algorithm performs the best, with an output AUC value of approximately 0,946. The stacked generalization shows a better AUC of 0.954. The recognition of banking fraud using artificial intelligence algorithms is a topical issue in our digital society.

</details>


### [72] [Data-Driven Fire-Zone Segmentation for Improved Short-Term Wildfire Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07472)
*Nicolas Caron,Christophe Guyeux,Hassan Noura,Benjamin Aynes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无监督火灾区分割的野火预测新范式，通过分水岭检测与K-means聚类结合的方式定义预测单元，替代传统均匀网格划分。实验表明，在法国六个省份和六种预测模型上，该方法的平均IoU提升3-6%，且计算成本低（<10秒/配置）并支持并行化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统野火预测模型将研究区域离散化为均匀网格，忽略了火源在空间上的异构分布特征。作者挑战这一范式，认为数据离散化方式比模型选择本身更重要。

Method: 提出一种无监督的火灾区（fire-zone）分割算法，将分水岭检测与K-means聚类相结合，直接从历史火灾模式中定义预测单元，替代统一网格划分。

Result: 在法国六个省份和六种预测模型上，火灾区分割方法一致优于基于网格的方法，平均IoU提升3-6%（取决于空间尺度），且计算轻量（每次配置<10秒），支持完全并行化。

Conclusion: 优化空间离散化策略能为短期野火预测带来显著且可复现的性能提升，表明数据划分方式对预测效果有重要影响。

Abstract: Wildfire prediction models typically discretize study areas into uniform grids, ignoring the heterogeneous spatial distribution of ignitions. We challenge this paradigm by showing that how data is discretized matters more than which model is used. We propose an unsupervised fire-zone segmentation algorithm combining watershed detection with K-means clustering to define prediction units directly from historical fire patterns. Experiments across six French departments and six forecasting models show that fire-zone segmentation consistently outperforms grid-based approaches, with mean IoU improvements of +3--6% depending on spatial scale. The method is computationally lightweight (<10s per configuration) and fully parallelizable. Our results demonstrate that optimizing spatial discretization yields significant, reproducible performance gains for short-term wildfire forecasting.

</details>


### [73] [Evolving Safety Landscape of Multi-modal Large Language Models: A Survey of Emerging Threats and Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07535)
*Xi Li,Shu Zhao,Xiaohan Zou,Fei Zhao,Fuxiao Liu,Yusen Zhang,Cheng Han,Yushun Dong,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的安全格局进行了系统性综述，提出了基于多模态的分类体系来分析安全威胁，并总结了最新的安全策略，最后讨论了开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs通过异构模态对齐与融合提升理解能力，但这种架构转变重塑了机器学习的安全格局。模型复杂度增加和跨模态交互带来了新型威胁（如模态集成受损、模态错位、融合安全风险），而现有基于单模态学习的框架无法涵盖这些新约束，亟需系统性的安全分析。

Method: 首先提出一个多模态基础的威胁分类体系，分析威胁模型的转变，涵盖对抗攻击、数据投毒、越狱和幻觉四大类；然后总结更新后的安全假设，并据此组织整理MLLM安全策略的最新进展。

Result: 提出了一个系统的MLLM安全威胁分类体系，梳理了威胁模型的演变，并基于新的安全假设组织归纳了最新的MLLM安全防护策略。

Conclusion: 作者指出了当前工作中的开放挑战与未来研究方向，以推动多模态系统更原则化、可扩展的安全机制的发展。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) integrate heterogeneous modalities through modality alignment and fusion, enabling stronger understanding and reasoning. However, this architectural shift reshapes the safety landscape of machine learning. Increased model complexity and cross-modal interactions give rise to novel threats, including compromised modality integration, modality misalignment, and fused safety risks, reflecting shifts in threat modeling beyond uni-modal assumptions. These shifts, in turn, impose new constraints on safety solutions not captured by existing frameworks rooted in uni-modal learning. Motivated by these challenges, this survey provides a systematic analysis of the evolving safety landscape of MLLMs. We first propose a multimodal grounded taxonomy of safety threats and analyze shifts in threat models, covering adversarial attacks, data poisoning, jailbreaks, and hallucinations. We then summarize updated safety assumptions and organize recent advances in MLLM safety strategies accordingly. Finally, we discuss open challenges and future directions to inform the development of more principled and scalable safety mechanisms for multimodal systems.

</details>


### [74] [Tracing sources of epistemic uncertainty in deep learning predictions: homo- and hetero-scedastic linearized estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07630)
*Pierre Nodet,Thomas George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将两种经典统计估计器适配到现代深度学习框架中，用于量化两种不确定性来源：偶然不确定性（数据内在噪声）与认知不确定性（局部数据稀缺），并利用近似Fisher信息矩阵实现大规模架构的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中不确定性量化对于提升模型鲁棒性至关重要，但传统方法难以清晰区分偶然不确定性与认知不确定性两种来源，需要更有效的估计工具。

Method: 采用两种经典统计估计器，并利用近期在近似Fisher信息矩阵方面的进展，使其能够扩展到实际深度学习架构中。

Result: 实验表明不同测试点受两种不确定性来源的影响程度不同，验证了估计器在提升现实应用鲁棒性方面的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效区分并量化偶然不确定性与认知不确定性，为深度学习模型的不确定性分析提供了更清晰的视角。

Abstract: We adapt two classical statistical estimators for quantifying uncertainty to modern deep learning, in order to provide clearer insights into uncertainty attributable to two sources : aleatoric uncertainty, or locally scarce data. Our approach leverages recent advances in approximate Fisher Information Matrices, to enable scaling to actual architectures. Experimental results demonstrate how each test points is differentially impacted by both sources, highlighting the practical utility of our estimators in improving the robustness of real-world applications.

</details>


### [75] [SkillConsist: Detecting Inconsistencies in Agent Skills via Bidirectional Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07639)
*Chaofan Meng,Yuhang Zheng,Yingnan Zhou,Sihan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillConsist，一种用于检测LLM Agent技能（Skill）声明与实现之间不一致性的方法。它通过LLM将声明和实现内容分离为行为记录，构建双向行为图，并通过图对齐与图差分来识别冲突。在633个Skill基准测试上，包级检测F1达到87.93%，较最优基线提升20.43个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能的不一致可能暴露未声明的危险行为或导致错误的技能选择。现有方法基于预定义类别或声明范围评估行为，但声明与实现行为可能混合在文本和代码中，且简洁声明可能对应多个关联的实现步骤，现有方法难以有效处理这两种挑战。

Method: SkillConsist包含三个主要步骤：1) 使用LLM将声明和实现内容分离为行为记录（声明侧和实现侧），静态分析补充实现记录；2) 从任一侧的行为记录出发，通过双向图对齐在另一侧搜索候选子图，并沿行为关系扩展直至完整表达源侧行为；3) 通过图差分识别对齐子图之间的冲突并输出检测结果。

Result: 在由ClawHub 500个最受欢迎技能和133个Skill-Inject包构建的633-Skill基准上（含319个不一致和314个一致技能，442个局部不一致标注），SkillConsist在包级检测上达到86.85%精确率、89.03%召回率和87.93% F1，较最佳基线F1提升20.43个百分点；局部化检测达到67.60%精确率、58.14%召回率和62.52% F1。

Conclusion: SkillConsist通过双向行为图对齐和图差分方法有效解决了Agent技能声明与实现之间的不一致性检测问题，显著优于现有基线方法，为Agent技能安全验证提供了有效工具。
