Method: 提出一种后端无关的控制平面，包含五个核心组件：不可变目标（immutable objective）、控制器拥有的测量（controller-owned measurement）、提交键控的崩溃恢复（commit-keyed crash recovery）、证据分级的技能库（evidence-graded skill library）、以及具有标准化记录调用面的工具注册表（tool registry）。LLM控制器驱动可丢弃的工作节点执行训练-评估-改进事务。

Result: 实验结果表明：加固后每次运行的虚假晋升率从0.005-0.021降至0.001；支持任意停止下的随时有效决策；在终止注入下零丢失或零重复效应；签名验证器能捕获六种工件篡改类型中的全部六种。

Conclusion: 标题中的"无操作者"是操作性声明而非选择性声明：操作者可以离开是因为晋升具有证据门控、状态可恢复、能力质量来自记录，而非因为循环选择检查点优于人类（在测量的单一谱系上并非如此）。门控机制切实带来了虚假晋升控制、有效决策和篡改检测等可量化的收益。

Abstract: Coding agents such as Claude Code and Codex close the software loop: a main agent manages the loop, subagents analyze and execute, tools do the work. We port this architecture to robot-policy improvement, where one difference dominates the design: robotic tools---trained policies, training pipelines, data collection---fail routinely, so a tool's quality must be measured, recorded at every call, and expired when the artifact behind it changes. AgenticRobotics is a backend-independent control plane in which an LLM controller drives disposable workers through durable train--evaluate--improve transactions: an immutable objective, controller-owned measurement, commit-keyed crash recovery, an evidence-graded skill library, and a tool registry with a standardized, recorded call surface. The title is an operational claim, not a selection claim: the operator can leave because promotion is evidence-gated, state is recoverable, and capability quality is derived from records---not because the loop picks better checkpoints than a human; on the one lineage we measured, it does not. The gates measurably buy false-promotion control (0.001 per run hardened versus 0.005--0.021 shipped), anytime-valid decisions under optional stopping, zero lost or duplicate effects under kill injection, and six of six artifact-tampering classes caught by a signed verifier.

</details>


### [144] [AeroDPO: Unleashing Lightweight UAV Navigation with High-Fidelity Perception and Automated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07557)
*Peng Xu,Chengcheng Wang,Shaohua Wan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroDPO，一种零成本的自动化直接偏好优化管道，用于解决无人机视觉语言导航中轻量级模型（2B）在行为克隆范式下的鲁棒性缺陷。通过物理模拟状态回滚机制自动生成拒绝动作和偏好动作，将2B模型的成功率提升至49.16%，同时大幅降低碰撞率，达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN端到端范式依赖数十亿参数的大语言模型，推理延迟过高难以在边缘设备部署；而轻量级模型在纯行为克隆训练下缺乏显式负反馈，在OOD场景中碰撞率高，鲁棒性不足。

Method: AeroDPO包含四个核心步骤：(1) 物理模拟状态回滚——检测到碰撞后自动回退环境状态；(2) 提取因果推理错误作为拒绝动作（rejected actions）；(3) 应用解耦特权干预合成避撞偏好动作（preferred maneuvers）；(4) 利用离线视觉语言检查器过滤视觉歧义。由此构建自动化数据飞轮，无需人工标注，对2B轻量模型进行直接偏好优化训练。

Result: 实验表明：轻量级2B模型配合高保真视觉输入即可完全匹配7B大模型的整体成功率；AeroDPO进一步将2B模型在未测绘场景中的成功率提升至49.16%，同时大幅抑制碰撞率，树立了自主空中代理的新SOTA。

Conclusion: 感知质量的重要性远超语言推理容量，参数规模并非UAV-VLN性能的关键瓶颈；通过自动化数据飞轮注入偏好信号，可以在不扩大模型规模的前提下同时提升任务成功率与鲁棒性，为边缘部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) requires rapid and reactive control in complex 3D environments. Recent minimalist end-to-end paradigms show great promise but typically rely on massive language models containing billions of parameters, incurring prohibitive latency for real-world edge deployment. In this paper, we challenge this parameter-heavy reliance. Comprehensive cross-scale evaluations reveal the critical insight that perception quality fundamentally outweighs language reasoning capacity. We demonstrate that a lightweight 2B model equipped with high-fidelity visual inputs completely matches the overall success rates of massive 7B baselines. However, this minimalist policy exposes a fundamental robustness flaw inherent to pure Behavior Cloning (BC). Lacking explicit negative feedback, the agent fails to internalize robust spatial constraints and exhibits alarming collision rates in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. To overcome this vulnerability without relying on unscalable human annotations, we propose AeroDPO, a zero-cost automated Direct Preference Optimization pipeline driven by deterministic physical simulation state rollback. Upon detecting collisions, the system autonomously rewinds the environment to extract causal reasoning errors as rejected actions, applies decoupled privileged interventions to synthesize collision-avoidance preferred maneuvers, and leverages an offline vision language inspector to filter visual ambiguities. By equipping our 2B model with this automated data flywheel, AeroDPO boosts success rates to 49.16% on unmapped scenarios while drastically suppressing collision rates, establishing a new SOTA for autonomous aerial agents.

</details>


### [145] [Learning Physical Interaction: A Survey of Tactile- and Force-aware Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07558)
*Shilin Shan,Chuhao Zhou,Ruize Wang,Xinyan Chen,Xiangyu Chen,Xinyu Zhou,Boyu Ma,Iris Yuxuan Hu,Jingliang Li,Celeste Yuxuan Hu,Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Tianrui Zhu,Zhe Li,Yanjie Ze,Haoran Geng,Zhiyang Dou,Jianxin Bi,Yuejiang Liu,Jianshu Zhou,Jiachen Li,Paul Liang,Tatsuya Harada,Robert Katzschmann,Harold Soh,Na Li,Edward Johns,Danica Kragic,Jan Peters,Wojciech Matusik,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jitendra Malik,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TF-ART，一个面向力觉和触觉感知机器人学习的统一分类框架，从多模态感知和多阶段系统设计的视角系统梳理了接触敏感操作中的机器人学习方法，填补了现有综述缺乏统一视角的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触敏感操作中，机器人需要整合力、触觉、视觉、语言和本体感知等多种模态，并采用多阶段架构衔接高层语义与底层反应式执行。然而现有综述未从统一视角同时涵盖多模态感知和多阶段系统设计，缺乏系统性分类框架。

Method: 提出TF-ART分类体系，将各方法映射到统一的分层结构中，从观测模态组织、异构感知编码与融合、多阶段动作生成与优化、学习策略与反应式末端控制的连接四个维度进行刻画，同时考察物理交互的任务设定与基础设施需求。

Result: 构建了涵盖算法与实践双重视角的统一分类框架，系统梳理了力觉和触觉感知机器人学习方法，明确了该领域的技术体系与前沿研究方向。

Conclusion: TF-ART为力觉和触觉感知机器人学习提供了完整的综述视角，融合了多模态感知与多阶段系统设计，既关注算法方法也重视实践基础设施，为后续研究提供了清晰的参考框架。

Abstract: Physically grounded robot intelligence requires robots to perceive, reason about, and regulate their interactions with the physical world. This capability is particularly critical in contact-sensitive manipulation, where successful task execution depends not only on visual perception and motion generation, but also on force regulation and adaptive control. In this context, recent robot learning methods have made substantial progress by integrating force, tactile, vision, language, and proprioceptive sensing into learned manipulation policies. In parallel, many systems adopt multi-phase architectures that combine high-level policies, action-refinement modules, and low-level controllers to bridge semantic task understanding with reactive physical execution. Despite these advances, existing surveys have not explicitly reviewed force- and tactile-aware robot learning from a unified perspective that jointly captures multimodal sensing and multi-phase system design. This survey addresses this gap by proposing TF-ART, a Tactile/Force-Aware Robot learning Taxonomy for multimodal and multi-phase frameworks, which maps individual methods into a unified hierarchical structure. The framework characterizes how recent works organize observation modalities, encode and fuse heterogeneous sensory inputs, generate and refine actions across multiple phases, and connect learned policies to reactive robot-end control. Building on this methodological view, we further examine the task settings and infrastructure requirements of physical interaction, thereby integrating both algorithmic and practical perspectives on force- and tactile-aware robot learning.

</details>


### [146] [Projection-Retraction MPPI: Exact Constraint-Manifold Control for Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07573)
*Seulchan Lee,Leesai Park,Minhyeong Kang,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出投影-回缩MPPI（PR-MPPI），通过在采样动力学内部强制约束（等式约束投影到子空间、不等式约束投影到半空间），并在返回命令上执行回缩操作到数值公差，使14自由度双臂系统在闭链等式和关节极限/障碍物避开等约束下满足硬约束，而不依赖任务权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI控制广泛应用于操作任务，但对全程约束（如闭链运动学、关节极限、障碍物避开）只能通过成本函数进行软惩罚处理，在强任务成本下约束只能近似满足甚至失效。

Method: 在每个滚转步骤中，将采样速度投影以满足两类约束：等式约束将速度限制在子空间内，不等式约束将其限制在该子空间内的半空间中，从而保证不等式处理不破坏等式。由于投影仅一阶满足约束，有限步长会在等式上产生漂移，因此再将返回的命令回缩到约束上，达到数值公差且独立于任务权重。

Result: 在14自由度双臂系统上验证：仿真中，返回命令在关节极限压力测试和随机障碍物避开场景中，闭链等式满足达到数值公差；真机实验中，Unitree H1-2人形机器人的双臂能够反应式避开移动障碍物。

Conclusion: PR-MPPI通过将硬约束嵌入采样动力学内部并施加回缩操作，有效解决了MPPI中约束仅作软惩罚的问题，在仿真和真机上均验证了约束满足的可靠性。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is widely used in manipulation for its gradient-free, parallel handling of non-convex costs. Manipulation tasks, however, often impose constraints that hold throughout the motion: a closed kinematic chain that two grasping arms keep exactly, or joint limits and obstacle clearances that are never crossed. MPPI handles such constraints only through the cost, as soft penalties that hold approximately and fail under a strong task cost. To address this, we propose Projection-Retraction MPPI (PR-MPPI), which enforces the constraints inside the sampled dynamics. At every rollout step, the sampled velocity is projected to satisfy both constraint types: the equality restricts it to a subspace, and each inequality to a half-space within that subspace, so inequality handling never breaks the equality. This projection, however, satisfies the constraints only to first order, and a finite step leaves a small drift off the equality. Therefore, we retract the returned command back onto the constraint to numerical tolerance and independent of task weighting. We validate PR-MPPI on 14-DoF dual-arm systems. In simulation, the returned commands satisfy the closed-chain equality to numerical tolerance through a joint-limit stress test and randomized obstacle avoidance. On real hardware, the arms of a Unitree H1-2 humanoid reactively avoid a moving obstacle. Code and experiment videos are available at https://rcilab.github.io/prmppi.

</details>


### [147] [Reconfigurable Structural Robotic Assembly: Interlocking 3D Aggregations with Self-Aligning Compound Nested Lattice Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07576)
*Alexander Htet Kyaw,Miana Smith,Paul Richard,Neil Gershenfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自对齐复合嵌套晶格模块（由共轭立方八面体-八面体单元组成），将几何智能编码于材料系统中，同时满足机器人抓取、自对齐、可逆连接、结构性能和三维聚合需求，并通过家具及建筑尺度结构验证了其可重构和循环建造能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人建造系统将材料系统与机器人视为独立设计问题，智能主要集中于硬件、传感、运动规划和控制，缺乏将几何智能嵌入材料系统本身以同时满足机器人操作与结构性能需求的方案。

Method: 提出一种自对齐复合嵌套晶格模块，由共轭立方八面体-八面体单元构成；立方八面体特征提供机器人抓取和对齐表面，八面体特征集成可螺丝释放的卡扣连接器及对应受体；嵌套排列实现x、y、z三轴互锁聚合。

Result: 通过机械臂和移动组装器构建了家具及建筑尺度结构（座椅、跨结构、表面、垂直框架）；压缩测试显示模块刚度4556 N/mm、最大载荷3445 N、压缩模量17.5 MPa；模块可拆解并在不同构型间重复使用。

Conclusion: 该系统成功证明了将几何智能嵌入建筑材料系统可实现机器人抓取与结构性能的一体化设计，并支持可重构和循环建造。

Abstract: Robotic construction systems often treat the material system and the robot as separate design problems, locating intelligence primarily in hardware, sensing, motion planning, and control. This project instead investigates how geometric intelligence can be encoded within architected material systems to simultaneously address requirements for robotic grasping, self-alignment, reversible connection, structural performance, and three-dimensional aggregation. We introduce a self-aligning compound nested lattice module composed of conjoined cuboctahedral-octahedral units. The cuboctahedral features of the modules provide defined surfaces for robotic grasping and alignment, while the octahedral features incorporate screw-releasable snap-fit connectors and corresponding receptors. Additionally, we present a nested arrangement that enables interlocking aggregation along the x, y, and z axes. We demonstrate the system through furniture and architectural scale structures assembled using both a robotic arm and mobile assembler. The resulting configurations include seating, spanning structures, surfaces, and vertical frames. Compression testing of the compound module produced a stiffness of 4,556 N/mm, a maximum load of 3,445 N, and a compressive modulus of 17.5 MPa. The modules can also be disassembled and reused across different configurations, supporting reconfigurable and circular construction.

</details>


### [148] [LIRA: Local Cross-Layer Information Routing for Vision-Language-Action Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07596)
*Zhewei Zhang,Puyue Wang,Guanren Qiao,Yijie Weng,Jiawei Hu,Guo Li,Lujia Wang,Junyan Wang,Tao Gu,Hongliang Lu,Guiliang Liu,Hong Jia,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIRA，一种局部跨层动作条件化机制，通过深度感知的信息路由来改进VLM特征到动作解码器的接口设计，在不改变骨干架构、动作解码器和训练流程的情况下，在多个机器人操控基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的VLM特征到动作解码器的接口设计存在两大局限：要么只暴露表征层级中的狭窄部分，要么将每个解码器块简单匹配到单个VLM层，无法获取跨深度的互补任务证据，限制了任务执行能力。

Method: LIRA将VLM-to-action条件化建模为深度感知的信息路由：基于任务token特征和中层VLM状态派生的LIRA Query特征，为每个并行融合块分配以其对应VLM层为中心的深度对齐局部窗口，并行融合块聚合邻近LIRA Query特征并与任务token特征和本体感受输入集成后进行动作预测。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、CALVIN ABC→D和真实机器人操控任务上，LIRA在相同0.5B参数配置下优于VLA-Adapter基线。零样本迁移到LIBERO-Plus时，平均成功率从59.1%提升至78.0%，提高18.9个百分点，展示了在受控分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LIRA通过深度感知的局部跨层路由机制有效利用了VLM不同层级的互补信息，为VLA模型提供了一种更优的特征-动作接口设计，在性能和鲁棒性方面均取得显著提升，同时保持架构和训练方案不变。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models transform representations from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) into robot actions, yet the interface that routes intermediate VLM features into action decoders remains underexplored. Existing designs either expose only a narrow part of the representation hierarchy or rigidly match each decoder block to one VLM layer, restricting access to complementary task evidence across depths. We introduce LIRA, a local cross-layer action-conditioning mechanism that formulates VLM-to-action conditioning as depth-aware information routing. LIRA operates on task-token features and LIRA Query features derived from intermediate VLM states, then assigns each Parallel Fusion Block a depth-aligned local window centered on its corresponding VLM layer. Parallel Fusion Blocks aggregate neighboring LIRA Query features and integrate them with task-token features and proprioceptive inputs before action prediction. This routing interface leaves the backbone architecture, action decoder, and supervised training recipe unchanged. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, CALVIN ABC$\rightarrow$D, and real-world manipulation, LIRA improves the principal aggregate metrics over the VLA-Adapter baseline under the same 0.5B-parameter configuration. In zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, LIRA increases average success from 59.1% to 78.0%, an 18.9-point gain indicating improved robustness under controlled distribution shifts.

</details>


### [149] [AdaDexGrasp: Adaptive Dexterous Grasping via 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07600)
*Xirui Liang,Jiaqi Liang,Jingkai Xu,Yuran Wang,Ruochong Li,Yuanpei Chen,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Wei Zhan,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，集成了抓取生成、可行性预测和自适应精化，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联的高效表征，实现接触前后的动态抓取调整，在仿真和真实环境中显著提升抓取成功率与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人抓取方法主要依赖视觉输入，缺乏接触后的触觉引导自适应机制，导致鲁棒性和泛化能力受限。人类能够无缝整合视觉感知与触觉反馈实现稳定自适应抓取，而这一能力难以在机器人系统中复现，因此需要构建融合视觉-触觉的统一抓取框架。

Method: 提出统一的视觉-触觉融合抓取框架，包含三个核心模块：抓取生成、可行性预测与自适应精化。核心创新在于高效的视觉-触觉表征，通过将触觉信号与手指身份关联，将物体几何信息与触觉反馈紧密融合。该统一表征支持规划阶段的接触感知抓取姿态生成以及接触后的触觉引导精化，使系统能够推理细粒度的手指-物体交互并动态调整抓取。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界环境中进行了全面实验，结果表明该方法显著提升了抓取成功率，并且在不同物体上展现出更强的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了视觉-触觉统一表征在机器人抓取中的有效性，表明将触觉感知引入抓取流程可显著增强系统的鲁棒性和适应性，为未来灵巧机器人抓取研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Humans achieve stable and adaptive grasps by seamlessly integrating visual perception and tactile feedback, a capability that remains challenging to replicate in robotic systems. Existing robotic grasping approaches predominantly rely on visual inputs and lack mechanisms for tactile-guided adaptation after contact, limiting robustness and generalization. To address this challenge, we propose a unified visuo-tactile-fusion grasping framework that integrates grasp generation, feasibility prediction, and adaptive refinement. At its core, our method introduces an efficient visuo-tactile representation that tightly fuses object geometry with tactile feedback by associating tactile signals with finger identities. This unified representation supports contact-aware grasp pose generation during planning and tactile-guided refinement after contact, enabling the system to reason about fine-grained finger-object interactions and adjust grasps dynamically. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances grasp success rates and generalization across diverse objects.

</details>


### [150] [Enhanced Real-Time 6-DOF Extended Reality Catheter Tracking for Evaluating Potential Improvement in Efficiency, Precision, and Depth Perception for Cardiac Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07606)
*Mohsen Annabestani,Sandhya Sriram,Andrew Kuzemczak,S. Chiu Wong,Alexandros Sigaras,Bobak Mosadegh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于扩展现实(XR)的3D可视化平台，实现6自由度导管实时追踪和患者特异性3D心脏模型可视化。在20名医学生的对照实验中，3D模式比传统2D视图快5倍以上、导管行程减少约5倍，同时提高了定位精度、降低了性能变异性，并改善了运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管3D超声技术不断进步，大多数经皮心脏介入手术仍依赖2D可视化，这限制了术者的深度感知和空间理解能力，影响了手术精度和效率。

Method: 系统结合了自定义机器视觉算法实现5自由度导管追踪，并配以3D打印的机电编码器测量导管滚动，实现完整6自由度运动重建。在概念验证研究中，20名医学生分别在沉浸式3D可视化或传统2D视图下，将心内超声导管导航至6个解剖目标。

Result: 3D条件下参与者平均54.6秒完成、行程1939mm；2D条件平均267.5秒、行程7854mm。3D模式速度提升5倍以上，导管行程减少约5倍，同时提高了靶向精度、降低了性能变异性，且参与者对3D可视化的准确性、速度、可用性和临床价值评价更高。运动学分析显示3D模式下深度轴导航更平滑，而2D用户依赖重复性修正动作。

Conclusion: 基于XR的可视化能够显著提升介入手术培训效率、精度和运动控制，具有重要的临床训练价值。

Abstract: Despite advances in 3D ultrasound, most percutaneous cardiac interventions still rely on 2D visualization, limiting depth perception and spatial understanding. To address this challenge, we developed an Extended Reality (XR)-based platform that enables real-time six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) catheter tracking and visualization within a patient-specific 3D heart model. The system combines a custom machine-vision algorithm for 5-DOF catheter tracking with a 3D-printed electromechanical encoder that measures catheter roll, providing complete 6-DOF motion reconstruction. In a proof-of-concept study, 20 novice medical students navigated an intracardiac echocardiography (ICE) catheter to six anatomical targets using either immersive 3D visualization or a conventional 2D cathlab-style view. Participants in the 3D condition completed the task in 54.6 seconds and traveled 1,939 mm on average, compared with 267.5 seconds and 7,854 mm in the 2D condition. Therefore, the XR-based 3D system was more than 5x faster and required ~5x less catheter travel. The 3D mode also improved targeting precision and reduced performance variability. Participants consistently rated immersive visualization higher for accuracy, speed, usability, and clinical value. Kinematic analysis showed smoother depth-axis navigation in 3D, whereas 2D users relied on repeated corrective movements. These findings demonstrate that XR-based visualization can substantially improve procedural training efficiency, precision, and motor control.

</details>


### [151] [GWM-VLA: Geometry-Aware Latent World Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07619)
*Yanping Zhao,Hang Yu,Yiwei Wang,Chen Ye,Siyu Tian,Di Zhang,Qingjun Wang,Qian Chen,Junqiao Zhao,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GWM-VLA，一种几何感知的潜在世界建模框架，通过几何感知多视角状态编码、全局上下文条件下的目标视角预测以及共享潜在动作表征，提升VLA模型在视觉和环境变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在视觉和环境变化下性能下降明显，虽然潜在世界建模有望提升鲁棒性，但已有方法通常独立编码相机视角并预测整体场景动态，缺乏对相机间几何关系的显式建模。

Method: GWM-VLA包含三个核心组件：(1) 利用VGGT-Ω在每个时间步联合聚合多视角观测，构建几何感知的多视角状态；(2) 潜在世界模型通过聚合后得到的patch和register tokens预测选定目标视角的下一时间步patch tokens，而非预测完整多视角状态；(3) 共享潜在动作表征同时条件化潜在世界模型和流匹配动作头，使潜在预测监督和真实机器人动作监督共同塑造同一潜在动作表征。实验以腕部视角作为目标视角。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人环境中的实验均验证了GWM-VLA的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模多视角几何关系并利用共享潜在动作表征进行联合监督，GWM-VLA有效提升了VLA模型在分布偏移下的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve strong robotic manipulation performance but often degrade under visual and environmental shifts. Latent world modeling offers a promising approach to improving robustness, yet existing methods commonly encode camera views independently and predict holistic scene dynamics without explicitly modeling their geometric relationships. We propose GWM-VLA, a geometry-aware latent world modeling framework for VLA learning. GWM-VLA combines geometry-aware multi-view state encoding, global context-conditioned target-view prediction, and shared latent-action representations grounded by robot-action supervision. Specifically, VGGT-$Ω$ jointly aggregates multi-view observations at each timestep to construct geometry-aware multi-view states. The latent world model predicts the next-step patch tokens of a selected target view using patch and register tokens obtained after multi-view aggregation, thereby retaining multi-view geometric information without predicting the complete multi-view state. We use the wrist view as the target in our experiments, placing greater emphasis on end-effector motion and local gripper-object interactions. Finally, the shared latent-action representations condition both the latent world model and the flow-matching action head, allowing latent-prediction supervision and ground-truth robot-action supervision to jointly shape the same latent-action representations. Experiments across both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of GWM-VLA.

</details>


### [152] [A Reconfigurable Tracked Robot for Enhanced Obstacle Traversal Through Movable Articulation Point and Internal Mass Relocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07624)
*Yuki Uda,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TRASER的可重构履带式机器人，通过铰接式脊柱机构实现铰接点和内部质量的重新定位，从而克服了前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的固有矛盾，显著提升了越障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 履带式机器人在非结构化环境中应用广泛，但其越障能力受限于前端可达性与运动稳定性之间的权衡。传统设计难以同时兼顾这两方面性能。

Method: 采用带式弹簧（tape-spring）机构，将柔性集中在弯曲区域，同时保持机体其余部分的高刚度，从而提升前端可达性和质心移动能力。建立了几何模型和静力学模型，分析铰接点位置和质心位置对台阶及沟壑越障性能的影响。

Result: 实验实现了机器人本体长度74%的台阶越障、66%的悬挂平台越障以及59%的沟壑越障，据作者所述，这是当前履带式移动机器人中报道的最高越障能力。

Conclusion: TRASER通过在弯曲区域局部化柔性并保持机体高刚度的设计，有效提升了两端铰接点的可达性和质量转移能力，显著增强了履带式机器人的越障表现，为该类机器人的设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Tracked robots are widely used in unstructured environments; however, their obstacle traversal capability is fundamentally limited by a tradeoff between front-end reachability and locomotion stability. This study presents TRASER (Tracked Robot with Articulated Spine for Extended Reach), a reconfigurable tracked robot capable of relocating both its articulation point and internal mass. TRASER employs a tape-spring mechanism that localizes compliance to the bending region while maintaining high stiffness in the remaining body, thereby improving both front-end reachability and center-of-mass (CoM) shifting capability. Geometric and static models are developed to analyze the effects of articulation point and CoM position on step and ditch traversal performances. Experiments demonstrate step traversal, suspended-platform traversal, and ditch traversal of 74\%, 66\%, and 59\% of the robot body length, respectively. To the best of our knowledge, these results represent the highest reported obstacle traversal capabilities among tracked mobile robots.

</details>


### [153] [Hölder Signed Distance: A Differentiable, Signed, Parallelizable Metric for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07707)
*Felipe Bartelt,Ali Umut Kaypak,Anthony Tzes,Farshad Khorrami,Luciano C. A. Pimenta,Vinicius M. Gonçalves*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的可微有符号距离函数，用于凸多面体之间的距离计算。通过引入Hölder最小和最大算子替换经典SDF中的min-max算子，得到闭式表达的Hölder有符号距离，避免了迭代算法的收敛问题，且天然适合GPU并行化。实验验证了其计算性能和机器人操控中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典欧几里得有符号距离函数并非处处可微，现有替代方案往往在可微性、符号信息和计算效率之间存在权衡，难以同时满足机器人运动规划与控制中实时优化的需求。

Method: 首先提出可微的Hölder最小和最大算子，然后将其替换经典SDF公式中的原始min-max算子，从而得到闭式形式的Hölder有符号距离。该方法无需迭代算法，避免了收敛问题，并可自然地在GPU上进行并行化计算。

Result: 通过运行时对比实验证明了该方法在计算性能上的优势，并在机械臂操控实验中验证了其在控制应用中的适用性和实际可行性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Hölder有符号距离在保持可微性的同时，通过闭式解兼顾了计算效率和符号信息，为机器人运动规划与控制中的实时距离优化提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Computing distances between sets is essential in robotic motion planning and control, where differentiable gradients enable real-time optimization. The Euclidean Signed Distance Function (SDF), however, is not differentiable everywhere, and existing alternatives often sacrifice differentiability, sign information, or computational efficiency. In this letter, we introduce a novel differentiable signed distance between convex polyhedra. To this end, we first propose differentiable versions of the minimum and maximum operators, termed the Hölder minimum and Hölder maximum. We then replace the original min-max operators in the classical SDF formulation, yielding the Hölder signed distance. Unlike prior differentiable distance formulations that rely on iterative algorithms, our approach is computed in closed form, eliminating convergence issues while remaining naturally amenable to GPU parallelization. We validate the practical advantages and computational performance of the proposed distance through runtime comparisons with existing approaches. We also present a robotic manipulator experiment, demonstrating its suitability for applications in control.

</details>


### [154] [Complete, Scalable, and Robust Prioritized Planning for Multi-Robot Ordered Storage and Retrieval at Maximum Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07734)
*William Zhang,Tzvika Geft,Jingjin Yu,Kostas Bekris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对自动化仓库中高密度存储与高效检索之间的根本性矛盾，提出了一种新的多机器人有序存储与检索问题（OSRP）求解框架。该方法利用无重排存储布局的几何特性，设计了一个基于优先级的在线多智能体路径规划算法，在保证完备性和无死锁的同时实现可扩展性，实验表明其性能随机器人数量近似线性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化仓库需要在最大化存储密度和高检索吞吐量之间取得平衡。拼图式存储架构通过消除通道来提升容量，但在高密度空间中协调多机器人面临死锁等计算难题，现有集中式规划器难以兼顾完备性与可扩展性。

Method: 提出一种新的多机器人有序存储与检索问题公式化方法，聚焦于由单一边界可访问的矩形网格。利用无重排存储布局的特性（几何可行性与执行效率之间的桥梁），设计在线、基于优先级的多智能体路径搜索算法，通过利用存储布局的具体不变量来保证完备性并防止死锁。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在机器人数量达到网格宽度C之前，使完工时间随机器人数量呈近线性改善。同时，为支持鲁棒性所增加的算法开销可忽略不计，系统使用鲁棒存储布局处理出发序列不确定性，在执行速度上与不鲁棒的基线相比无显著损失。

Conclusion: 该工作通过利用无重排布局的结构特性，成功弥合了几何可行性与执行效率之间的差距，提供了一种兼具完备性、无死锁且可扩展的多机器人仓库存储检索方案，并能在不牺牲执行性能的前提下处理不确定性。

Abstract: Automated warehouses face a fundamental trade-off between maximizing storage density and achieving high retrieval throughput. While puzzle-based storage (PBS) architectures increase capacity by eliminating aisles, coordinating multiple robots in these high-density spaces is computationally challenging due to the potential for deadlocks. This paper introduces a novel multi-robot formulation for the ``ordered storage and retrieval problem at maximum capacity''. The focus is on rectangular grids accessible from a single boundary, where loads need to be first stored up to full capacity and then efficiently retrieved, given a planned departure sequence. This work bridges the gap between geometric feasibility and execution efficiency by leveraging the properties of relocation-free arrangements. These properties guide an online, prioritized multi-agent path-finding algorithm, which is the main contribution of this work. Unlike general centralized planners, the approach exploits the specific invariants of the storage arrangement to guarantee completeness and prevent deadlocks, enabling scalability. Experiments demonstrate that the method achieves near-linear improvement in makespan with respect to the number of robots, up to $m = C$, where $C$ is the grid width. Crucially, the algorithmic overhead of supporting robustness is negligible; the system handles uncertainty in departure sequences using robust storage arrangements with no significant penalty in execution speed compared to the non-robust baseline.

</details>


### [155] [Enhancing Autonomous Vehicle Navigation with a Clothoid-Based Lateral Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07740)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于回旋曲线（Euler spiral）与自适应预瞄距离的自动驾驶车辆横向控制策略。通过准反馈控制算法在每个时间步构建最优回旋曲线生成转向输入，并利用前馈补偿器修正车辆横向动力学延迟，在TruckSim和Simulink联合仿真中验证了该方法在不同驾驶场景下的横向控制性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶横向控制方法在弯道场景中存在路径跟踪精度不足、乘客舒适度差以及车辆侧翻风险等问题，需要一种能够实现平滑曲率过渡且具备自适应能力的控制策略。

Method: 采用基于回旋曲线（Euler spiral）的横向控制方案，结合自适应预瞄距离机制，根据实时车辆动力学和道路几何动态调整预瞄距离；使用准反馈控制算法在每个时间步构造最优回旋曲线以生成转向输入，并引入前馈补偿器（lead filter）来补偿车辆横向动力学滞后，提升控制响应速度和稳定性。

Result: 通过TruckSim与Simulink联合仿真验证，所提出的控制策略在多种驾驶场景下均展现出显著的横向控制性能提升，包括增强的横向稳定性和路径跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 回旋曲线结合自适应预瞄的横向控制策略在自动驾驶车辆中具有良好的有效性；未来工作包括将控制器扩展到更高速度应用场景，以及进一步优化减少横向偏移误差，特别是针对铰接式车辆。

Abstract: This study introduces an advanced lateral control strategy for autonomous vehicles using a clothoid-based approach integrated with an adaptive lookahead mechanism. The primary focus is on enhancing lateral stability and path-tracking accuracy through the application of Euler spirals for smooth curvature transitions, thereby reducing passenger discomfort and the risk of vehicle rollover. An innovative aspect of our work is the adaptive adjustment of lookahead distance based on real-time vehicle dynamics and road geometry, which ensures optimal path following under varying conditions. A quasi-feedback control algorithm constructs optimal clothoids at each time step, generating the appropriate steering input. A lead filter compensates for the vehicle's lateral dynamics lag, improving control responsiveness and stability. The effectiveness of the proposed controller is validated through a comprehensive co-simulation using TruckSim and Simulink, demonstrating significant improvements in lateral control performance across diverse driving scenarios. Future directions include scaling the controller for higher-speed applications and further optimization to minimize off-track errors, particularly for articulated vehicles.

</details>


### [156] [CoCoNav: Conformal Control for Safe Robot Navigation in Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07751)
*Cheng Guo,Mingzhe Ni,Zheng Liang,Yihu Ling,Yuan Hu,Michele Caprio,Daniele Pucci,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoNav，一种结合在线共形校准与运行时认证规划的群体导航框架，通过水平特定的共形比例-积分控制器自适应轨迹误差边界，并采用"先松弛后验证"的规划器在保证求解可行性的同时实现碰撞安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反应式方法易产生振荡行为，而预测式规划器要么将预测视为精确、要么依赖过于严格的误差模型；将保守不确定性集作为硬约束又会导致MPC不可行。需要一种既能应对预测误差漂移又能保持求解可行性的导航方法。

Method: 提出CoCoNav框架：(1) 水平特定的共形比例-积分(conformal PI)控制器在线校准轨迹误差边界，调节长期经验覆盖率以应对变化的预测误差；(2) "松弛-验证"(relax-then-verify)规划器先用软约束MPC生成名义轨迹以保持求解可行性，再在执行前连同应急机动一起针对校准后的边界进行独立认证。

Result: 在仿真和四足机器人实验中，CoCoNav在碰撞避免、任务成功率和导航效率之间取得了优于对比基线的平衡。

Conclusion: CoCoNav通过将预测不确定性的在线校准与运行时安全性认证解耦，有效兼顾了求解可行性与碰撞安全，为群体导航中不确定预测误差下的安全高效规划提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient robot navigation in crowds requires anticipating pedestrian motion despite uncertain and potentially shifting prediction errors. Existing reactive methods can produce oscillatory behavior, while predictive planners often treat forecasts as exact or rely on restrictive error models. Incorporating conservative uncertainty sets as hard constraints can also render model predictive control (MPC) infeasible. We propose \textit{CoCoNav}, a crowd-navigation framework that combines online conformal calibration with runtime-certified planning. A horizon-specific conformal proportional--integral controller adapts trajectory-error bounds to regulate long-run empirical coverage, enabling the framework to respond to changing prediction errors. A \textit{relax-then-verify} planner preserves solver feasibility by generating nominal trajectories with soft-constrained MPC and separately certifying them, together with contingency maneuvers, against the calibrated bounds before execution. Simulations and quadruped experiments show that CoCoNav achieves a favorable balance among collision avoidance, task success, and navigation efficiency relative to the evaluated baselines.

</details>


### [157] [Drone-Assisted UAV-UGV Collaboration for Autonomous Navigation in Snow-Covered Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07797)
*Shreyam Gupta,P. Agrawal,Priyam Gupta,R. Gautam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无人机-无人地面车辆（UAV-UGV）协作导航框架，用于高海拔积雪地形。通过定制的Efficient U-Net进行实时道路分割（96.5%准确率）、EKF融合GPS/IMU实现无人机定位（误差±0.5米）、YOLOv5与深度数据实现UGV视觉跟踪，并利用动态路径规划应对雪堆偏移，在能见度低的测试环境中实现了成功导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高海拔积雪地形中，低能见度与不稳定的地面使传统导航方法失效，亟需一种结合空中地面协作的鲁棒导航方案。

Method: 采用定制高效U-Net架构（结合新颖的合成雪数据增强技术）进行实时道路分割；使用扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）融合GPS和IMU数据实现UAV定位；通过YOLOv5与UAV的RGB-D深度数据构建UGV视觉跟踪管线；最后利用动态路径规划算法根据分割结果调整雪堆偏移。

Result: 道路分割准确率达96.5%，UAV定位最大位置误差为±0.5米，在能见度受限的测试环境中导航成功且偏差极小。

Conclusion: 该协作框架有效解决了高海拔积雪环境下的导航难题，验证了轻量级分割模型与多传感器融合在恶劣环境中的可行性与实用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a collaborative UAV-UGV navigation framework for high-altitude, snow-covered terrain, where reduced visibility and unstable ground render conventional methods ineffective. We introduce a custom efficient U-Net architecture that falls under the computational constraints for real-time road segmentation, utilizing a novel synthetic snow data augmentation technique to achieve 96.5% segmentation accuracy. For UAV localization, we implement an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fusing onboard GPS and IMU data, achieving a maximum observed positional error of +-0.5 meters. The UGV position is determined via a visual tracking pipeline using YOLOv5 and depth data from the UAV's RGB-D camera. A dynamic path planning algorithm utilizes this segmentation to adjust for snow drifts, enabling successful navigation in obscured test environment with minimal deviation.

</details>


### [158] [A Mixed-Stiffness Anthropomimetic Fingertip Broadens the Operating Range for Coin Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07887)
*Kaigen Go,Yinlai Jiang,Hiroshi Yokoi,Shunta Togo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用混合刚度（中心软、两侧硬）的拟人指尖来抓取硬币等薄而平的物体。相比均匀柔软指尖，混合刚度指尖在更多测试条件下保持高成功率，并成功提升了薄物体抓取的可操作范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的薄、平物体（如硬币）抓取方法需要重新定向物体、接触其底面或添加额外的指甲机构。此前研究发现刚性指甲可以阻止软垫变形从而形成几何约束，本文进一步探究是否可以通过材料选择而非解剖结构在垫内创造额外的约束边界，以扩展该约束成立的条件范围。

Method: 研究人员制作了中心为Shore E10硅胶、两侧为Shore A60硅胶的拟人指尖，并与均匀软E10指尖进行对比。使用自动化装置执行倾斜旋转指尖捏取，指尖垫接触硬币侧面并在无缝隙的情况下将其从水平接触状态提起。实验系统性地变化水平接近距离、垂直手指位移和食指旋转角度，并针对不同硬币尺寸进行调优。

Result: 混合刚度指尖在几何约束形成及向稳定抓取的过渡阶段，在更多测试条件下保持高成功率；均匀指尖在沿手指轴方向硬币位置变化时表现更好，存在条件依赖的权衡；无指甲手指在所有36种条件中均失败；调优后两种指尖都能抓取全部六种日本硬币面额。

Conclusion: 薄物体抓取的操作范围不仅取决于垫的柔软度，还取决于刚度在指甲支撑垫内的布置位置，边界放置位置可作为指尖设计的一个候选参数。

Abstract: Robotic grasping of thin, flat objects such as coins on hard surfaces remains challenging because conventional methods require reorienting the object, accessing its underside, or adding a dedicated nail mechanism. We previously showed that a rigid nail arrests soft-pad deformation and thereby forms a geometric constraint that improves precision grasping. Here we asked whether an additional constraint-forming boundary, created within the pad by material choice rather than by anatomy, could extend the conditions under which that constraint holds. We fabricated anthropomimetic fingertips with Shore E10 silicone at the center and Shore A60 at the sides, and compared them with uniformly soft E10 fingertips. An automated apparatus performed an oblique rotational tip pinch in which the pad engaged the coin's lateral surface, lifting it from flush contact with no gap beneath it. Over variations in horizontal approach distances, vertical finger displacements, and index-finger rotation, the mixed-stiffness pair maintained high success rates across more tested settings than the uniform pair during both geometric-constraint formation and the transition to a stable grasp. The nail-free pair failed in all 36 conditions of Experiment 1-1. However, the uniform pair performed better when coin position along the finger axis was varied, a condition-dependent trade-off. After tuning for coin size, both fingertip types grasped all six Japanese denominations. These results suggest that the operating range for thin-object grasping depends not only on pad softness but also on where stiffness is placed within a nail-supported pad, making boundary placement a candidate fingertip design variable.

</details>


### [159] [Auditing Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches in Multimodal Robot Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07895)
*Simon Holk,Ryosuke Takanami,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Yueh-Hua Wu,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPF（多模态概率融合）框架，用于事后审计机器人演示数据集中存在的"指令-轨迹不匹配"（ITM）问题——即轨迹行为正确但配错了语言指令。该方法无需训练，将每个模态视为专家，通过局部邻域一致性和全局原型相似度估计任务标签分布，并以预测熵加权进行多模态融合。在LIBERO基准和真实机器人数据上取得了最佳检测和标签纠正效果，且审计能显著改善下游策略学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作策略训练所用的机器人演示数据集中常存在一种隐蔽但有害的错误：轨迹行为正确但配错了语言指令（即指令-轨迹不匹配ITM）。这类错误与失败的rollout不同，往往看起来合理，会破坏策略学习的语言-行为映射关系，因此亟需一种有效的事后审计方法来检测和纠正这些不匹配。

Method: 提出MMPF（Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion）框架，这是一个免训练的审计框架。其核心思路：(1) 将每个模态（如视觉、语言）视为独立的专家；(2) 通过局部邻域一致性（local neighborhood agreement）和全局原型相似度（global prototype similarity）估计任务标签分布；(3) 使用预测熵加权（predictive-entropy weighting）在"专家乘积"（product of experts）机制中融合各模态的预测。

Result: 在LIBERO基准（注入指令不匹配）和带噪声的真实机器人数据上，MMPF取得了最强的ITM检测和标签纠正精度。实验还表明审计能改善大多数需要语言来消歧任务的下游策略学习。真实机器人实验证实该方法可提升策略表现，并揭示了过滤演示与重新标注之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: MMPF作为一种无需训练的审计框架，能够有效检测和纠正机器人演示数据集中的指令-轨迹不匹配问题，并通过提升数据质量进而改善下游策略学习性能。该方法在处理语言消歧任务时尤为有效，同时为实际应用中"过滤坏数据vs重新标注"的选择提供了有价值的权衡分析。

Abstract: Robot demonstration datasets used to train vision-language-action policies can contain a subtle but harmful failure mode: trajectories that are behaviorally correct but paired with the wrong language instruction. We study post-hoc auditing of these Instruction-Trajectory Mismatches (ITMs). Unlike failed rollouts, ITMs often look plausible, and can corrupt the language-behavior mapping learned by the policy. We propose Multimodal Probabilistic Fusion (MMPF), a training-free auditing framework that treats each modality as an expert, estimates a task-label distribution from local neighborhood agreement and global prototype similarity, and then fuses modalities with predictive-entropy weighting in a product of experts. Across LIBERO benchmarks with injected instruction mismatches and noisy real-robot data, MMPF achieves the strongest overall ITM detection and label correction accuracy. We also show that auditing improves most downstream policy learning in settings where language is needed to disambiguate the task. We demonstrate in real robot experiments that our method can achieve improved policy performance and show the trade-off of filtering demonstrations compared to relabeling.

</details>


### [160] [4D-WAM: Infusing Spatiotemporal Awareness into World Action Models through Trajectory Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08023)
*Lishan Yang,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Pingyue Sheng,Zheng Fang,Ziyang Zhou,Junjie He,Haodong Yan,Jiayi Chen,Nan Sun,Qiao Sun,Lingqiao Liu,Yan Wang,Yuxiang Gao,Feras Dayoub,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出4D-WAM，一种模型无关的训练策略，通过表示对齐将3D轨迹场中的时空知识注入世界动作模型（WAMs），利用运动对齐和目标对齐两个互补目标，提升模型在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性和泛化能力上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs通常将视频表示为2D像素空间，与机器人执行动作的3D空间存在表示鸿沟；近期3D方法虽引入3D信息，但未能充分利用3D结构的动态特性。

Method: 提出4D-WAM训练策略，包含两个互补目标：1）运动对齐——对齐相邻帧间的时序特征变化，鼓励模型建立局部4D感知；2）目标对齐——通过最小化源帧与最终目标之间的注意力相似性分布差异，引导模型推断最终目的地。两者结合提供局部运动监督和长时程目标引导。

Result: 在多种基础模型上的分布内和分布外实验中，该策略在空间理解、执行精度、鲁棒性、泛化能力和多场景适应性方面均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过局部运动监督与长时程目标引导的结合，4D-WAM能够使WAMs学习到轨迹级别的时空表示，从而弥合2D表示与3D执行之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Building on recent advances in world models, World Action Models (WAMs) jointly model video prediction and action generation. However, they typically represent videos in 2D pixel space, creating a representation gap with 3D space in which robotic actions are executed. Recent 3D approaches introduce 3D information, but fail to fully exploit the dynamics of 3D structures. In this work, we propose 4D-WAM, a model-agnostic training strategy that injects spatiotemporal knowledge from 3D trajectory fields into WAMs through representation alignment. To this end, we introduce two complementary objectives: 1) motion alignment, which aligns temporal feature variations across adjacent frames and encourages the model to build local 4D awareness during training, and 2) destination alignment, which guides the model to infer the final destination from the source frame by minimizing the gap between their attention-like similarity distributions. Together, these objectives provide both local motion supervision and long-horizon goal guidance, enabling WAMs to learn trajectory-level spatiotemporal representations. Extensive in-distribution and out-of-distribution experiments across different base models demonstrate the model's improvements in spatial understanding, execution precision, robustness, generalization, and versatility.

</details>


### [161] [DA-NBV: A Direction-Aware Next-Best-View Planner for Efficient 3D Reconstruction of Ships at Sea](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08025)
*Jiaming Chen,Juntao Yang,Zhentao Zou,Qi Ming,Yi Yu,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang,Xue Jiang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DA-NBV，一种方向感知的下一最佳视角规划策略，通过引入可学习的Position Advantage Field（PAF）增强传统占据状态的方向观测信息，结合局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖整形奖励，并在船用SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置海况仿真环境下验证，将重建完整性提升约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%，同时提高路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上船舶三维重建对海事监管和自主作业至关重要，但现有数据采集依赖人工设计的轨迹，成本高且可扩展性差。现有的NBV策略仅建模空间占用而忽视方向观测历史，导致船舶复杂上层结构和严重自遮挡场景下重建不完整；海上的波浪摇晃和有限机载功率进一步加剧了挑战，需要更高效和方向感知的规划方法。

Method: 提出DA-NBV方向感知NBV策略：1）在传统占据状态中增强方向观测统计信息；2）引入可学习的Position Advantage Field（PAF）利用方向信息引导视角选择；3）采用局部约束动作空间和非线性覆盖整形奖励提升扫描效率；4）构建船舶专用的SeaShip-3D数据集和可配置海况仿真环境。

Result: 在不同升沉、横摇和纵摇条件下，DA-NBV将重建完整性提升约3个百分点，Chamfer距离降低43%，并实现了更高的路径效率。

Conclusion: 方向感知的状态建模能够有效缓解船舶观测中的自遮挡问题，所提方法在重建完整性和精度上均优于现有NBV策略，为海上自主三维重建提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Accurate 3D reconstruction of ships at sea is important for maritime supervision, damage assessment, and autonomous maritime operations. Although 3D reconstruction has advanced considerably, high-quality data acquisition still largely relies on manually designed trajectories or skilled operators, resulting in high costs and limited scalability. Next-best-view (NBV) planning automates this process by selecting subsequent viewpoints based on the current state. However, existing NBV policies mainly model spatial occupancy while overlooking directional observation history. This limitation is particularly problematic for ships: their complex superstructures and severe self-occlusions require observations from multiple viewpoints, and insufficient directional coverage often yields incomplete reconstructions. These challenges are further amplified at sea, where wave-induced heave, roll, and pitch continuously alter the ship's pose and surface visibility. Meanwhile, wind disturbances and limited onboard power impose stricter requirements on scanning efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose DA-NBV, a direction-aware NBV policy that augments the conventional occupancy state with directional observation statistics. We introduce a learnable Position Advantage Field (PAF) that uses directional information to guide viewpoint selection. The policy further adopts a locally constrained action space and a nonlinear coverage-shaping reward to improve scanning efficiency. We also develop the ship-oriented SeaShip-3D dataset and a configurable sea-state simulation environment. Experiments under varying heave, roll, and pitch conditions show that DA-NBV improves reconstruction completeness by approximately 3 percentage points and reduces Chamfer distance by 43% while achieving higher path efficiency.

</details>


### [162] [Compiling and Benchmarking Task-State Horizons for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08036)
*Meiqi Wang,Shichao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务状态地平线（TSH）概念，用于度量智能体在长时程具身任务中需要跟踪的任务相关状态转换跨度，并据此构建了RoboGraph任务编译器和包含84个场景、588个episode的基准测试，评估了15个先进智能体模型，发现多数模型在高TSH任务中表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基准主要从动作序列长度和子任务复杂度来衡量任务难度，忽略了智能体在长时程任务中需要跟踪由自身探索和环境动态引起的不断演变的任务相关世界状态这一独特挑战。

Method: 提出RoboGraph：一种将状态转换依赖转化为可执行符号图的任务编译器，从空间和时间因果关系（包括执行过程中的意外失败与干预）构建任务状态地平线，并生成具有不同TSH的基准测试集。

Result: 在语义和视觉闭环环境中评估了15个先进智能体模型，结果表明多数模型在高TSH任务中表现挣扎，在维持、探索和更新任务相关状态方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前前沿智能体模型在长时间跨度内跟踪和更新任务相关状态的能力仍然不足，TSH是一个评估长时程具身智能的重要且被忽视的维度，RoboGraph和相应基准为该方向提供了有效评估工具。

Abstract: Frontier agentic models are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for long-horizon embodied tasks. Existing robotic benchmarks have advanced long-horizon evaluation, but primarily characterize difficulty through action-sequence length and subtask complexity, overlooking a distinct challenge: agents must track evolving task-relevant world states induced by both their exploration and environmental dynamics. We define the span of task-relevant state transitions that an agent must track as task-state horizon (TSH). To evaluate how agent performance varies with TSH, we introduce RoboGraph, a robotic task compiler that translates state-transition dependencies into executable symbolic graphs. Specifically, RoboGraph constructs task-state horizons from spatial and temporal causal dependencies, including those induced by unexpected failures and interventions during task execution. Building on RoboGraph, we release a benchmark comprising 588 episodes across 84 scenes with varying TSHs. Experiments evaluating 15 advanced agentic models in both semantic and visual closed-loop environments show that most models struggle with demanding TSHs, revealing substantial gaps in maintaining, exploring, and updating task-relevant state over long horizon.

</details>


### [163] [PhysX-CoT: Structured Physical Reasoning from a Single Image to Simulation-Ready 3D Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08053)
*Jie Huang,Xiaohe Li,Jiahao Li,Fangli Mou,Chen Qian,Yuqiang Fang,Junhao Fan,Kaixin Zhang,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysX-CoT将单图像3D资产生成建模为显式的结构化物理推理过程，通过可解析的部分级状态轨迹（分解、2D/3D定位、关系、粗略几何、表面线索）进行独立监督、条件化和奖励优化，在统一评测协议下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图像3D资产生成方法采用输出中心视角，将图像到3D的推理隐式化，导致部件放置与局部形状在全局坐标token流中纠缠，且中间物理状态从未被显式监督、条件化或验证，这是性能提升的瓶颈。

Method: PhysX-CoT提出将资产生成转换为有序、可机器解析的部分级物理状态轨迹，覆盖分解、2D/3D定位、关系、粗略几何和表面线索；几何被分解为3D包围盒（承载放置信息）与局部编码（承载形状信息）；使用CoT对齐的GRPO优化解析有效性、定位、几何、放置和物理一致性。

Result: 在统一协议（相同骨干、数据、冻结解码器，重训所有可学习基线）下，PhysX-CoT在几何、尺度和物理属性指标上优于最接近的全任务基线；Oracle、token匹配和状态顺序对照实验表明显式状态是功能性而非装饰性的；在虚幻引擎5中生成的资产具有高解析、碰撞和关节有效性。

Conclusion: 显式结构化物理推理状态对单图像3D资产生成是有效且必要的，它能解耦放置与形状推理，提供可监督、可条件化、可验证的中间物理状态，从而显著提升生成资产的几何精度和物理一致性。

Abstract: Simulation-ready 3D assets are central to robotics and embodied AI. Generating them from a single image is usually framed as a vision-language model that emits a serialized asset for a decoder to turn into geometry and physical fields, leaving the image-to-3D reasoning implicit. We argue the limiting factor is this output-centric view: part placement and local shape are entangled in one global-coordinate token stream, and the intermediate physical states are never exposed for supervision, conditioning, or verification. PhysX-CoT instead casts single-image asset generation as an explicit structured physical reasoning process, an ordered and machine-parseable trajectory of part-level states covering decomposition, 2D and 3D grounding, relations, coarse geometry, and surface cues that we separately supervise, use to condition geometry, and treat as reward targets. Geometry is factorized so that 3D boxes carry placement and local codes carry shape, and CoT-aligned GRPO optimizes parse validity, grounding, geometry, placement, and physical consistency. Under a unified protocol that retrains all learned baselines on the same backbone, data, and frozen decoder, PhysX-CoT outperforms the closest full-task baseline across geometry, scale, and physical-attribute metrics. Oracle, token-matched, and state-order controls show the explicit states are functional rather than cosmetic, and in Unreal Engine~5 the generated assets parse, collide, and articulate at high validity.

</details>


### [164] [SurgWMBench: A Vision-Based Benchmark for World-Modeling Surgical Instrument Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08070)
*Huanrong Liu,Weiliang Huang,Bob Zhang,Weichao Cai,Chunlin Tian,Qingbiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SurgWMBench，一个面向短时程手术运动规划与动力学预测的视觉基准，用于评估手术世界模型在器械运动预测和视觉状态演化方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法缺乏对器械运动的显式建模，而工具运动预测方法又无法捕捉手术视频状态的耦合演化。此外，现有手术世界模型评估依赖FVD等生成质量指标，与器械运动规划需求不匹配，且缺乏公开数据集和标准化评估协议。

Method: SurgWMBench利用术中图像序列和器械历史轨迹，评估模型在近未来器械运动预测方面的能力，同时检验模型在连续展开或输入扰动下的稳定性。

Result: 论文提出了一个新的基准SurgWMBench，但摘要中未给出具体的实验结果数据。

Conclusion: SurgWMBench填补了手术世界模型在运动中心评估方面的空白，提供了评估手术器械运动规划能力的标准化基础设施，推动可靠手术规划模型的发展。

Abstract: Reliable surgical planning requires models that move beyond recognizing the current surgical step or imitating expert demonstrations, and instead anticipate how instrument motion reshapes subsequent operative states. Most surgical video understanding methods focus on recognizing phases, actions, or workflow states, while providing limited support for explicitly modeling instrument motion. Conversely, existing tool motion prediction methods can forecast instrument trajectories, but they generally do not capture the coupled evolution of future surgical video states. World models offer a natural framework for jointly modeling visual state transitions and instrument motion dynamics. However, existing surgical world model studies remain largely centered on visual generation quality, relying on generation-oriented metrics such as FVD and CD-FVD. These metrics are poorly aligned with instrument motion planning, as they do not directly measure whether predicted trajectories are geometrically accurate, temporally coherent, or actionable for downstream planning. This limitation is partly structural, since the field lacks public datasets and standardized evaluation protocols that provide the benchmarking infrastructure needed to assess motion-centric capabilities in surgical world models. In this paper, we introduce SurgWMBench, a vision-based benchmark for short-horizon surgical motion planning and dynamics prediction. Given intraoperative image sequences and historical instrument trajectory, SurgWMBench evaluates both near-future instrument motion prediction and stability under continuous rollout or input perturbations.

</details>


### [165] [Event-Time Hybrid Optimal Control for Robotic Table Tennis Serves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08157)
*Thomas Gossard,Till Köpff,Andreas Ziegler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件时间最优控制（OCP）的乒乓球机器人发球生成方法，通过优化球拍撞击速度与姿态，直接约束弹跳、过网和落点时间，实现对球速和旋转的精确控制，同时保证机器人运动学与动力学可行性。数值实验和KUKA Agilus真机实验验证了该方法的高效性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人乒乓发球需要高球速和旋转，同时满足机器人运动学与动力学限制。与对打不同，发球必须在本方台面弹跳并过网，构成包含非线性飞行和撞击动力学的混合系统，传统固定步长方法计算效率低下且约束处理困难。

Method: 该方法包含两个OCP：(1) 第一个OCP为事件时间OCP，以撞击时间、弹跳时间、过网时间和落点时间作为优化变量，直接优化球拍撞击速度和姿态；(2) 第二个OCP将球拍速度和姿态转化为满足关节位置、速度和力矩限制的完整运动轨迹，确保运动学与动力学可行性。

Result: 相比固定步长+根定位方法，事件时间公式将中位求解时间降低4.1倍，同时保持相当的落点精度、旋转精度和发球有效性。真机实验实现了受控的落点布置以及上旋、下旋和侧旋发球，平均落点误差为13.1±7.3 cm，旋转速率最高达30 rps。

Conclusion: 事件时间最优控制能够高效生成物理有效、运动学与动力学可行的乒乓球发球，同时充分考虑了非线性气动效应和碰撞影响，展示了在实际机器人系统上的实用价值。

Abstract: Robotic table tennis serves require high ball velocity and spin while respecting the robot's kinodynamic limits. Unlike rally strokes, a valid serve must also bounce on the server's side and clear the net, yielding a hybrid system with nonlinear flight and impact dynamics. We formulate spin-controlled serve generation as an event-time \ac{OCP} that optimizes the racket impact velocity and orientation together with the bounce, net-crossing, and landing times, enabling direct enforcement at phase boundaries of table-bounce and net-clearance constraints. The racket velocity and orientation are then converted into a complete kinodynamically feasible motion through a second \ac{OCP} enforcing joint-position, velocity, and torque limits. We evaluate the method numerically and on a KUKA Agilus robot. Compared with a fixed-step formulation with root localization, the proposed event-time formulation reduces the median solve time by a factor of 4.1 while maintaining comparable landing accuracy, spin accuracy, and serve validity. Real-robot experiments demonstrate controlled placement and topspin, backspin, and sidespin serves, with a mean landing error of $13.1 \pm 7.3$~cm and spin rates up to 30~rps. These results show that event-time optimal control efficiently generates physically valid, kinodynamically feasible serves while accounting for nonlinear aerodynamic and impact effects.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-modal Interactive Control of Robotic Arm based on Offline Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08183)
*Hanxiao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了"Socratic Models-ChatGLM"算法，基于离线大型语言模型（如ChatGLM），通过PyBullet平台实现机械臂的多模态交互控制，能够低成本地解决复杂文本-图像集成的多步长时程机器人操控任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数大型语言模型（如ChatGPT）并非开源友好，且需要用户持续付费使用AI服务，成本高昂。因此，需要在本地服务器部署开源大语言模型，以更低成本和更稳定的免费使用方式设计和实现具身AI算法。

Method: 提出"Socratic Models-ChatGLM"算法，以离线大语言模型为核心，结合多模态信息（文本和图像），通过PyBullet仿真平台实现机械臂的交互式控制，支持多步长时程的复杂操控任务。

Result: 该算法在多模态交互控制机械臂方面表现良好，展现出处理复杂文本-图像集成多步长时程机器人操控任务的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 基于开源离线大语言模型实现具身智能控制是可行且高效的方法，能以较低成本和稳定免费的使用方式完成复杂的机器人操控任务，为具身AI算法设计提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have significantly revolutionized the modern society with numerous advanced interactions between humans and AI agents, whereas the usage of most large language models including ChatGPT are not friendly open-sourced and must require the users paying a lot for such AI services continuously. Therefore, deploying open-sourced large language models on local servers can be considered as an efficient approach to design and implement creative embodied AI algorithms with lower cost and more stable free usage. Inspired by this ordinary motivation, we originally propose and implement the "Socratic Models-ChatGLM", which is a well-performed algorithm for multi-modal interactive control of robotic arm based on offline large language models via the facile PyBullet platform, even presents extraordinary potential to address complicated text-image integrated multi-step long-horizon robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [167] [Spatiotemporal Context-dependent Personalized Movement Compensation in Delayed Telemanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08200)
*Sai Jiang,Zonghe Chua*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以人为本的个性化运动缩放方法，针对每个参与者在不同延迟、方向和距离条件下拟合专属的缩放参数，以缓解遥操作中的通信延迟。20名参与者完成了虚拟仿真中的延迟到达任务，缩放增益在仿真中计算以最小化过冲，并在仿真和遥手术机器人上评估辅助效果。结果表明运动缩放相较无辅助条件一致提升性能，关键指标获得20-25%的增益，在较长延迟下效果最为显著，个性化在中等延迟和短距离内收运动中展现出额外精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信延迟是遥操作中的核心挑战，会破坏视觉运动协调并降低任务精度。运动缩放是应对延迟引起过冲的有效手段，但传统部署使用统一增益，忽略了个体和情境（方向、距离、延迟）的差异性，限制了辅助效果的上限。

Method: 采用个性化、延迟/方向/距离专属的缩放参数拟合方法。20名参与者在虚拟仿真器中执行延迟到达任务，缩放增益通过最小化每种实验条件下的平均过冲来计算。评估在仿真环境和遥手术机器人上同时进行，使用过冲、端点误差、轨迹平滑度、运动经济性及复合误差-时间指标衡量性能。

Result: 运动缩放相较于无辅助条件一致提升遥操作性能，关键指标上获得20-25%的性能增益，且此效果在较长延迟下最为显著。个性化方法在中等延迟下的短距离内收运动中展示了额外的精度提升。

Conclusion: 个性化运动缩放具备作为更自适应性遥操作框架基础的潜力，通过整合情境信息可提升遥操作程序的安全性与精度，为未来结合更多上下文感知的辅助系统提供了实证支撑。

Abstract: Communication delay remains a central challenge in telerobotics, where it disrupts visuomotor coordination and reduces task precision. Motion scaling is an effective countermeasure to delay-induced overshoot, yet typical deployments rely on uniform gains that neglect individual and contextual variability. We propose a human-centered method that fits personalized delay-, direction-, and distance-specific scaling parameters for each participant. We conducted experiments with twenty participants who performed delayed reaching tasks in a virtual simulator. Scaling gains were computed to minimize mean overshoot in simulation in each combination of experimental conditions. Evaluation was done in simulation and on a telesurgical robot to evaluate assistance benefits. Performance was assessed across multiple delays, distances, and movement directions using overshoot, endpoint error, trajectory smoothness, economy of motion, and a composite error-time metric. Motion scaling consistently improved performance relative to unassisted trials, yielding up to 20-25% performance gains in key metrics. Effects were most pronounced at longer delays. Personalization demonstrated additional accuracy benefits for inward reaching at a short distance under moderate delay. The results highlight the potential of personalized scaling as a foundation for more adaptive frameworks that integrate contextual information to improve the safety and precision of teleoperated procedures.

</details>


### [168] [Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment for Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08273)
*Hwanhee Kim,Jaehyun Jang,Seungmin Cha,Hyeonseo Yun,Donghoon Lee,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ALVA，一种基于动作与语言条件化的视频评估器，用于多步自然语言指令下的具身智能体任务。它通过预训练VLM两阶段处理：先基于执行动作总结帧间视觉转换，再对照指令评估总结生成离散进度分数。实验表明该方法具有近零假阳性率，作为闭环策略优化的终端反馈时优于静态图像和嵌入相似度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于最终帧匹配或连续嵌入相似度的反馈机制可能忽略完成指令所必需的关键中间转换，难以准确评估完整轨迹中的任务进度。

Method: 利用预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）进行两阶段评估：第一阶段对帧间视觉转换进行动作条件化的总结，第二阶段将生成的总结与自然语言指令进行对照评估，输出离散的轨迹级进度分数。

Result: 在模拟3D家庭环境中，ALVA表现出保守的评估模式，假阳性率接近零；作为闭环策略优化的终端反馈时，优于静态图像和基于嵌入的视觉基线，缩小了与真实标注oracle的性能差距。

Conclusion: 动作与语言条件化的视频评估可作为模拟具身控制任务中可解释的有效反馈机制。

Abstract: Vision-based embodied agents executing multi-step natural language instructions require feedback mechanisms that assess task progress over complete trajectories. Conventional approaches based on final-frame matching or continuous embedding similarity may overlook intermediate transitions that are necessary for determining whether an instruction has been completed. We propose ALVA (Action- and Language-Conditioned Video Assessment), a trajectory evaluator that conditions its assessment on visual observations, the executed action sequence, and the natural language instruction. The method uses a pre-trained vision-language model (VLM) in two stages: it first summarizes frame-to-frame visual transitions conditioned on the executed actions and then assesses the generated summary with respect to the instruction to produce a discrete trajectory-level progress score. In simulated 3D household environments, ALVA exhibits a conservative assessment pattern with near-zero false-positive rates. When used as terminal feedback for closed-loop policy optimization, it provides more effective feedback than the evaluated static image and embedding-based visual baselines and reduces the performance gap to a ground-truth oracle. These results support action- and language-conditioned video assessment as an interpretable feedback mechanism for the evaluated simulated embodied-control tasks.

</details>


### [169] [Scalable High-Speed Lateral Control for Single-Body and Articulated Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08293)
*Aashish Shaju,Steve Southward,Mehdi Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的横向控制框架，用于高速场景下单车体和铰接式自动驾驶车辆的鲁棒路径跟踪。该框架基于clothoid控制器，集成了三项关键改进：优化预瞄距离的切向检查与Frechet距离方法、实时轨迹段分类以动态调整预瞄搜索范围、以及响应横向误差和前方路径几何的双自适应速率控制预瞄机制。同时支持灵活的跟踪点选择和曲率-转向查表，可适用于不同车辆配置。通过TruckSim和Simulink高保真仿真验证，结果表明在双车道变换和蜿蜒道路场景中实现了稳定的高速路径跟踪、降低振荡、有效预瞄自适应和低横向误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速条件下单车体和铰接式车辆的路径跟踪面临预瞄距离不精确、横向振荡、以及不同车辆配置（如牵引车后轴、铰接点、挂车位置）需要统一控制架构的挑战，现有方法缺乏可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 集成切向检查与Frechet距离方法优化预瞄距离并抑制振荡；2) 实时轨迹段分类实现预瞄搜索范围的动态调整；3) 双自适应、速率控制的预瞄机制，响应横向偏差和前方路径几何；4) 灵活的跟踪点选择与曲率-转向查表，支持多种车辆配置无需改变核心控制架构。

Result: 在TruckSim和Simulink高保真仿真中，对单车体和铰接式车辆在双车道变换和蜿蜒道路场景进行了评估。结果表明实现了稳定的高速路径跟踪、减少振荡、有效的预瞄自适应、较低的横向误差和可接受的横向加速度，覆盖多种车辆配置。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了统一横向控制架构的可行性，可从单车体自动驾驶车辆扩展至铰接式自动驾驶车辆，为异质车队提供通用横向控制解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable lateral control framework for robust path tracking of single-body and articulated autonomous vehicles at high speeds. A clothoid-based controller is extended with three key adaptations: 1) an integrated tangential check and Frechet distance method for optimized lookahead and oscillation mitigation; 2) real-time trajectory segment classification for dynamic adjustment of the lookahead search range; and 3) a dual-adaptive, rate-controlled lookahead mechanism responsive to cross-track error and upcoming path geometry. To support different vehicle configurations, the framework also incorporates flexible tracking point selection and curvature-to-steering lookup tables, enabling control of points such as the tractor rear axle, hitch point, or trailer-related locations without changing the core control architecture. The controller is evaluated using high-fidelity TruckSim and Simulink simulations for both standalone and articulated vehicles across dual lane changes and winding-road scenarios. Results demonstrate stable high-speed path tracking, reduced oscillations, effective lookahead adaptation, low cross-track errors, and acceptable lateral acceleration across different vehicle configurations. The results support the use of a unified lateral control architecture that can scale from single-body to articulated autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [170] [Diminishing Returns of Intelligence: The Non-Linear Relationship Between LLM Scale and User Perception in Short-Duration Open-Ended Social Human-Robot Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08320)
*Amanda Rasille Røn Volf,Morten Roed Frederiksen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过一项19名参与者的被试内研究，考察了LLM参数规模（4B、8B、30B）对短时开放式社交人机交互体验的影响，发现更大模型并未显著提升用户感知，表明在短暂社交HRI中模型规模存在收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM越来越多地用于驱动具身社交智能体，但尚不清楚在短暂的人机接触中，更大规模的模型是否真的能改善用户的感知体验。

Method: 采用被试内设计，19名参与者与由Qwen3-VL模型（4B、8B、30B参数）驱动的机器人面部进行交互，并从感知智能、自然度、愉悦度和幽默感四个维度进行评估。

Result: 结果显示30B模型相比小型变体没有显著的整体偏好优势，在感知自然度和智能度上也未显著优于4B模型。此外，AI交互频率与30B模型的智能排名之间存在显著关系，表明经验更丰富的用户可能对模型能力差异更敏感。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在短暂的开放式社交人机交互中，模型规模扩展带来的收益递减，对话流畅性、响应性和社交适当行为等可能与参数数量同样重要甚至更为关键。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to drive embodied social agents, yet it remains unclear whether larger models improve user perception during brief human-robot encounters. This paper examines the effect of LLM parameter size on short-duration, open-ended social interactions with a robot interface. In a within-subjects study, 19 participants interacted with robot faces driven by Qwen3-VL models at 4B, 8B, and 30B parameters. Participants evaluated the interactions in terms of perceived intelligence, naturalness, enjoyment, and humor. Results showed no significant overall preference for the 30B model over the smaller variants, including no significant advantage over the 4B model in perceived naturalness or intelligence. A significant relationship between AI interaction frequency and intelligence rankings for the 30B model suggests that more experienced users may be more sensitive to differences in model capability. Overall, the findings indicate diminishing returns from model scaling in brief open-ended social HRI, where conversational flow, responsiveness, and socially appropriate behavior potentially matter as much as raw parameter count.

</details>


### [171] [MPPI Planning with Gaussian Based Human Cost Function for Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08323)
*Chinmay Mundane*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性高斯交互场（PGIF），一种时空代价公式，将行人预测在整个规划时域内传播并编码为各向异性高斯排斥场，用于MPPI控制器的代价函数中，在不增加计算开销的情况下显著降低拥挤场景中的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPPI控制器在安全机器人导航中通常将行人视为静态点障碍，未考虑其未来运动，在动态场景中会低估风险，导致碰撞率过高。

Method: 基于预测性高斯交互场（PGIF）的时空代价公式：将行人的运动预测沿完整规划时域传播，编码为与行人运动方向对齐的各向异性高斯排斥场；场的前向扩散随行人速度增长，形成"运动锥"危险区域，对进入行人前进路径的机器人轨迹施加强惩罚。公式为闭式解，可完全并行化所有rollouts。

Result: 在300个随机人群场景、三个密度级别下评估，PGIF-MPPI在各密度级别均实现0%碰撞率，而vanilla MPPI最高达82%碰撞率；同时保持实时规划性能，计算开销可忽略。

Conclusion: PGIF为采样式规划器提供了一种高效且可拓展的行人动态感知代价编码方式，在不牺牲实时性的前提下显著提升拥挤动态场景中的导航安全性，验证了将预测融入代价函数相较于静态障碍假设的有效性。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in crowded spaces requires planning that accounts for where people will be, not only where they are now. Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is an effective sampling-based planner, but many implementations encode humans as static point obstacles at their current positions, underestimating risk in dynamic scenes. We propose Predictive Gaussian Interaction Fields (PGIF), a spatiotemporal cost formulation that propagates pedestrian predictions forward over the full planning horizon and encodes them as anisotropic Gaussian repulsive fields aligned with each pedestrian's direction of motion. The forward spread of each field grows with the pedestrian's speed, creating a motion cone danger zone that penalises robot trajectories entering the pedestrian's path of travel more strongly than those approaching from behind. The formulation is closed-form and fully parallelisable across rollouts, adding no measurable computational overhead. Evaluated over 300 randomised crowd scenarios at three density levels, PGIF-MPPI achieves a 0\% collision rate at every density level, compared with up to 82\% for vanilla MPPI, while maintaining real-time planning performance.

</details>


### [172] [Stochastic Physics-Informed Neural Networks on Lie Groups for Learning Underwater Vehicle Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08356)
*Evan F. Palmer,Ross L. Hatton,Geoffrey A. Hollinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于李群几何和欧拉-庞加莱动力学的随机物理信息神经网络框架，用于学习水下航行器的随机运动动力学模型，在仿真和真实港口环境中均展现出高精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器的运动建模对于自主执行海洋任务（如基础设施检查和科学采样）至关重要，但传统基于物理的方法难以准确刻画水下运动的复杂性，因此需要数据驱动的解决方案。

Method: 基于欧拉-庞加莱动力学和李群几何构建随机物理信息神经网络架构，保持水下航行器的物理与几何约束；利用保结构随机积分技术，并结合矩匹配和有限维匹配方法确保几何一致的训练过程。

Result: 在仿真环境及真实港口（航行器绕码头桩柱导航）中验证，结果表明该方法能够学习准确且鲁棒的动力学模型，支持在复杂海洋环境下安全执行基于模型的控制。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动框架成功结合物理先验与神经网络，实现了对水下航行器随机动力学的精确建模，为恶劣海洋环境下的安全自主控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate models of underwater vehicle motion are needed for autonomous execution of marine tasks like infrastructure inspection and scientific sampling. However, such motion is challenging to characterize using traditional physics-based methods. This paper presents a novel data-driven framework for learning stochastic underwater vehicle dynamics. Using Euler-Poincaré dynamics and the geometry of Lie groups, we develop a stochastic physics-informed neural network architecture that respects the physical and geometric constraints of underwater vehicles. Our approach leverages structure-preserving stochastic integration and builds upon moment matching and finite dimensional matching to ensure geometrically-consistent training. We evaluate our approach in simulation and on an underwater vehicle navigating dock pylons in a harbor environment. The results demonstrate that our method learns accurate and robust dynamics models, enabling safe model-based control in challenging marine environments.

</details>


### [173] [EsaacSim: A Multimodal Event Camera Add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08522)
*Ignacio Bugueno-Cordova,Malte Kuhlmann,Nicolás Navarro-Guerrero,Miguel Campusano,Rodrigo Verschae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EsaacSim，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的事件相机多模态仿真插件，支持灰度与Bayer RGGB事件生成，提供同步的RGB、APS、事件、深度和IMU输出，并采用运动引导的帧间隙合成策略提升有效时间分辨率，实验表明其在5种分辨率下可实现240-960Hz的有效事件率，且GPU内存开销低于400MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件视觉在机器人领域日益重要，但受限于传感器可用性以及缺乏与现代机器人平台集成的仿真工具，限制了其采用与发展。

Method: 基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim构建多模态事件相机插件，支持可配置分辨率与灰度/Bayer RGGB事件生成，通过原生ROS2接口输出同步的RGB、APS、事件、深度和IMU数据，并提出运动引导的帧间隙合成策略以提高有效时间分辨率，同时保持与Isaac Sim渲染管线的兼容性。

Result: 在代表性机器人场景中实现了同步多模态仿真，5种事件相机分辨率下有效事件率达240-960Hz；灰度事件生成耗时6.98-27.28ms，Bayer RGGB事件生成耗时7.58-29.16ms，在RTX 4060 GPU上额外内存占用低于400MB。

Conclusion: EsaacSim能够支持机器人研究中的在线多模态事件相机仿真与合成数据生成，作者已发布早期版本并报告了当前架构与性能。

Abstract: Event-based vision is becoming an increasingly important sensing paradigm for robotics, yet its adoption remains limited by sensor availability and the lack of integrated simulation tools for modern robotics platforms. This paper presents EsaacSim, a multimodal event camera add-on for NVIDIA Isaac Sim that enables online simulation of configurable event cameras with grayscale and Bayer RGGB event generation. The framework supports multiple event camera resolutions and provides synchronized RGB, APS, event, depth, and IMU outputs through native ROS2 interfaces. A motion-guided frame-gap synthesis strategy further increases the effective temporal resolution while preserving compatibility with the Isaac Sim rendering pipeline. Experimental evaluation demonstrates synchronized multimodal simulation across representative robotic scenes and efficient online performance over five event camera resolutions at effective event rates from 240 to 960Hz. Event stream generation requires 6.98--27.28ms for grayscale events and 7.58--29.16ms for Bayer RGGB events while using less than 400MB of additional GPU memory on an NVIDIA RTX~4060 GPU. These results show that EsaacSim enables supports online multimodal event-camera simulation for robotics research and synthetic data generation. We release an early version of the simulator and report its current architecture and performance.

</details>


### [174] [Curriculum Generation under Structured Parametric Environments for Robust Navigation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08545)
*Prishita Ray*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于重参数化的单向梯度优化课程生成框架，用于结构化连续环境参数下的强化学习策略训练，并引入分布偏移正则化目标以提升多模态观测空间（图像+标量）下的鲁棒性。实验在Car Racing和Bipedal Walker两个连续控制环境中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体的鲁棒导航策略需要在连续变化的环境条件（如转弯率、障碍物、摩擦、坑洞、坡度等）下保持泛化能力。课程学习提供了一种逐步调整训练环境的机制来改善泛化，但如何在样本高效且自动化的方式下设计课程仍然是一个挑战。

Method: 提出重参数化课程生成框架，利用单向梯度优化处理结构化连续环境参数；针对图像与标量输入组成的多模态观测空间，引入分布偏移正则化目标以促进学习更细粒度的潜在表征。

Result: 在五个随机种子的实验中，该方法在Car Racing和Bipedal Walker两个环境中始终优于普通策略训练、随机参数采样、手动课程、基于边界的方法、SPRL、ALP-GMM和反向课程学习等基线方法。消融实验验证了重参数化课程机制的有效性，同时表明辅助正则化目标的收益与具体环境相关。

Conclusion: 该方法为连续环境参数下的自动化课程生成提供了高效方案，重参数化机制是提升性能的核心因素，而正则化目标的效果因环境而异。

Abstract: Robust navigation policies for autonomous agents must generalize across continuously varying environmental conditions such as turn rates, obstacles, friction, pits, and slopes. Curriculum generation provides a principled mechanism for improving generalization by progressively adapting training environments, but designing such curricula in a sample-efficient and automated manner remains challenging. This paper proposes a reparameterized curriculum generation framework for structured continuous environment parameters using unidirectional gradient-based optimization. To improve robustness in multimodal observation spaces consisting of image-based and scalar inputs, a distribution-shift regularization objective is incorporated to encourage the learning of finer-grained latent representations. The proposed method is evaluated across two continuous-control OpenAI Gym environments: a 2D obstacle-based Car Racing variant and Bipedal Walker variant, where coupled environment parameters jointly influence policy performance. Across five random seeds, our method consistently outperforms vanilla policy training, random parameter sampling, manual curricula, frontier-based methods, Self-Paced Reinforcement Learning (SPRL), Absolute Learning Progress with Gaussian Mixture Models (ALP-GMM), and reverse curriculum learning baselines. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the reparameterized curriculum mechanism across both environments, while highlighting environment-dependent benefits of the auxiliary regularization objective.

</details>


### [175] [Vid2WAM: Distilling Video Diffusion Priors into World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08558)
*Chenhao Qiu,Ruixiang Wang,Runyi Zhao,Sixu Lin,Songen Gu,Shufeng Nan,Guiliang Liu,Kui Jia,Yanwei Fu,Simo Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Vid2WAM，一种离线蒸馏框架，利用大规模视频基础模型的视觉扩散先验来训练紧凑的世界动作模型（WAM）学生网络，通过任务条件未来轨迹和逆动力学模型生成伪动作进行双重监督，在减少专家演示依赖的同时提升新任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过联合建模未来视觉动态和动作来改进机器人策略学习，但其扩展性和泛化能力受限于对昂贵专家演示数据的依赖。现有方法忽略了未来监督是否必须来自目标任务专家轨迹这一关键问题，需要探索替代监督来源。

Method: Vid2WAM采用三种核心设计：(1)利用视频基础模型生成任务条件未来滚动轨迹，直接监督学生网络的未来预测分支；(2)通过逆动力学模型从生成视频中恢复具身特定的伪动作用于动作学习；(3)引入源感知残差动作适应机制，学习围绕共享动作骨干的源特定修正，缓解合成与真实监督干扰。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，Vid2WAM在有限专家演示条件下有效提升了新任务泛化能力和数据效率，同时保持了低延迟推理性能。

Conclusion: 该工作证明未来监督不一定需要来自目标任务专家轨迹，视频基础模型可以作为有效的替代监督来源，实现高效、可扩展的机器人策略学习，且推理时无需视频教师和逆动力学模型。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot policy learning by jointly modeling future visual dynamics and actions. However, their scalability and generalization remain constrained by their reliance on costly expert demonstrations. We challenge this by asking whether future supervision for WAMs must originate from target-task expert trajectories. In this paper, we propose Vid2WAM, an offline distillation framework that transfers visual diffusion priors from a large video foundation model into a compact WAM student. Given an observation and language instruction, Vid2WAM distills supervision through two complementary channels: task-conditioned future rollouts directly supervise the student's future prediction branch, while an inverse dynamics model recovers embodiment-specific pseudo-actions for action learning. To robustly integrate synthetic and real supervision, we introduce source-aware residual action adaptation that learns source-specific corrections around a shared action backbone and mitigates interference from noisy pseudo-actions. During inference, both the video teacher and inverse dynamics model are discarded, leaving only the WAM student for efficient deployment. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that Vid2WAM improves novel-task generalization and data efficiency under limited expert demonstrations while preserving low-latency inference.

</details>


### [176] [Knowledge-Distilled End-to-End Reinforcement Learning for Smooth 6-DOF Thrust Control and Rapid Adaptation to Ocean Currents in Remotely Operated Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08598)
*Tiankuang Wen,Huiping Li,Gang Liu,Yong Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TSRCA-PPO方法，通过两阶段蒸馏学习框架实现遥控潜水器在洋流扰动下的端到端强化学习最优控制，相比传统P-PID控制器在稳态误差、响应速度、能效和推力平滑度等指标上均显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端强化学习方法在洋流扰动下难以同时实现低稳态跟踪误差、快速瞬态响应、节能运行和平滑推力输出，且缺乏统一控制框架。

Method: 提出TSRCA-PPO方法，采用两阶段蒸馏学习框架学习近最优策略，核心创新在于奖励函数设计和特权多编码器架构。

Result: 仿真结果表明TSRCA-PPO在所有评估指标上优于传统级联P-PID控制器：稳态位置误差、稳态姿态误差、调节时间、能量指数和推力平滑指数分别降至P-PID对应值的42.7%、76.5%、10.6%、93.5%和15.9%。消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: TSRCA-PPO通过创新的奖励函数与特权多编码器架构，在洋流扰动下实现了兼顾精度、速度、能效和平滑性的统一遥控潜水器控制，为端到端强化学习在水下机器人控制中的应用提供了新方案。

Abstract: With the continuous improvement of computational capabilities, end-to-end reinforcement learning has been rapidly developed for remotely operated vehicles control. Nevertheless, existing end-to-end reinforcement-learningbased methods still face challenges in achieving optimal control under oceancurrent disturbances. In particular, there remains a lack of a unified control framework that can simultaneously achieve low steady-state tracking error, rapid transient response, energy-efficient operation, and smooth controlforce outputs under disturbances. To address the issue, this paper proposes the thrust smoothness rapid current adaptation proximal policy optimization (TSRCA-PPO) method which learns a near-optimal strategy by a twostage distillation learning framework. The core innovations of this work lie in the reward-function design and the privileged multi-encoder architecture. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each module. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed TSRCA-PPO method consistently outperforms the conventional cascaded P-PID controller across all evaluation metrics. Specifically, TSRCA-PPO reduces the steady-state position error, steady-state attitude error, settling time, energy index, and thrustsmoothness index to 42.7%, 76.5%, 10.6%, 93.5%, and 15.9% of the corresponding P-PID values, respectively.

</details>


### [177] [RAG-Based Auto-Configuration for Industrial Fieldbus Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08618)
*Aadil Gani Ganie,Saad Ezzini,Naveed Farooz Marazi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SysName是一个面向工业设备配置的生产级流水线，通过混合稠密-稀疏检索索引（基于ECLASS、AAS和SOSA/SSN构建的本体图增强）和BGE-M3编码器加交叉编码器重排序器，结合本地LLM（T=0.1）生成本体对齐的JSON-LD配置，并配备两阶段弃权门控和SHACL验证。在28个字段级查询上达到0.96 HitRate@10，字段级F1=0.87，端到端单设备配置耗时2.6-6.6秒，零不安全写入和零静默失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业设备调试需要工程师从异构PDF手册中手动提取数百个协议特定参数并转录到监控系统，这一过程耗时且易出错。论文旨在解决这一手动流程的效率与可靠性问题。

Method: 构建混合稠密-稀疏检索索引，由来自ECLASS、AAS、SOSA/SSN的本体图增强，使用BGE-M3编码器和交叉编码器重排序器检索手册相关段落；本地LLM（T=0.1）通过协议特定提示和四步修复流水线生成本体对齐的JSON-LD配置；两阶段弃权门控（重排序器分数阈值+IRI解析比率）在SHACL验证前阻止不安全的LLM调用。

Result: 混合检索器在28个字段级查询上达到0.96 HitRate@10，重排序器将MRR@10从0.56提升到0.63；生成器字段级F1=0.87，12次运行中9次完全匹配；H100 GPU上端到端每设备2.6-6.6秒，五设备基准零不安全写入和零静默失败。案例研究在UR5e机器人上达到字段级F1=1.0。

Conclusion: SysName证明了自动化工业设备配置端到端管线的可行性，组件级评估定位了OPC-UA生成这一系统性失败点（端到端指标不可见的盲区），验证了弃权机制对安全性的关键保障作用。

Abstract: Industrial device commissioning requires engineers to manually extract hundreds of protocol-specific parameters from heterogeneous PDF manuals and transcribe them into supervisory control systems, a time-intensive, error-prone workflow. This paper presents SysName, a production-oriented pipeline that automates device configuration end-to-end for Modbus RTU, OPC-UA, Profibus DP, and CANopen. It builds a hybrid dense-sparse retrieval index augmented by an ontology graph derived from ECLASS, AAS, and SOSA/SSN, using a BGE-M3 encoder with a cross-encoder reranker to surface relevant manual passages. A local LLM (T=0.1) generates ontology-aligned JSON-LD configurations via protocol-specific prompts and a four-step repair pipeline. A two-stage abstention gate, combining a reranker-score threshold and an IRI resolution ratio, blocks unsafe LLM invocations and filters low-coverage configurations before SHACL validation. On a gold set of 28 field-level queries, the hybrid retriever reaches 0.96 HitRate@10, and the reranker raises MRR@10 from 0.56 to 0.63 with perfect score separation for abstention. The generator attains field-level F1=0.87 with exact match on 9 of 12 runs. End-to-end runs on an H100 GPU complete in 2.6-6.6s per device with zero unsafe writes and zero silent failures on a five-device benchmark; every unsuccessful run is flagged by abstention or deployment verification. Component-wise evaluation localises the single systematic failure to OPC-UA generation, invisible to end-to-end metrics alone. A case study commissions a physics-simulated Universal Robots UR5e robot from unmodified vendor documentation (254-page manual, 8-page register list, 496 chunks), reaching field-level F1=1.0 over three runs with read-back and joint-consistency verification. An ablation study and comparison with five industrial-LLM systems complete the analysis.

</details>


### [178] [Anchor-Based AI Approach for Pre-Crash Object Detection Utilizing Micro-Doppler Signatures in Automotive Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08701)
*Patrick Zaumseil,Rainer Engert,Dagmar Steinhauser,Jonathan Wache,Soumya Dewangan,Thomas Brandmeier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于锚点的AI模型，利用高分辨率车载雷达数据的微多普勒特征进行碰撞前目标检测与运动参数估计，通过创新的雷达图像膨胀技术增强局部特征，相比传统跟踪方法在动态场景中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级自动驾驶的安全系统（如智能安全气囊和自适应安全带）依赖可靠的目标检测和运动参数估计，以实现不可逆安全系统的早期可靠激活。当前雷达点云的稀疏性和波动性以及多径反射和幽灵目标等问题，制约了传统方法在碰撞前场景中的检测可靠性。

Method: 提出一种基于锚点的AI模型，处理高分辨率雷达数据并重点关注微多普勒特征；开发了创新的雷达图像膨胀技术，在特征输入通道上放大局部雷达模式（如微多普勒特征），以应对点云稀疏和波动问题；使用系列传感器记录碰撞前相关场景的真实雷达数据集进行验证和对比。

Result: 该锚点AI模型优于先进的汽车雷达跟踪方法，在动态场景中能有效估计目标参数，展现出对不同数据集的强大处理能力，并显著减少了近场关键区域的漏检情况。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了基于微多普勒特征的锚点AI模型在碰撞前目标检测中的显著优势，提升了安全系统激活的可靠性和准确性，为未来自动驾驶安全系统的智能化发展提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Advanced automated driving presents significant potential to improve modern automotive safety systems, but it depends highly on the reliable activation of restraint systems. Forward-looking sensors are crucial for immediate and precise object detection. Recent developments in automotive radar technology enable detailed environment detection and the recognition of high-resolution features, such as micro-Doppler signatures. Combined with advanced AI techniques, these features significantly enhance object detection and improve the accuracy of kinematic parameter estimation. This is essential for the early and reliable activation of irreversible safety systems, such as smart airbags and adaptive seat belts.
