Therefore, an anchor-based AI model is presented, designed to process high-resolution radar data with an explicit focus on micro-Doppler signatures to improve pre-crash object detection. Furthermore, these signatures can improve the accuracy of kinematic object parameter estimation and reduce false negatives, especially in the critical near-field. To address the challenges of sparse and fluctuating radar point clouds, an innovative radar-image dilation technique on the feature input channels was developed to amplify local radar patterns, like micro-Doppler features. Therefore, this approach increases the system's reliability and increases its ability to detect objects in pre-crash scenarios despite radar multipath reflections and ghost objects. In order to investigate the applicability and compare the model's performance with advanced automotive radar tracking methods, a radar data set using series sensors and pre-crash relevant scenarios was recorded. The results demonstrate the advantages of the anchor-based AI model over established tracking approaches. It excels at estimating object parameters in dynamic scenarios and underscores its ability to process different data sets effectively.

</details>


### [179] [WA-SpecDec: World-Aware Speculative Decoding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08725)
*Zikang Wen,Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WA-SpecDec，一种世界感知的投机解码框架，通过在VLA预填充阶段注入世界模型物理场景感知，在保持松弛接受规则不变的前提下提升任务成功率和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA投机解码中的松弛接受规则是场景无关的，固定的token距离容忍度在自由空间和安全区域附近场景中同样对待动作偏差，导致靠近接触区域时可能引发碰撞或抓取失败。

Method: 在VLA预填充阶段注入世界模型推导的物理场景感知，生成共享的世界感知预填充状态，用于草稿提案和目标验证，不改变松弛接受规则。

Result: 在三种最先进的松弛接受方案中保持更高任务成功率并支持更长接受前缀；在可比较成功率操作点上，相比VLA投机解码实现1.5倍匹配成功加速，平均减少18.6%的近接触失败。

Conclusion: 场景感知的世界模型信息能有效提升VLA投机解码的安全性与效率，在保持相同任务成功率的同时实现更快的闭环推理。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies generate robot controls autoregressively, making closed-loop latency dominated by repeated target-model forward passes. Speculative decoding reduces this cost by verifying blocks of draft action tokens in parallel, and recent VLA methods further relax token-level acceptance because small differences in action-token space often map to similar continuous controls. However, this relaxation remains scene-agnostic. A fixed token-distance tolerance treats the same action-token deviation as equally safe across states, although deviations that are harmless in free space can cause collisions or grasp failures near contact. We propose WA-SpecDec, a world-aware speculative decoding framework that injects world-model-derived physical scene awareness during the VLA prefill stage, producing shared world-aware prefill states for draft proposal and target verification without changing the relaxed acceptance rule. Across three state-of-the-art relaxed acceptance schemes, WA-SpecDec preserves higher task success under looser relaxation and enables longer accepted prefixes. At comparable-success operating points, WA-SpecDec achieves a 1.5x matched-success speedup over VLA speculative decoding alone and reduces near-contact failure (NCF) by 18.6% on average relative to the corresponding speculative baselines.

</details>


### [180] [OnEvoMemory: Evolving Memory through Online Robot Rollouts for Pretrained Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08749)
*Zhongxi Chen,Shenqi Zong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了OnEvoMemory，一种面向预训练机器人策略的价值引导记忆模块，用于长时程操作任务中跟踪子任务完成状态和关键交互事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要策略跟踪已完成的子任务和关键交互事件，但现有记忆机制高度依赖外部模型或预定义更新规则，缺乏自适应性。

Method: OnEvoMemory维护近期上下文、高价值经验和显著状态转换，通过从轨迹结果中学习应保留哪些经验。离线演示初始化记忆先验，在线成功/失败回滚细化记忆选择，帮助策略识别任务阶段转换并避免重复已完成子任务。

Result: 在长时程操作基准上的实验表明，OnEvoMemory通过离线初始化和在线记忆演变两方面均提升了基础VLA策略的性能。

Conclusion: OnEvoMemory作为一种价值引导的记忆模块，能够有效增强预训练机器人策略在长时程操作中的表现，不依赖外部模型或预定义规则。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation requires policies to track completed subtasks and critical interaction events. However, existing memory mechanisms heavily rely on external models or predefined update rules. To address this, we propose OnEvoMemory, a value-guided memory module for pretrained robot policies. It maintains recent context, high-value experiences, and salient transitions, while learning which experiences should be retained from trajectory outcomes. Offline demonstrations initialize the memory prior, whereas successful and unsuccessful online rollouts refine memory selection, helping the policy recognize task-stage transitions and avoid repeating completed subtasks. Experiments on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks show that OnEvoMemory improves the performance of the base VLA policy through both offline initialization and online memory evolution.

</details>


### [181] [PEEL: Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly Planning via Scale-Invariant Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08773)
*Servet B. Bayraktar,Andreas Orthey,Zachary Kingston,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PEEL算法，通过尺度不变的采样方案和多臂老虎机RRT（MAB-RRT）规划器并行求解长时程多部件物体拆卸问题，在76个组装体上实现100%的单部件拆卸成功率，并成功在Fetch机械臂上完成4个包含10-17个部件的长时程拆卸任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程多部件物体拆卸需要机器人在存在狭窄、紧凑的逃生通道的情况下，计算无碰撞移除运动的可行序列，这是一个具有挑战性的问题。

Method: PEEL算法包含两个核心组件：一是尺度不变采样方案，通过预热阶段估计物体尺度，随后利用方向的采样器利用该尺度；二是将该采样方案集成到多臂老虎机RRT（MAB-RRT）规划器中，根据奖励信号在不同采样器之间切换。PEEL并行运行一批MAB-RRT规划器，获得一个有序图，指定部件移除的序列。

Result: MAB-RRT在76个组装体上实现100%的单部件拆卸成功率，并且对参数具有鲁棒性。PEEL在Fetch机械臂上成功解决了4个长时程拆卸问题，涉及10到17个独立部件。

Conclusion: PEEL算法通过并行批量规划和自适应采样策略，有效解决了长时程多部件拆卸的可行性序列计算问题，并在真实机器人平台上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon multi-part object disassembly requires robots to compute feasible sequences of collision-free removal motions, even in the presence of tight, narrow escape corridors. To efficiently solve such disassembly problems, we propose Parallel Extraction for Long-Horizon Disassembly (PEEL), an algorithm which efficiently computes disassembly motions for object assemblies and feeds them to a robot manipulator for execution. PEEL uses sampling-based motion planning to compute single-object motions through the use of a scale-invariant sampling scheme, where the object scale is estimated in a burn-in phase and a subsequent directional sampler exploits the scale. This sampling scheme is integrated into a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner, which switches between different samplers depending on the reward signal received. Using MAB-RRT, the PEEL algorithm runs a batch of planners in parallel to obtain an ordered graph specifying the sequence in which object parts have to be removed. We show that MAB-RRT can efficiently solve single-part disassemblies with 100 percent success rate on 76 assemblies, and that it is robust to its parameters. By integrating MAB-RRT into PEEL, we solve four long-horizon disassembly problems using the Fetch manipulator robot involving 10 to 17 individual object parts.

</details>


### [182] [SG-WAM: Text-Grounded and Spatial-aware Semantic Guidance for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08839)
*Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Zhide Zhong,Haodong Yan,Ruixin Li,Yangyang Zheng,Jiaguan Zhu,Tianran Zhang,Yuqiao Du,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SG-WAM，一种通过视觉语言模型（VLM）作为语义规划器来增强世界动作模型（WAM）指令跟随能力的语义引导方法。VLM规划器预测文本锚定和空间感知的语义预见，并将其注入世界动作模型作为高层语义引导，确保未来视频生成和动作预测都严格遵循语言指令。实验在仿真和真实世界均展示了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型主要依赖视觉线索而非语言指令来生成未来视频和动作，因为现成的文本编码器将指令与视觉观察独立嵌入，导致预测的视频与语言指令语义不对齐，进而降低动作预测的准确性。

Method: 训练一个基于VLM的语义规划器，预测两种语义预见：(1)文本锚定语义预见——识别正确的目标物体以锚定指令；(2)空间感知语义预见——提供场景几何用于精确操作。然后将这些预见作为高层语义引导注入世界动作模型。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的广泛实验表明，SG-WAM在精确操作和指令跟随能力上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 利用VLM作为语义规划器注入语义引导，能有效增强世界动作模型的指令接地能力，实现更精确的机器人操作。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, most existing WAMs generate future videos and actions by relying mainly on visual cues rather than language instructions, since off-the-shelf text encoders embed instructions independently of visual observations. As a result, the videos predicted by these WAMs are often semantically misaligned with their corresponding language instructions, which degrades the accuracy of the predicted actions. To overcome this limitation, we propose SG-WAM, a semantic guidance method for world-action models that leverages a vision-language model (VLM) as a semantic planner to enhance the instruction-grounding capacity of world-action models. Specifically, we train a VLM-based planner to predict text-grounded and spatial-aware semantic foresight. The text-grounded semantic foresight grounds the instruction by identifying the correct target objects, and the spatial-aware semantic foresight provides the scene geometry for precise manipulation. We then inject this foresight into the world-action model as high-level semantic guidance, ensuring that both future-video generation and action prediction faithfully follow the language instruction. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate the superiority of our semantic guidance method, showcasing precise manipulation and strong instruction-following capabilities.

</details>


### [183] [Hierarchical Topology-Aware Planning and Control of Underwater Vehicle-Manipulator Systems in Confined Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08871)
*Mohamed Abdelwahab,Ruggero Carli,Damiano Varagnolo,Alberto Dalla Libera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MANTA，一个三层分层规划与控制框架，用于在水下车辆-机械臂系统（UVMS）在受限、杂乱且部分已知环境中进行自主干预任务，通过全局连通性推理、联合轨迹优化和基于GP的模型强化学习策略，实现安全可靠的干预操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在狭窄、杂乱且部分已知的水下环境中，UVMS面临机动性差、通道窄和不确定执行等问题，可能导致机器人进入不可恢复区域。现有方法缺乏同时考虑通道可达性、操作可行性和闭环执行的结构化方案。

Method: 提出MANTA三层框架：第一层在保守降维基座空间中做全局连通性推理，提取可达通道候选；第二层联合优化连续基座运动与机械臂轨迹，生成无碰撞的基座-臂组合轨迹；第三层使用基于高斯过程模型强化学习（MC-PILCO）学习"到达-保持"基座策略，实现轨迹跟踪和操作状态保持。执行过程中监控地图更新，可触发恢复和路径修复机制。

Result: 在120个匹配规划查询中，MANTA相比全状态采样基线实现了更高的任务成功率，同时产生更大的安全余量和更低的机械臂运动量。学习的MC-PILCO策略在训练和未见过的管状参考轨迹上均降低了位置和偏航跟踪误差。

Conclusion: MANTA作为结构化且数据高效的框架，为在洞穴、管道和杂乱海底结构中实现安全的水下自主干预提供了有效方案，验证了分层规划与控制耦合的可行性。

Abstract: This paper addresses autonomous intervention with an underwater vehicle--manipulator system (UVMS) in confined, cluttered, and partially known environments, where poor maneuverability, narrow passages, and uncertain execution may cause the robot to enter unrecoverable regions. We propose MANTA, a three-layer hierarchical planning-and-control framework that couples passage accessibility, manipulation feasibility, and closed-loop execution. The first layer performs global connectivity reasoning in a conservative reduced base space to extract traversable corridor candidates toward the task region. The second layer refines each candidate corridor by jointly optimizing the continuous base motion and arm trajectory, producing a collision-free base--arm trajectory. The third layer learns a reach-and-hold base policy using Gaussian-process model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) through MC-PILCO, enabling trajectory tracking and station keeping at the planned manipulation state. During execution, the framework monitors map updates and can trigger recovery and route repair when the active passage becomes infeasible. MANTA is evaluated in confined UVMS planning and closed-loop tracking experiments. Across 120 matched planning queries, it achieves higher task success than full-state sampling-based baselines while producing larger clearance margins and lower arm motion. The learned MC-PILCO policy further reduces position and yaw tracking errors on both training and unseen tube-like references. These results show MANTA as a structured and data-efficient framework for safe autonomous underwater intervention in caves, tubes, and cluttered subsea structures.

</details>


### [184] [SHRIMP: Iterative Refinement of Robot Task Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08884)
*Mya Schroder,Yuna Hwang,Callie Y. Kim,Leqian Cheng,Jeffrey Li-cheng Liu,Chenchen Zheng,Xinning He,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SHRIMP是一个利用自然语言自动生成层次化机器人原始动作计划，并通过重新提示和显式修正进行迭代修订的系统，支持用户在模拟环境中验证计划后再在实体机器人上执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作机器人已进入制造、农业和医疗等领域，但编程通常需要机器人专业知识，而大多数终端用户缺乏这些技能。虽然大型语言模型（LLM）使得自然语言转机器人任务计划成为可能，但语言规格存在语义歧义，且生成模型缺乏透明度，用户难以在执行前验证计划。

Method: SHRIMP系统允许用户使用自然语言自动生成层次化机器人原始动作计划，并通过重新提示和显式修正进行迭代修订。每次修订后，用户可在模拟环境中验证计划，满意后再在物理机器人上执行。研究采用用户研究方式，涉及35名参与者在桌面厨房任务中进行规划。

Result: 通过涉及35名参与者的用户研究（桌面厨房任务），验证了SHRIMP能够提升用户感知控制感并增强机器人透明度。

Conclusion: SHRIMP通过迭代式的计划修订与模拟验证机制，有效解决了自然语言任务规划的语义歧义和透明度不足问题，提升了用户对机器人系统的控制感和信任度。

Abstract: As collaborative robots have entered domains such as manufacturing, agriculture, and healthcare, programming or adapting robot behavior typically requires robotic expertise that most end users lack. Natural language lowers this barrier. Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have made it feasible to translate natural language into robot task plans. However, language-based task specification suffers from semantic ambiguity, and generative models lack transparency for how language instructions become robot actions, making it difficult for users to validate the plan before execution. To address these issues, we introduce SHRIMP, a system that allows users to automatically generate a hierarchical robot primitive plan using natural language and iteratively revise their plan through re-prompting and explicit correction. At each revision, SHRIMP allows users to validate their plan in simulation, and once satisfied, execute it on the physical robot. Through a user study involving participants planning tabletop kitchen tasks (n=35), we validate that SHRIMP improves perceived control and enhances robot transparency. System videos and source code are available at https://wisc-hci.github.io/SHRIMP.

</details>


### [185] [Estimation of Spacecraft Inertia Tensor Using Attitude-Only Data from Torque-Free Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08886)
*Daigo Kobayashi,Vakhtang Putkaradze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于姿态观测的航天器归一化惯性张量估计框架，无需陀螺仪或已知控制力矩，可处理单弧段和多短弧段的联合估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的惯性张量估计通常依赖陀螺仪测量或已知控制力矩，这在许多实际场景（如非合作目标或传感器受限）中不可用，因此需要探索仅依赖姿态观测的估计方法。

Method: 采用Karush-Kuhn-Tucker公式提供快速线性初始化，再通过使用Euler方程精确Jacobi椭圆解和Magnus展开四元数映射的非线性打靶法进行精化。支持单一连续弧段和多个短力矩自由弧段的联合使用。

Result: 单弧段（500秒）实验中，惯性张量误差较相同初始化下的扩展卡尔曼滤波器降低约一个数量级，计算量减少近两个数量级。三弧段（各100秒）联合估计获得类似精度提升且速度提升超过一个数量级。真实感近接操作仿真中，2000秒单弧段实现亚千分之一的中位惯性张量误差，并支持10小时姿态预测（中位误差为个位数度）。

Conclusion: 提出的姿态-only框架在精度和计算效率上均显著优于扩展卡尔曼滤波器基线，性能受旋转激励和时间采样密度影响，适用于无陀螺仪场景下的航天器惯性参数估计。

Abstract: We present an attitude-only framework for estimating a spacecraft's normalized inertia tensor from torque-free rotational motion. Our method supports both continuous single-arc observations and the joint use of multiple short torque-free arcs, while requiring neither gyroscope measurements nor known control torques. A Karush-Kuhn-Tucker formulation provides a fast linear initialization, which is refined by nonlinear shooting using the exact Jacobi-elliptic solution of Euler's equations and a Magnus-expansion quaternion map. Under controlled attitude noise, tests using a single 500-second arc reduced inertia-tensor error by approximately one order of magnitude relative to an Extended Kalman Filter initialized from the same estimate, while requiring nearly two orders of magnitude less computation. Joint estimation from three 100-second arcs provided a similar improvement in accuracy and remained more than one order of magnitude faster. Photorealistic proximity-operations simulations further evaluated both strategies using monocular image-derived attitudes. The 2000-second single-arc cases achieved sub-thousandth median inertia-tensor error and supported 10-hour attitude predictions with single-digit-degree median error. In three-arc cases using 30-300 seconds per arc, our method consistently outperformed the EKF refinement, with performance governed by rotational excitation and temporal sampling.

</details>


### [186] [From Operational Design Domain to Action: A Systematic Behavioral Taxonomy for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.08941)
*Chaitanya Shinde,Hadi Hajieghrary,Miguel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于标准的自动驾驶系统行为能力分类体系，包含21项行为能力，分布在高速公路（HWY）、城市（URB）和枢纽（HUB）三个运营域中，并基于PEGASUS六层模型ODD系统推导而来。该分类体系从纵向和横向控制轴分解行为，并以安全性、合规性、舒适性和效率四个属性框架进行表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统的运营设计域（ODD）规格仅规定了系统允许运行的环境条件，但未规定系统在该域内必须展示的具体行为，这一"运营条件规格"与"行为验证"之间的鸿沟是ADS安全保证中尚未解决的关键挑战。

Method: 从PEGASUS六层模型ODD系统推导出21项行为能力的结构化分类体系，覆盖三个运营域；每项行为沿纵向和横向控制轴分解，并以四属性框架（安全性、合规性、舒适性、效率）进行表征；同时展示了ODD层参数化与行为能力规格的交叉可产生适合系统行为测试和SOTIF覆盖证据的具体场景族。

Result: 该分类体系基于AVSC00008202111、SAE J3237和SAE J3016等标准建立，并通过部署在规则强制的轨迹优化系统中验证其作为运营规格层的有效性。研究还发现枢纽（Hub）域是一个结构上独特且规格不足的领域，值得专门研究关注。

Conclusion: 本文填补了ODD规格与行为验证之间的空白，提供了一个标准化的行为能力分类体系，可支持系统化的行为测试和SOTIF覆盖证据生成，并指出HUB域是需要进一步研究的弱点领域。

Abstract: Operational Design Domain (ODD) specifications describe where an automated driving system (ADS) is permitted to operate, but they do not prescribe what the ADS must demonstrably do once deployed within that domain. This gap between operating condition specification and behavioral validation represents a critical unresolved challenge in ADS safety assurance. This paper presents a structured, standards-grounded taxonomy of 21 behavioral competencies organized across three operational domains-Highway (HWY), Urban (URB), and Hub (HUB)-derived systematically from the PEGASUS six-layer model-based ODD. Each behavior is decomposed along longitudinal and lateral control axes and characterized against a four-property framework: Safety (gap maintenance, conflict avoidance, kinematic stability), Compliance (legal rules and behavioral norms), Comfort (rider dynamics and trust), and Efficiency (mission completion and product-level metrics). We further demonstrate that the crossing of ODD layer parameterizations with behavioral competency specifications yields concrete scenario families suitable for systematic behavioral testing and SOTIF coverage evidence. The taxonomy is grounded in AVSC00008202111, SAE J3237, and SAE J3016, and is validated as an operational specification layer through its deployment in a rule-enforced trajectory optimization system. The Hub domain is identified as a structurally distinct, underspecified domain warranting dedicated research attention.

</details>


### [187] [Personalized Lower-limb Exoskeleton Assistance via Preference-based Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09015)
*Xiao-Yin Liu,Guotao Li,Weiqun Wang,Zeng-Guang Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于偏好的贝叶斯优化（PbBO）框架，用于个性化外骨骼辅助的参数优化，通过利用候选集采样分布知识提高样本效率，仅需20次迭代即可以90.7%的验证精度收敛到用户偏好参数，并在跑步机和户外实验中相比无辅助行走降低了14.5%-15.4%的代谢率、6.3%-7.6%的心率和6.7%-31.5%的肌肉激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户偏好优化方法过度依赖大量人机在线交互，优化速度慢，导致用户疲劳且优化效果不佳，因此需要探索一种能最小化交互次数的高效个性化外骨骼辅助参数优化框架。

Method: 提出基于偏好的贝叶斯优化（PbBO）方法，利用候选集采样分布的知识来提高样本效率；同时设计分层控制器，针对不同任务生成个性化力矩并实时实现交互力矩跟踪。

Result: PbBO在优化六个控制参数时，通过20次迭代达到90.7%的验证精度；跑步机和户外实验显示，优化参数相比无辅助行走可降低代谢率14.5%-15.4%、心率6.3%-7.6%、肌肉激活6.7%-31.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的PbBO框架能够以更少的交互次数高效地学习个性化外骨骼辅助参数，且优化后的参数在多种生理指标上均表现出显著的改善效果，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: A significant challenge in exoskeleton robotics is the need to dynamically adapt control profiles to individual motion preferences, thereby ensuring both efficient and comfortable assistance. Currently, since user experience can serve as a comprehensive metric for evaluating the effectiveness of assistance, user preference-based optimization methods have been widely studied for parameter tuning. However, the existing methods rely heavily on extensive human-robot online interactions and suffer from slow optimization speed, which not only induces user fatigue but also compromises optimization effectiveness. Therefore, this paper aims to explore an efficient preference-based optimization framework for personalized exoskeleton assistance that can learn optimal parameters with minimal interaction. We propose a preference-based Bayesian optimization (PbBO) approach that can improve sample efficiency by leveraging knowledge about the sampling distribution of candidate sets. For optimizing six control parameters, PbBO can converge to user-preferred parameters with 90.7% validation accuracy via 20 iterations. Moreover, the hierarchical controller is designed to generate personalized torque for different tasks and achieve interaction torque tracking in real time. The results of treadmill and outdoor experiments demonstrate that the optimized parameters can reduce metabolic rate by 14.5%-15.4%, heart rate by 6.3%-7.6%, and muscle activation by 6.7%-31.5% compared to unassisted walking.

</details>


### [188] [Latent World Models with Monotone Planning Costs for Image-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09073)
*Amirhosein Chahe,Siwei Cai,Lifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜在世界模型的图像目标导航方法，通过自回归展开损失和单调成本排名（MCR）损失训练模型，在GNM数据集上达到最先进性能，并在真实机器人上实现零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像目标导航中的潜在世界模型不仅需要准确的未来预测，还需要可靠的规划成本来对候选动作序列进行排序。研究发现余弦距离成本排序不佳会误导CEM等采样型规划器，因此需要改进成本建模。

Method: 基于冻结的DINO系列编码器构建潜在世界模型，采用两个互补的训练目标：（1）自回归展开损失，减少训练与多步规划展开之间的差距；（2）单调成本排名（MCR）损失，直接鼓励受到更大扰动的动作序列获得更高的规划成本。同时研究了基于InfoNCE的动作对比训练，发现时间置换负样本会扭曲潜在几何结构。

Result: 在GNM导航数据集上，该方法优于NWM、DINO-WM、OmniVLA和NoMaD，达到最先进的图像目标导航性能，相比相同编码器的DINO-WM基线将方向误差降低了2.7倍。在真实机器人上零样本部署后，能在未见过的室内外环境中沿目标导向路径行驶。

Conclusion: 通过直接优化规划成本的排序能力并解决训练与推理展开之间的差距，可以显著提升基于潜在世界模型的图像目标导航性能，且该方法具备良好的真实世界泛化能力。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation with latent world models requires not only accurate future prediction, but also a planning cost that reliably ranks candidate action sequences. We define the cost as the cosine distance between the predicted future embedding and the goal embedding, and show that poor cost ordering can mislead sampling-based planners such as Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). To address this, we propose a latent world model built on a frozen DINO-family encoder and train it with two complementary objectives. An autoregressive rollout loss reduces the gap between training and multi-step planning rollouts, while a Monotone Cost Ranking (MCR) loss directly encourages increasingly perturbed action sequences to receive higher planning costs. We also study InfoNCE-based action-contrastive training and find that temporal permutation negatives distort the latent geometry and degrade planning performance. On the GNM navigation dataset, our method outperforms Navigation World Models (NWM), DINO-WM, OmniVLA, and NoMaD, achieving state-of-the-art image-goal navigation performance while reducing orientation error by $2.7\times$ over the same-encoder DINO WM baseline. We also deploy the model zero-shot on a physical robot, where it follows goal-directed paths in unseen indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [189] [UnsDrive: Towards Robust End-to-End Autonomous Driving in Unstructured Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09098)
*Nanxin Zeng,Ruiqi Song,Xiangyu Guo,Baiyong Ding,Yunfeng Ai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UnsDrive，一种面向非结构化矿区场景的端到端自动驾驶规划器，通过构建未知感知占据表示并采用流匹配规划器生成多模态轨迹，配合占据轨迹一致性损失和不确定性感知轨迹评分器来提升部分可观测条件下的安全性。同时提出MineLoop闭环模拟器，实验证明该方法在轨迹精度、碰撞避免和长时程鲁棒性方面优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端规划方法主要针对结构化城市道路设计，在非结构化矿区环境中泛化能力差。矿区存在道路结构弱、地形遮挡、能见度差和大范围未观测区域等问题，使安全规划极具挑战性。

Method: UnsDrive包含三个核心组件：(1)未知感知占据表示，利用多帧可见性线索显式建模占据、自由和未知空间；(2)流匹配规划器，基于该表示生成多模态未来轨迹；(3)占据轨迹一致性损失和不确定性感知轨迹评分器，用于惩罚进入不可通行或未观测区域的轨迹。此外提出MineLoop闭环模拟器用于矿区场景评估。

Result: 在开环和闭环实验中，UnsDrive在轨迹精度、碰撞避免和长时程驾驶鲁棒性方面均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 显式推理未知空间对于非结构化矿区环境的自动驾驶规划具有重要价值，验证了未知感知建模方法的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end planning has shown strong promise for autonomous driving, but most existing methods are designed for structured urban roads and generalize poorly to unstructured mining environments. In such settings, weak road structure, terrain-induced occlusions, degraded visibility, and large unobserved regions make safe planning particularly challenging. To address these challenges, we propose UnsDrive, an end-to-end planner designed for unstructured mining scenes. UnsDrive builds an unknown-aware occupancy representation that explicitly models occupied, free, and unknown space using multi-frame visibility cues, and conditions a flow-matching planner on this representation to generate multimodal future trajectories. To improve safety under partial observability, we further introduce an occupancy trajectory consistency loss and an uncertainty-aware trajectory scorer that penalize trajectories entering non-traversable or unobserved regions. We also present MineLoop, a mining-oriented closed-loop simulator for evaluating autonomous driving under irregular road geometry, degraded visibility, heavy-vehicle interactions, and mining-specific operational constraints. Experiments in both open-loop and closed-loop settings show that UnsDrive consistently outperforms strong baselines in trajectory accuracy, collision avoidance, and long-horizon driving robustness. These results demonstrate the value of explicit unknown-space reasoning for autonomous driving in unstructured mining environments.

</details>


### [190] [ROEVO: Robust Organized Edge Feature-based Visual Odometry Using RGB-D Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09112)
*Mingrui Liu,Xingxing Zuo,Renlang Huang,Minglei Zhao,Jiming Chen,Liang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于组织化边缘特征的视觉里程计（VO）系统，将离散边缘像素转化为序列化簇，支持边缘级跨帧关联、边缘级残差追踪以及形状保持的边缘联合优化，在室内环境中达到或超越现有最优方法的精度与鲁棒性，源码已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘是环境中普遍存在的空间表达线索，蕴含丰富的纹理与结构信息，但现有基于边缘的VO方法未能充分利用其潜力。

Method: 提出"组织化边缘"特征表示，将离散边缘像素序列化为簇，实现边缘级跨帧关联并构建共视性图；在追踪中采用边缘级而非像素级残差进行帧间配准；在联合优化中引入形状保持的边缘拟合方法和基于组织化边缘的束调整（BA），将传统BA分解为拟合与配准两个步骤以保持结构完整性。

Result: 在室内环境中进行了大量实验，其精度和鲁棒性优于或达到与最先进方法相当的水平。

Conclusion: 该VO系统仅使用组织化边缘特征即可实现高效的追踪和精确的局部建图，验证了边缘特征在视觉里程计中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a visual odometry (VO) system that leverages image edge features. Edges are spatially expressive cues commonly present across diverse environments, offering rich textural and structural information. However, existing edge-based VO methods often fail to fully exploit this potential. To this end, we introduce a novel feature representation termed \textit{organized edges}, which transforms disjoint edge pixels into sequentialized clusters, enabling more effective retention and utilization of the underlying textural and structural information. Another nice property of this formulation is that organized edges can perform edge-level association across multiple frames, enabling the establishment of a co-visibility graph. To achieve precise and efficient pose estimation, we propose a range of particularly designed tracking and joint optimization methods based on the characteristics of organized edges. For tracking, we formulate edge-wise rather than pixel-wise residuals to achieve robust and accurate inter-frame registration. For joint optimization, we introduce a novel shape-preserving edge-fitting method and an organized edge-based Bundle Adjustment (BA) approach, which decomposes the traditional BA problem into fitting and registration to preserve the structural integrity. Based on these novel techniques, we develop a complete VO system that exclusively employs organized edge features, achieving efficient tracking and precise local mapping. Extensive experiments demonstrate its accuracy and robustness in indoor environments, outperforming or achieving comparable performance to state-of-the-art methods. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/liumingrui814/ROEVO

</details>


### [191] [Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision for Reliable Dual-Arm Surgical Subtask Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09125)
*Mingwu Su,Guankun Wang,Jinsong Lin,Rulin Zhou,Ziyi Hao,Zhiwei Fang,Huxin Gao,Jiewen Lai,Jiazheng Wang,Fan Zhang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TDHD（轨迹发散视界决策）机制，通过测量流匹配生成轨迹在噪声扰动下的发散度来估计动作可靠性，并采用双阈值规则截断执行以触发实时重规划，从而解决VLA模型在手术机器人操作中因固定长度开环动作序列导致的误差累积问题。在达芬奇式双臂真实硬件平台上（600次遥操作演示，20次/任务设置），TDHD将缝合针操作成功率从55%提升至60%，组织操作从55%提升至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人临床负荷日益增加，需要自主化解决方案。现有VLA模型虽然提供统一的视觉-语言-动作框架，但依赖固定长度开环动作序列，在场景条件变化时会产生累积误差和潜在安全风险，限制了其在手术操作中的可靠部署。

Method: 将手术VLA部署建模为自适应执行视界决策问题，提出TDHD机制：通过测量两个流匹配生成轨迹在小噪声扰动下的发散度来估计逐步动作可靠性，并使用双阈值规则截断执行以触发及时重规划。同时构建了具有同步多视角感知和语言指令的真实达芬奇式双臂基准，收集了涵盖针和组织操作套件的600次遥操作演示数据。

Result: 在真实硬件上每个任务设置20次试验中，TDHD较最新VLA基线一致提升性能：针操作成功率从55%提升至60%，组织操作从55%提升至80%，且最大增益出现在最终操作阶段。

Conclusion: 自适应执行控制对于VLA模型在手术机器人操作中的可靠部署至关重要，TDHD机制有效解决了开环执行中的误差累积问题，提升了手术操作的鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Surgical robotic systems are increasingly being adopted as clinical workload rises, motivating autonomous solutions for repetitive manipulation subtasks. Learning-based controllers improve generalization compared with rule-based and analytic approaches, but most are trained for individual tasks and remain difficult to reuse across procedures. Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide a unified framework that integrates visual perception, language grounding, and action generation, offering a promising path toward more composable surgical autonomy. However, existing VLA policies rely on fixed-length open-loop action sequences, where changing scene conditions can lead to accumulated errors and potential risks in surgical manipulation. To mitigate this issue, we formulate surgical VLA deployment as an adaptive execution-horizon decision problem and propose Trajectory Divergence Horizon Decision (TDHD), a test-time mechanism that estimates step-wise action reliability by measuring the divergence between two flow-matching-generated trajectories under small noise perturbations and truncates execution using a dual-threshold rule to trigger timely replanning. We further establish a real-world da Vinci-like dual-arm benchmark with synchronized multi-view perception and language instructions, and collect 600 teleoperated demonstrations across needle (reach, pick, regrasp) and tissue (reach, lift, resection) manipulation suites. On real hardware with 20 trials per task setting, TDHD consistently improves performance over the latest VLA baselines: success increases from 55\% to 60\% for needle manipulation and from 55\% to 80\% for tissue manipulation, with the largest gains observed in the final manipulation stages. These results highlight the importance of adaptive execution control for reliable deployment of VLA models in surgical robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [192] [High Fidelity Capture, Reconstruction, and Transfer of Human Demonstrations for Robot-Assisted Bathing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09127)
*Arjun S. Lakshmipathy,Jonathan P. King,Ethan Zuo,Rohit Satishkumar,Hongyi Chen,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Dan Ding,Zackory Erickson,Nancy S. Pollard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以接触区域为关键处理原语的框架，用于采集、理解并迁移高动态、接触密集的人类沐浴演示数据，并利用该数据集驱动软手机器人完成沐浴任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床级机器人（如沐浴任务）需要与人进行复杂、持续的物理交互，但现有的采集、理解和迁移人类动态接触性演示数据的方法非常困难，阻碍了安全可靠的pHRI系统开发。

Method: 以接触区域（contact regions）作为核心处理原语构建框架，采集临床医护人员对人体进行沐浴演示的数据集（包含同步的运动、形状、接触和力信息），并利用该数据集通过开环和闭环策略设计并控制安装在机械臂上的灵巧软手在人体模型上执行沐浴任务。

Result: 构建了首个提供持续接触密集人-人交互过程中高质量同步运动、形状、接触和力数据的数据集，并在机器人堆栈的多个层面验证了数据迁移策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架及公开数据集将促进物理人机交互（pHRI）领域的进一步研究与发展。

Abstract: Despite the demand for robots in high-value clinical tasks like bathing, contemporary systems still lack the safety and reliability required for complex, sustained physical interaction with humans. A key challenge hindering the development of such systems is that collecting, understanding, and effectively transferring highly dynamic, contact-rich human bathing demonstrations is difficult, even with modern motion and tactile sensing equipment. We present a straightforward, but effective framework for doing so with high fidelity by utilizing contact regions as a key processing primitive. We use our framework to build a dataset of bathing demonstrations performed by trained clinicians on human subjects. We then use this dataset to design and control an arm-mounted dexterous soft hand to perform bathing tasks on a mannequin using open- and closed-loop strategies. Our dataset is the first to provide high quality synchronized motion, shape, contact, and force during sustained, contact-rich human-human interaction, and our transfer strategies demonstrate effective use of these data across multiple levels of the robotics stack. All relevant materials will be publicly released to enable further advancements in physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) research.

</details>


### [193] [SpeedTuning: Speeding Up Policy Execution with Lightweight Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09138)
*David D. Yuan,Tony Z. Zhao,Kaylee Burns,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeedTuning框架，通过强化学习预测最优执行速度来加速机器人操作策略，在无需额外数据收集的情况下实现超过2.4倍的加速，同时保持较高的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型机器人策略的实际部署常受执行速度过慢的制约。模仿学习策略受硬件约束和数据采集时操作者速度的限制，且缺乏成熟的策略加速方法和关于执行速度与任务成功率之间关系的实证研究。

Method: SpeedTuning是一个强化学习框架，学习预测动作的最优执行速度，作为基础策略的补充模块，无需额外收集数据即可实现策略加速。

Result: SpeedTuning实现了超过2.4倍的执行速度提升，与原始任务策略和固定速度线性插值等简单加速方法相比，保持了足够的任务成功率。在倒水、投掷、抓取等多样化的动态和精细任务中验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SpeedTuning为模仿学习策略的加速提供了有效解决方案，在显著提升执行速度的同时保证了任务成功率，填补了该领域策略加速方法的空白。

Abstract: While learned robotic policies hold promise for advancing generalizable manipulation, their practical deployment is often hindered by suboptimal execution speeds. Imitation learning policies are inherently limited by hardware constraints and the speed of the operator during data collection. In addition, there are no established methods for accelerating policies learned via imitation, and the empirical relationship between execution speed and task success remains underexplored. To address these issues, we introduce SpeedTuning, a reinforcement learning framework specifically designed to enhance the speed of manipulation policies. SpeedTuning learns to predict the optimal execution speed for actions, thereby complementing a base policy without necessitating additional data collection. We provide empirical evidence that SpeedTuning achieves substantial improvements in execution speed, exceeding 2.4x speed-up, while preserving an adequate success rate compared to both the original task policy and straightforward speed-up methods such as linear interpolation at a fixed speed. We evaluate our approach across a diverse set of dynamic and precise tasks, including pouring, throwing, and picking, demonstrating its effectiveness and robustness in enhancing real-world robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [194] [Particle-Based Conformal Prediction for Contact-Aware Uncertainty Calibration in Stratified Configuration Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09166)
*Luís Marques,Kristian Popov,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CaPTURe算法，一种基于共形预测的几何感知算法，用于任意保真度的粒子模型在机器人接触障碍物场景下生成概率有效的未来构型预测区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在与环境交互时需要可靠的不确定性表示，但现有的解析或学习运动模型往往因数据有限、简化假设等而不准确，且机器人接触障碍物时未来构型分布可能变为多模态、不连通或沿低维流形分布，传统方法难以处理。

Method: CaPTURe使用标定数据集对运动不确定性估计进行局部标定，构造以用户设定概率包含未来机器人构型的预测区域。标定过程能够区分接触丰富运动和无接触运动中不确定性的变化，确保两种情况都具有充分覆盖率。

Result: 在两个模拟规划任务（迷宫弹珠控制和机械臂紧公差销孔插入）中，CaPTURe在接触和非接触情况下均达到用户指定覆盖率要求，相比最优基线方法任务成功率绝对提升高达30%。

Conclusion: CaPTURe通过几何感知的共形预测标定方法，有效解决了接触-rich场景下不确定性表示的多模态和低维流形问题，显著提升了安全规划的可靠性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty representation is essential for deploying autonomous systems that interact with their environment, as robots must reason about how uncertainty arising from both stochasticity and model mismatch is impacted by contacts with obstacles (e.g., when navigating through a cluttered environment or inserting a part into an assembly). We propose Calibrated Particle-sets for Trans-dimensional Uncertainty Representation (CaPTURe), a geometry-aware, conformal prediction-based algorithm that generates probabilistically valid prediction regions of the unknown future system configuration using particle-based models of arbitrary fidelity. While calibrated uncertainty predictions are essential for safe and efficient planning, analytical or learned motion models are often inaccurate - due to limited data, simplifying assumptions, unmodeled effects, etc. - which can lead to unsafe executions or task failure. Additionally, when a robot contacts an obstacle, the distribution of its future configurations can become multimodal or disjoint, or lie along manifolds of lower intrinsic dimension than the space of possible robot configurations. Our method uses a calibration dataset of system transitions to locally calibrate motion uncertainty estimates, constructing regions guaranteed to contain the future robot configuration at a user-set probability. Our calibration procedure captures how motion uncertainty varies between contact-rich and contactless motions, leading to sufficient coverage in both cases. We evaluate our method on two simulated planning tasks: controlling a marble around a labyrinth and performing tight-tolerance peg-in-hole insertion with a manipulator. Compared to relevant baselines, CaPTURe achieves the user-specified coverage requirement both in and out of contact and achieves up to a 30% absolute improvement in task success rate over the best baseline.

</details>


### [195] [SAIN: Structure-Aware Interactive Navigation with Active Dialogue Grounding for Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09196)
*Yuhao Cao,Xiao Liu,Yang Xie,Lu Liu,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAIN是一个零样本框架，将主动对话转化为持久化导航状态，通过结构化记忆（价值、房间、图、物体）存储目标证据、走廊记忆和候选标签，在VL-LN IIGN基准上将成功率从20.2提升至25.4，SPL从13.07提升至14.17，且无需任务特定策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航任务假设指令完整无歧义，但真实机器人常遇到模糊或不完整的自然语言指令。现有的对话式方法将oracle答案作为即时文本上下文消费，而非持久化的空间或物体中心结构化状态，导致长期交互导航中信息利用效率低下。

Method: SAIN提出对话到状态的转换机制：将oracle答案编译为目标证据、路线级走廊记忆和物体候选标签，存储在结构化价值记忆、房间记忆、图记忆和物体记忆中，并通过统一策略进行前沿排序和最终目标接近。

Result: 在VL-LN IIGN基准上，SAIN将成功率从20.2提升到25.4，SPL从13.07提升到14.17，超越了最强的对话式基线方法，且无需任务特定的策略训练。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持"对话到状态转换"（dialogue-to-state conversion）作为长时程交互式实例导航的有效零样本机制，为处理模糊人类指令的具身智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Most existing vision-language navigation tasks assume that instructions are complete and unambiguous. However, real-world robots often encounter natural human instructions that are ambiguous, underspecified, or incomplete, requiring them to resolve such uncertainties through active questioning. Interactive Instance Goal Navigation (IIGN) requires an embodied agent to find the specific instance under an ambiguous category-level instruction through active dialogue. However, existing dialogue-enabled methods often consume oracle answers as transient textual context for immediate decisions, rather than persistent spatial or object-centric structured state. We present SAIN, a zero-shot framework that turns active dialogue into persistent navigation state. Instead of consuming oracle answers as one-step text hints, SAIN compiles them into target evidence, route-level corridor memory, and object-candidate labels. These states are stored in structured value, room, graph, and object memories, then consumed by a unified policy for frontier ranking and final target approach. On the VL-LN IIGN benchmark, SAIN improves SR from 20.2 to 25.4 and SPL from 13.07 to 14.17 over the strongest reported dialogue-enabled baseline, while requiring no task-specific policy training. The results support dialogue-to-state conversion as an effective zero-shot mechanism for long-horizon interactive instance navigation. Project website: https://zorattc.github.io/SAIN/

</details>


### [196] [Ultra-Low-Impedance Robotic Gripper for High-Bandwidth and Transparent Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09198)
*Joon Lee,Ari Choi,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型9自由度三指差分直驱（DDD）夹持器，结合直驱电机与低传动比（1:2）差分传动架构，在保持低惯性、低机械阻抗的同时实现约18N的标称抓取力和4.7N的指尖力，解决了传统机器人夹持器中高传动比与外部传感器带来的摩擦、惯性和复杂度问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人夹持器依赖高传动比来产生抓取扭矩，并借助外部力传感器测量物理交互。然而高传动比会增加摩擦、反射惯量和机械阻抗，外部传感器则增加硬件复杂性，这些权衡限制了夹持器在高带宽交互和力感知方面的性能。

Method: 提出9自由度三指差分直驱夹持器设计：采用直驱（DD）电机结合低传动比（1:2）差分传动机构。关键设计包括将致动器质量集中布置于基座以最小化运动连杆惯性，以及利用差分架构将两个电机并联耦合以在屈曲时放大扭矩。

Result: 实验结果表明：原型机标称抓取力约18N，指尖力4.7N；电机对系统惯量的贡献低至0.236%；在电机不通电时被动机械阻抗最大测量值为50.1 N/m，表现出优秀的物理透明性。

Conclusion: 所提出的硬件有效平衡了扭矩、物理透明性和运动灵巧性之间的权衡，为高带宽交互和无传感器本体感受力估计奠定了基础，解决了传统夹持器在高传动比与外部力传感器方案上的固有矛盾。

Abstract: Conventional robotic grippers often use high-ratio transmissions to generate grasping torque and external force sensors to measure physical interaction. High-ratio transmissions increase friction, reflected inertia, and mechanical impedance, while external sensors add hardware complexity. To address these trade-offs, this study proposes a novel 9-DOF, three-fingered Differential Direct-Drive (DDD) gripper that combines DD motors with a low-ratio (1:2) differential transmission. The mechanism centralizes actuator mass at the base to minimize moving-link inertia, while the differential architecture couples two motors in parallel to amplify torque during flexion. Experiments show that the prototype delivers a nominal grasping force of approximately 18 N and a fingertip force of 4.7 N, while maintaining a low motor contribution to system inertia (0.236%) and low passive mechanical impedance, with a maximum measured value of 50.1 N/m when the motors are unpowered. The proposed hardware addresses the trade-offs among torque, physical transparency, and kinematic dexterity, providing a foundation for high-bandwidth interaction and sensorless proprioceptive force estimation.

</details>


### [197] [Task-Oriented Formation Decision via Reinforcement Learning: Herding an Attacking Swarm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09258)
*Zhaozong Wang,Guibin Sun,Jinyong Chen,Rui Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究多机器人系统中面向任务的编队决策问题，以牧群任务为例。提出用低维参数向量编码编队形状，将编队决策转化为参数优化问题；并设计了基于强化学习的策略来调节编队参数，以应对攻击者的高机动性和未知策略。仿真和实体实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注多机器人形状编队本身，而较少考虑任务导向的编队决策。牧群任务中攻击者机动性更强且策略未知，传统预定义形状灵活性不足，难以满足任务需求。

Method: 1) 提出低维参数向量对编队形状进行参数化编码，将编队决策重构为参数优化问题，实现编队形状随任务需求连续自适应调整。2) 设计基于强化学习的策略来调节编队参数，在离线仿真中针对多样化攻击策略进行训练，用于在线部署时应对对抗性不确定性。

Result: 与三种基线方法对比的仿真实验显示，所提方法能够成功完成具有挑战性的牧群任务；扩展性仿真验证了方法可适用于数十个机器人的场景；在包含3个攻击者和7个防御者的实体机器人平台上验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 通过参数化编队表示和强化学习策略的结合，所提方法有效缓解了防御者机动性劣势，并能应对攻击者策略的不可预测性，为任务导向的多机器人编队决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems can accomplish tasks that are difficult for a single robot by organizing into task-specific formations. Different from existing studies on multi-robot shape formation, we here study the task-oriented formation decision problem, with a focus on the herding task. This task is challenging due to the attackers' superior maneuverability and their unknown strategies. To address these challenges, we propose the following novel results. First, we encode the formation shape using a low-dimensional parameter vector. This parametric representation reformulates the formation decision as a parameter optimization problem, thereby resolving the limited flexibility of predefined shapes. By optimizing these formation parameters, the defenders' maneuverability disadvantage is mitigated through a formation shape that continuously adapts to task requirements. Second, we develop a reinforcement learning-based policy to regulate the formation parameters. Trained offline in simulations covering diverse attacking strategies, the learned policy can effectively handle adversarial unpredictability during online deployment. Comparative simulations against three baselines demonstrate that our method can successfully accomplish challenging herding tasks. Additional scalability simulations further verify its applicability to simulated scenarios involving dozens of robots. We also validate the practical feasibility of our method on a physical robotic platform with 3 attackers and 7 defenders.

</details>


### [198] [WorldSimProbe: Diagnosing Simulator Faithfulness in Action-Conditioned World Models for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09298)
*Peterson Co,Sicheng Hu,Chunxuan Jiao,Hongyang Cheng,Yulin Luo,Yijie Xu,Sixiang Chen,Zhongxia Zhao,Zihao Wang,DaFeng Chi,Peidong Liu,YuTong Chen,Henghua Liu,Zhihao Yuan,Huizhu Jia,Yuzheng Zhuang,Tianle Zhang,Liang Lin,Huajie Tan,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了可观测模拟器契约（Observable Simulator Contract），并引入WorldSimProbe基准框架，从局部控制敏感性、全局轨迹变化、多源动作、交互锚定和动力学五个受控套件系统评估动作条件世界模型（ACWMs）的模拟器保真度，而非仅依赖视觉质量或任务结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ACWM评估侧重于视觉质量、任务结果或粗粒度的rollout响应性，无法直接检验模拟器保真度（即动作条件转换的精确性），导致其作为可扩展预测模拟器在规划、策略评估和数据生成中的应用难以确立。

Method: 形式化定义Observable Simulator Contract，要求动作必须引发相应的智能体运动、环境响应须基于已实现的运动。在此基础上构建WorldSimProbe，包含五个受控测试套件：局部控制敏感性、全局轨迹变化、源多样动作、交互锚定和动力学，并设计套件特定的评估指标（模拟器相对校准、密集动作-运动对应、虚假交互锚定、原语级动力学）。对六个开源ACWM在RoboTwin、ManiSkill和LIBERO上的18,000+实例进行评估。

Result: WorldSimProbe揭示了ACWM在控制变化下存在系统性的动作实现退化、交互锚定和动力学方面的结构化失败；基准信号与人类判断及下游结果一致。

Conclusion: 该基于能力的框架为诊断ACWM模拟器保真度提供了透明、标准化的范式，弥补了传统粗粒度任务导向评估的不足。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) promise to provide embodied AI with scalable predictive simulators for planning, policy evaluation, and data generation. Realizing this promise requires precise action-conditioned transitions rather than merely plausible outputs. Yet their applicability remains difficult to establish because prevailing evaluations emphasize visual quality, task outcomes, or coarse rollout-level responsiveness without directly testing simulator fidelity. To address this gap, we evaluate ACWMs through the observable capabilities expected of physical simulators. Accordingly, we formalize Observable Simulator Contract, a minimal contract that any action-conditioned physical simulator should satisfy: supplied actions must induce corresponding agent motion, and environment responses must be grounded in that realized motion. To operationalize this contract, we introduce WorldSimProbe, comprising five controlled suites spanning local control sensitivity, global trajectory variation, source-diverse actions, interaction grounding, and dynamics. Suite-specific evaluators assess simulator-relative calibration, dense action-to-motion correspondence, false-interaction grounding, and primitive-level dynamics. We evaluate six open-source ACWMs on more than 18,000 instances across RoboTwin, ManiSkill, and LIBERO. World-SimProbe reveals systematic action-realization degradation across control variation, structured failures in interaction grounding and dynamics, and benchmark signals consistent with human judgments and downstream outcomes. Together, this capability-based framework provides a transparent, and standardized paradigm for diagnosing ACWM simulator fidelity beyond coarse, task-directed evaluation.

</details>


### [199] [SAFE-CHEM: Uncertainty-Aware Policy Switching for Robust Robotic Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09303)
*Laura Jones,Shazil Shahzad,Ayesha Sana,Gabriella Pizzuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAFE-CHEM，一种面向化学实验室机器人的不确定性感知安全框架，通过集成循环神经网络模仿学习策略在线量化认知不确定性，并在不确定性超过阈值时自动切换到规则化后备控制器，从而提升任务成功率并减少安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据驱动的学习方法在灵巧操作任务中取得显著成功，但在化学实验室等高风险领域，学习型策略常无法区分安全与不安全的动作，导致过度自信的外推和潜在的灾难性失败，安全成为限制其部署的首要障碍。

Method: SAFE-CHEM框架通过集成多个基于循环神经网络的模仿学习策略，利用动作预测的方差在线量化认知不确定性；随后采用核密度估计刻画成功条件下的方差分布，建立混合控制架构，在不确定性超过校准安全阈值时自动从学习策略切换到确定性规则化后备控制器。

Result: 在三个基础实验室操作任务上的实验表明，该混合策略相比传统单策略基线显著提升了整体任务成功率，并减少了关键安全违规；此外，通过零样本仿真到实物迁移，在Franka Production 3机器人上验证了框架的实际可行性。

Conclusion: SAFE-CHEM证明了将不确定性感知机制与混合控制架构相结合，能够有效提升学习型化学实验室机器人在高风险操作中的安全性和可靠性，为自主化学实验系统的大规模部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The deployment of autonomous robotic systems in chemistry laboratories is accelerating experimental workflows and providing the foundational data for AI-driven scientific discovery. However, despite the success of data-driven methods in acquiring dexterous skills, safety remains a primary barrier to their deployment in high-risk domains, such as early-stage materials chemistry experiments. Specifically, learning-based policies frequently struggle to distinguish between safe and unsafe actions, leading to overconfident extrapolation and potentially catastrophic failures. To mitigate these safety risks, we propose SAFE-CHEM, an uncertainty-aware framework designed for robust, learning-based robotic chemists. Our approach leverages an ensemble of recurrent neural network-based imitation learning policies to quantify epistemic uncertainty online through the variance of action predictions. By characterising the success-conditioned density of this variance using kernel density estimation, we introduce a hybrid control architecture that autonomously switches from the learned policy to a deterministic, rule-based backup controller when uncertainty exceeds a calibrated safety threshold. We evaluate SAFE-CHEM across three fundamental laboratory manipulation tasks, where our empirical results demonstrate that this hybrid strategy improves overall task success rates and reduces critical safety violations compared to traditional single-policy baselines. Finally, we demonstrate the practical viability of the framework through zero-shot sim-to-real transfer onto a physical Franka Production 3 robot manipulator.

</details>


### [200] [DH-VLM: Dual-Horizon Cooperative Latent Reasoning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09333)
*Ziyi Song,Chen Xia,Hang Yu,Sheng Zhou,Zhisheng Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DH-VLM，一种双视界协同潜在推理框架，通过基础设施与自车之间的非对称语义协作实现自动驾驶规划。基础设施聚合多层隐藏状态形成全局推理视界的潜在引导，通过基础设施驱动的潜在演化机制在自车模型中进行条件性潜在细化，使自车兼顾全局上下文理解与本地规划。实验表明在L2误差和碰撞率上分别提升14.6%和26.9%，同时降低57.3%通信成本和25.5%GPU内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型在自动驾驶中虽有全局理解和长视界规划优势，但在孤立车辆上部署面临感知范围有限、遮挡、计算延迟等实际约束。现有协同驾驶方法在语义推理能力和实际约束下存在局限。

Method: 提出DH-VLM双视界协同潜在推理框架：(1)基础设施聚合多层隐藏状态形成全局推理视界的潜在引导；(2)通过基础设施驱动的潜在演化机制(Infrastructure-Driven Latent Evolution)实现条件性潜在细化；(3)构建面向协作的QA数据集，支持反事实和安全感知推理。

Result: 达到最先进的规划性能，L2误差较先前最优降低14.6%，碰撞率降低26.9%。相比基于查询的端到端协同驾驶方法，通信成本降低57.3%，GPU内存使用降低25.5%，并对基础设施引导误差保持强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DH-VLM为协同自动驾驶提供了一种实用且稳健的范式，在保持自车自主决策能力的同时充分利用基础设施的全局推理能力，有效解决了部署中的计算、通信和鲁棒性挑战。

Abstract: Large-scale language models for autonomous driving enable enhanced global understanding and long-horizon planning. However, when deployed in isolated vehicles, limited sensing range and occlusions restrict reliable decision-making, and the substantial computational and latency overhead makes on-board deployment impractical. Cooperative driving provides a potential solution by leveraging external agents for information exchange, but existing methods remain limited in semantic reasoning capability under practical constraints. To address these challenges, we propose DH-VLM, a dual-horizon cooperative latent reasoning framework that enables asymmetric semantic cooperation between the infrastructure and ego vehicle. The infrastructure aggregates multi-layer hidden states to form a global-reasoning horizon latent guidance, which is integrated into the ego model through an Infrastructure-Driven Latent Evolution mechanism for conditional latent refinement. This enables the ego vehicle to leverage long-range contextual understanding while preserving autonomous decision-making within its local planning horizon. Furthermore, we construct a cooperation-oriented question-answer (QA) dataset covering fundamental scene understanding and ego-personalized comprehension to support counterfactual and safety-aware reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DH-VLM achieves state-of-the-art planning performance, outperforming the previous state of the art by 14.6% in L2 error and 26.9% in collision rate. Compared with query-based end-to-end cooperative driving methods, our approach reduces the communication cost by 57.3% and GPU memory usage by 25.5%, while maintaining strong robustness against infrastructure guidance errors, providing a practical and robust paradigm for cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [201] [JEPA-WAM: Learning Vision-Language-Action Policies with Joint-Embedding World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09381)
*Yihan Lin,Jiawei He,Shifeng Bao,Chen Zhao,Yang Li,Xiaobo Wang,Yan Wang,Cheng Chi,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA-WAM，一种基于预训练V-JEPA空间的潜在世界动作模型，通过共享预测器将潜在状态转移预测与连续动作生成耦合，在LIBERO-Plus上达到79.2%准确率（无大规模策略预训练的最佳结果），其π0.5实例化版本达到86.3%的总体最佳性能，并在RoboTwin 2.0和真实双臂操作任务中展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成世界动作模型（WAMs）虽然能显式建模状态转移，但部署成本高昂；现有的潜在WAM虽然避免了显式未来生成，但往往压缩预测表示或将预测建模与动作生成所使用的表示分离，导致预测能力与动作控制之间存在鸿沟。

Method: JEPA-WAM构建在预训练的V-JEPA空间中，通过共享预测器将潜在状态转移预测与连续动作生成耦合。它预测一个空间结构化的联合当前-未来目标，捕获任务共享的视觉时间结构并保留密集的patch级对应关系。转移监督通过共享预测器直接塑造骨干网络，从中提取专用表示用于动作预测。该设计也可实例化到预训练VLA策略中，同时保留其原始感知和动作通路。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus基准上，JEPA-WAM达到79.2%的准确率，是无需大规模机器人策略预训练的最佳结果；其在预训练π0.5上的实例化达到86.3%，实现总体最佳性能。此外，在RoboTwin 2.0和真实世界双臂操作实验中展现出在视觉和空间偏移下的强大泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过在V-JEPA空间中耦合潜在转移预测与动作生成，JEPA-WAM有效弥合了预测建模与动作控制之间的鸿沟，在保持潜在WAM高效部署优势的同时取得了优异的控制性能和泛化能力，并可与预训练VLA策略无缝集成。

Abstract: Robust robot control benefits from explicitly modeling state transitions, but video-generation world action models (WAMs) introduce substantial deployment cost. Existing latent WAMs avoid explicit future generation, but often compress predictive representations or separate predictive modeling from the representations used for action generation. We introduce JEPA-WAM, a latent WAM built in a pretrained V-JEPA space, which couples latent transition prediction with continuous action generation through a shared predictor. JEPA-WAM predicts a spatially structured joint current-future target that captures task-shared visual temporal structure between current and future observations, while preserving dense patch-level correspondence. Through the shared predictor, transition supervision directly shapes the backbone, from which dedicated representations are extracted for action prediction. The same design can also be instantiated in pretrained VLA policies while preserving their original perception and action pathways. On LIBERO-Plus, JEPA-WAM achieves 79.2%, the best result without large-scale robot-policy pretraining, while its pretrained $π_{0.5}$ instantiation reaches 86.3%, achieving the best overall performance. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 and real-world bimanual manipulation further demonstrate strong generalization under visual and spatial shifts.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond the Plane: Coupling Planar Vehicle Dynamics with Three-Dimensional Road Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09402)
*Simon Sagmeister,Phillip Pitschi,Nico Haja,Markus Lienkamp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将平面车辆动力学模型与三维真实道路几何耦合的新方法，通过在三维空间中变换车辆状态并计算道路几何诱导的力与力矩，在不放弃简单平面模型的前提下消除了平面仿真与真实三维道路之间的差距，并在拉斯维加斯赛车场的高速数据和合成赛道上进行了验证，开源实现已发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统的开发与测试高度依赖车辆动力学仿真，但大多数车辆动力学模型是二维的，而真实道路是三维的。三维道路几何（如银行弯道）会显著影响轮胎法向力（可达66%以上），导致平面模型难以准确复现真实车辆行为。虽然存在三维动力学方案，但计算开销大、复杂度高且很少被采用，因此需要一种简单可行的方法来弥补这一差距。

Method: 提出一种将平面车辆动力学模型与三维道路几何耦合的方法：在三维空间中变换平面车辆状态的表示，同时计算由道路几何（坡度、倾角等）引起的附加力与力矩，并将其施加到平面车辆模型中，使平面模型能够考虑三维道路的真实影响。

Result: 在拉斯维加斯赛车场（高倾斜银行赛道）上使用全尺寸赛车的高速实测数据验证了方法的有效性；同时在合成赛道上的边缘情况下也展示了准确的仿真结果，证明了该方法在各类场景下均能取得一致的高精度。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了平面仿真与真实三维道路几何之间的差距，无需放弃简便的平面车辆模型即可获得准确的仿真结果。研究成果以开源软件形式发布，便于学术界和工业界直接采用。

Abstract: Simulation is crucial for developing and testing autonomous driving systems. In particular, the development of localization and control algorithms relies on an accurate vehicle dynamics simulation. However, most vehicle dynamics models are two-dimensional while real-world roads are three-dimensional. For example, effects from the three-dimensional road geometry on the Las Vegas Motor Speedway can increase the normal forces on the tires by more than 66% compared to the nominal load at standstill. As a result, even highly detailed planar vehicle dynamics models struggle to accurately reproduce the real vehicle's behavior. While solutions for three-dimensional vehicle dynamics exist, they are rarely adopted, computationally expensive, and complex. To address this issue, we present a novel method to couple planar vehicle dynamics models with real-world three-dimensional road geometry. We transform the planar vehicle state from the vehicle model's two-dimensional plane to its corresponding representation in three-dimensional space. Additionally, we calculate road-geometry-induced forces and moments and apply them to the planar vehicle model. We validate our approach using high-speed data recorded with a full-scale race car on the banked Las Vegas Motor Speedway. Furthermore, on synthetic tracks, we show that our method yields accurate results even in edge cases. Together, our results demonstrate that the gap between planar simulation and real-world three-dimensional roads can be closed without abandoning simpler planar models. To simplify adoption of our method, we provide the implementation as open-source software on github.com/TUMFTM/3d-road-geometry-coupling.

</details>


### [203] [Skills in Weights, Memory in Code: Hybrid Learning for Memory-Dependent Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09410)
*Yunhao Zhao,Zhenyang Ni,Haoyang Chen,Ruohan Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyMeS是一种混合学习框架，利用编码代理的推理和记忆管理能力来引导马尔可夫VLA策略处理记忆依赖的操控任务，在RoboMemArena上相比pi0.5和PrediMem显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的操控任务往往是非马尔可夫的，需要机器人从长时程交互历史中保留和推理任务相关信息。现有的VLA策略通常仅基于当前观测或短固定长度历史生成动作块，无法有效处理这类记忆依赖的操控场景。

Method: HyMeS采用混合学习框架：底层通过梯度模仿学习掌握可复用的低层运动技能；高层通过启发式学习，由编码代理迭代更新可执行的启发式系统来获取记忆管理策略。通过多模态阶段完成验证（结合本体感受信号和多帧VLM判断）闭环连接引导与执行，并更新记忆。

Result: 在RoboMemArena基准上，HyMeS相比pi0.5将平均累计成功率从52.5%提升至66.2%，平均任务成功率从41.3%提升至60.1%；相比PrediMem在累计成功率上提升4.5个百分点，任务成功率上提升14.5个百分点。

Conclusion: HyMeS通过将记忆管理策略与可复用运动技能分离，仅需为可复用的运动技能提供演示数据而非每种历史依赖任务配置，实现了数据高效的组合泛化，为长时程记忆依赖的机器人操控提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern vision-language-action (VLA) policies have acquired broad manipulation skills, but typically generate each action chunk from the current observation or a short fixed-length history. However, real-world manipulation is often non-Markovian, requiring robots to retain and reason over task-relevant information from long-horizon interaction histories to determine the next action. To address this challenge, we propose HyMeS, a hybrid learning framework that leverages the reasoning and memory-management capabilities of coding agents to steer a Markovian VLA for memory-dependent manipulation. Specifically, HyMeS learns low-level motor skills through gradient-based imitation learning, while a coding agent acquires high-level memory-management strategies through heuristic learning by iteratively updating an executable heuristic system from rollout feedback. Furthermore, we close the loop between steering and execution through multimodal stage-completion verification, which updates memory using proprioceptive signals and multi-frame VLM judgments. Compared with end-to-end memory-augmented VLAs, HyMeS requires demonstrations only for reusable motor skills rather than for every history-dependent task configuration, enabling data-efficient compositional generalization. On RoboMemArena, HyMeS improves mean cumulative success from 52.5% to 66.2% and mean task success from 41.3% to 60.1% over pi0.5, while outperforming PrediMem by 4.5 points in cumulative success and 14.5 points in task success.

</details>


### [204] [VANE: Reliable Test-Time Training for Vision-Language-Action Models via Future Visual Representation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09448)
*Hongjin Ji,Guoyang Xia,Luoyang Sun,Fangxiang Feng,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VANE是一种可靠的测试时训练（TTT）框架，用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略的闭环操作。它通过基于当前视觉-语言上下文的提示适应，并根据执行动作的未来视觉后果来学习，将候选更新与实时策略隔离，仅在后续观测证据支持下才提交更新，使适应具有选择性和可逆性。在SimplerEnv WidowX上比TTT基线提升3.2个百分点，在Google Robot上的结果表明部署时收益因任务和本体而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时训练方法在闭环操作中难以可靠使用：共享适应空间可能混合不兼容的任务修正，在线更新可能在后果未知前就改变后续动作，导致策略不稳定。

Method: VANE框架包含三个关键设计：一是将提示适应条件化在当前的视觉-语言上下文上；二是从执行动作的未来视觉后果中学习；三是将候选更新与实时策略隔离，在后续观测中评估，只有获得未来证据支持时才提交更新，从而实现选择性和可逆的适应。

Result: 在SimplerEnv WidowX基准上，VANE相比对应的TTT基线平均成功率提升3.2个百分点；在Google Robot上的实验表明部署时的性能提升依赖于具体任务和机器人体型。

Conclusion: VANE展示了一种受限的、基于证据的VLA策略交互式适应方法，能够在部署期间有效且可靠地适应，为测试时训练在闭环操作中的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) offers a lightweight way to adapt vision--language--action (VLA) policies from unlabeled deployment streams, but it remains difficult to use reliably in closed-loop manipulation. A shared adaptation space can mix incompatible task corrections, while an online update can alter subsequent actions before its consequences are known. We introduce a reliable TTT framework for VLA policies (VANE). VANE conditions prompt adaptation on the current vision--language context and learns from the future visual consequences of executed actions. Candidate updates are isolated from the live policy, evaluated on subsequent observations, and committed only when supported by future evidence, making adaptation selective and reversible. On SimplerEnv WidowX, VANE improves average success by $3.2$ percentage points over the corresponding TTT baseline. Results on Google Robot further show that deployment-time gains remain task- and embodiment-dependent. Together, these results demonstrate a constrained, evidence-based approach to adapting VLA policies during interaction.

</details>


### [205] [Graph-Guided Safe Diffuser: Topological Graph Guidance for Safe Diffusion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09484)
*Nakgyu Yang,KwangBin Lee,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G2SD（图引导的安全扩散器）框架，通过将数据流形抽象为潜在图结构，在高层次进行拓扑图规划，并以此引导低层次扩散模型生成连续轨迹，从而在结构层面保障安全性，避免扩散规划器因流形断裂导致的运动学不可行问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散规划器通过推断时引导来强制执行安全性，但这种轨迹变形方式容易导致流形断裂，从而降低运动学可行性。因此需要一种从结构层面保障安全性的方法。

Method: G2SD采用分层框架：高层使用拓扑图规划器在学习的潜在图上进行规划，低层扩散模型生成连续轨迹，并以高层规划器选出的图节点表示为条件进行引导。同时提供理论分析，说明扩散规划器中流形断裂发生的条件，并证明G2SD通过增加段数可降低约束违反概率。

Result: 在Maze2D导航任务中，G2SD将无碰撞到达目标率从40-50%提升至98%；在运动控制任务中也取得了更优的任务得分，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: G2SD通过将安全约束从轨迹层面提升到结构层面（图规划），有效避免了流形断裂问题，在安全性和运动学可行性之间取得了更好的平衡，为扩散规划器提供了一种新的安全保障范式。

Abstract: Many diffusion-based planners enforce safety through inference-time guidance, but such interleaved trajectory deformations often degrade kinematic feasibility due to manifold rupture. We propose Graph-Guided Safe Diffuser (G2SD), a hierarchical framework that leverages a high-level topological graph planner to guide a low-level diffusion model. G2SD enforces safety at a structural level by abstracting the data manifold into a learned latent graph, on which high-level planning is performed. Continuous trajectories are generated by diffusion planners, which are conditioned on the graph node representations selected by the high-level planner. Theoretical analyses demonstrate conditions under which manifold rupture occurs in diffusion planners, and show that G2SD improves safety by reducing the constraint violation probability as the number of segments increases. Experiments demonstrate that G2SD substantially outperforms baselines, increasing goal-reaching rate without any collision from 40-50% to 98% in Maze2D navigation and also achieving superior task scores in locomotion.

</details>


### [206] [Rethink Before You Execute: Adaptive Execution for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09492)
*Feng Ye,Yiming Zhao,Yong Yu,Hongxu Zhou,Yong Pan,Yuan Xue,Peng Jia,Chuanmin Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TempoWAM，一种轻量级即插即用的执行方案，通过在线监控任务进度来自适应决定何时重新规划，改善世界动作模型（WAM）在执行中固定执行时长与动态执行场景不匹配的问题，在多个基准和真实机器人上取得了效率与成功率上的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM在执行时使用固定的动作块前缀长度后重新规划，但不同任务阶段下动作块的可靠性差异很大，固定的重新规划时机无法适应执行过程中动态变化的环境，导致效率与成功率之间的权衡不佳。

Method: TempoWAM包含两个核心组件：1）递归进度监控器（Recurrent Progress Monitor），从当前观测、任务指令、剩余动作和执行历史中估计任务进度；2）自适应执行协议（Adaptive Execution Protocol），评估当前动作块是否在推进任务，以决定是否重新规划。协议通过任务相关校准因子进行校准，并支持在线自适应以弥合训练与部署之间的差距。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上的实验表明，TempoWAM持续改善了WAM执行的效率-成功率权衡。在真实机器人上，简单任务中WAM推理次数减少26.9%且保持成功率，困难任务中成功率提升13.3个百分点。

Conclusion: TempoWAM通过在线监控完成进度来替代固定的重新规划频率，有效解决了WAM中固定执行时长与实际执行动态不匹配的问题，验证了自适应规划时机对提升机器人执行性能的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future actions and the evolution of the environment. At each inference, a WAM generates a chunk of actions and the robot executes a fixed prefix before replanning. We argue that this fixed execution horizon is poorly matched to execution dynamics: the chunk reliability varies across task stages, so when to replan depends on the result of accumulated execution, not on the step counts. We propose TempoWAM (Timing Execution by Monitoring Progress Online), a lightweight plug-and-play execution scheme for WAMs. A Recurrent Progress Monitor first estimates task progress from the current observation, task instruction, remaining actions, and execution history; and an Adaptive Execution Protocol then evaluates whether the chunk is advancing the task to decide if replanning is needed. To bridge the training-deployment gap, the protocol is calibrated by a task-dependent calibration factor with online adaptation. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks show that TempoWAM consistently improves the efficiency-success trade-off of WAM execution. On real robots, it reduces WAM inferences by 26.9% on easy tasks while maintaining success, and improves success by 13.3 points on difficult tasks.

</details>


### [207] [Tether-Inertial Localization for Planetary Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09515)
*Dielof van Loon,Anton Bredenbeck,Lennart Puck,Martin Azkarate,Salua Hamaza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向系留无人机（TUAV）的绳索-惯性定位方法，利用绳索长度和角度测量来估计无人机相对基站的位姿，通过解析悬链线模型与高斯过程残差补偿相结合，实现了平均RMSE低至5.2cm的定位精度，比现有技术水平提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系留无人机在行星探测中具有潜力（如Ingenuity直升机），但有效载荷有限限制了飞行时间和计算能力。通过绳索连接可将电池和计算负担转移到基站，同时绳索本身可被利用来实现无漂移定位。然而现有定位方法精度不足，需要一种高效且准确的绳索辅助定位方法。

Method: 提出Tether-Inertial Localization方法，将绳索长度和角度测量与惯性数据相结合，使用计算高效的解析悬链线模型估计UAV位置，并通过高斯过程（GP）残差补偿来修正系统传感器误差和模型局限。

Result: 在圆形、三角形和八字形轨迹上进行了实验验证，绳索长度达4.5m，总飞行时间37分钟。仅使用绳索位置估计进行反馈时，解析悬链线模型平均RMSE为7.4cm，经GP残差补偿后降至5.2cm，比现有技术水平高出一个数量级。

Conclusion: Tether-Inertial Localization被证实是系留无人机上基于视觉和GNSS定位方法的实用替代方案，兼具高精度和低计算成本的优势。

Abstract: Recent developments in planetary exploration have shown the potential of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), such as the Ingenuity helicopter that provided valuable mapping data. However, limited payload capabilities constrain the flight times and compute available for localization, which restrict their applicability. By providing a tethered connection, issues such as battery and computational constraints are offloaded to the base rover. At the same time, the cable can be exploited for non-drifting localization. This work presents a novel Tether-Inertial Localization approach that uses tether length and angle measurements to estimate the UAV position relative to its base. The method combines a computationally efficient analytical catenary model with a Gaussian Process (GP) residual error compensation. This accounts for systematic sensor inaccuracies and model limitations. Experimental validation across circular, triangular, and figure-eight trajectories with tether lengths up to 4.5 m and a total flight time of 37 minutes demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed approach. Using only tether-based position estimates for feedback, the analytical catenary model achieves an average RMSE of 7.4 cm, which is further reduced to 5.2 cm through GP-based residual compensation, one order of magnitude better than the state-of-the-art. These results establish Tether-Inertial Localization as a practical alternative to vision- and GNSS-based localization for Tethered Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (TUAVs).

</details>


### [208] [HarnessWAM: Bridging Prediction and Deliberation in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09516)
*Zhaopeng Gu,Bingke Zhu,Tianxi Lin,Guibo Zhu,Yingying Chen,Kai Wang,Tingyu Yuan,Chaoyang Zhao,Zhaowen Li,Peng Su,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessWAM框架，通过视觉语言模型驱动的任务管理器维护场景信念和结构化任务图，结合能力约束的可执行空间投影和事件驱动的双时间尺度反馈循环，弥补World Action Models在全局规划、状态维护、执行验证和失败恢复方面的预测-决策鸿沟，在RoboMemArena和RoboCerebra基准上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的World Action Models在有限时域内进行预测和动作生成，难以应对需要全局规划、跨阶段状态维护、执行验证和失败恢复的复杂具身任务。作者将此不匹配定义为WAMs的预测-决策鸿沟，并希望通过模型外部机制弥补这一缺陷。

Method: HarnessWAM包含三个核心组件：1）基于VLM的任务管理器，维护证据支撑的场景信念和结构化任务图；2）能力条件化的可执行空间投影，将开放语义计划约束为满足任务依赖、具身状态约束和底层WAM能力边界的原子技能序列；3）事件驱动的双时间尺度反馈循环，包含高频进度估计器和在关键里程碑处进行深度决策的任务管理器，支持任务推进、获取额外观察、计划修订和局部恢复。

Result: 在RoboMemArena上全任务成功率达59.6%，子任务成功率达69.9%；在RoboCerebra Ideal上成功率达23.7%，均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 模型外部的结构化状态维护和闭环智能体决策可以有效扩展WAMs的局部控制能力，实现可规划、可验证、可恢复的具身任务执行。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly learn environmental dynamics and robot actions, introducing priors over physical evolution into embodied control. However, finite-horizon prediction and action generation are insufficient for complex embodied tasks that require global planning, cross-stage state maintenance, execution verification, and failure recovery. We refer to this mismatch as the prediction-deliberation gap of WAMs. To address this gap, we propose HarnessWAM, an agentic framework for WAMs. HarnessWAM employs a vision-language-model-based Task Manager to maintain an evidence-grounded scene belief and a structured task graph. A capability-conditioned executable-space projection further constrains open-ended semantic plans into sequences of atomic skills that satisfy task dependencies, embodiment-state constraints, and the capability boundary of the underlying WAM. During execution, HarnessWAM operates through an event-driven, dual-timescale feedback loop: a lightweight progress estimator continuously provides high-frequency execution evidence, while the Task Manager deliberates at salient milestones by jointly considering the current observation, task state, and interaction history to determine whether to advance the task, acquire additional observations, revise the plan, or initiate local recovery. This mechanism enables the robot to recover its state after a subtask failure and resume execution without discarding previously acquired scene knowledge. HarnessWAM achieves state-of-the-art full-task and subtask success rates of 59.6% and 69.9% on RoboMemArena, and an SR of 23.7% on RoboCerebra Ideal. These results demonstrate that model-external structured state maintenance and closed-loop agentic decision making can effectively extend the local control capabilities of WAMs into embodied task execution that is plannable, verifiable, and recoverable.

</details>


### [209] [Robotic Fabric Alignment System for Sewing Using Global Local Weighted ICP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09528)
*Wenbo Dong,Dipankar Bhattacharya Member,Kai Tang,Akinari Kobayashi,Fuyuki Tokuda,Akira Seino,Norman C. Tien,Kazuhiro Kosuge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型自动化织物对齐系统，采用全局局部加权迭代最近点（GLW-ICP）方法，在遮挡和非遮挡条件下均能实现毫米级对齐精度，适用于缝纫前的布料对齐场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 织物对齐是缝纫前必须执行的关键步骤，传统方法难以同时兼顾全局边缘和局部缝线的精确对齐，尤其在遮挡条件下表现不佳，因此需要一种更鲁棒的对齐方法。

Method: 系统通过GLW-ICP方法估计上下织物的位姿。该方法一方面全局对齐织物边缘点与CAD模型点，另一方面局部对齐缝线点与CAD模型对应点，同时移除遮挡区域中的未匹配点，实现全局边缘与局部缝线的联合鲁棒对齐。

Result: 在多种织物形状的真实世界实验中，系统在遮挡和非遮挡条件下均一致地实现了毫米级对齐精度，验证了其有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该系统在真实场景中展现了良好的对齐性能，可有效应用于自动化织物对齐，为缝纫自动化提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Accurate fabric alignment is a critical step that must be performed before sewing. This paper presents a novel automated fabric alignment system. The system estimates the poses of top and bottom fabric panels, lying flat and wrinkle-free in arbitrary positions, using a new Global Local Weighted Iterative Closest Point (GLW-ICP) method. The system then manipulates the top panel to achieve precise alignment at both edges and sewing lines. Unlike conventional approaches, GLW-ICP robustly aligns both global edges and local sewing lines by globally aligning fabric edge points and locally aligning sewing line points to their corresponding CAD model points, while removing unmatched points in occluded regions. Real-world experiments with various fabric shapes show that the system consistently achieves millimeter-level alignment accuracy under both occlusion and non-occlusion conditions, demonstrating its effectiveness and suitability for automated fabric alignment in practical scenarios.

</details>


### [210] [Model-Based Systems Engineering Framework for SysML-Driven Design of Autonomous UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09547)
*Deekshitha Angadi,Naveena Budda,Vikas Agarwal,Mohamed Samshad,Bharath Kumar Suryadevara,Narsimlu Kemsaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模型的系统工程（MBSE）框架，用于自主无人机的SysML驱动开发。该框架通过四个连接层（利益相关者需求、功能分解、逻辑架构和物理/软件分配）来结构化无人机开发过程，并将逻辑架构系统性地映射到ROS 2软件架构。作者使用多种SysML图表捕获无人机的功能、结构、行为和性能方面，并通过典型任务场景（自主起飞、航点导航、悬停稳定、避障、返航和紧急处理）加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多自主无人机开发工作流程仍依赖文档中心的需求、分离的架构描述和软件实现工件，导致早期设计中出现歧义、接口不一致和可追溯性薄弱的问题。

Method: 提出MBSE设计框架，以SysML为正式设计主干，通过四个连接层组织无人机开发；使用需求图、活动图、块定义图、内部块图、状态机图和参数图捕获系统不同方面；将逻辑架构映射至ROS 2软件架构（SysML块映射到ROS 2节点，流端口映射到话题，请求-响应映射到服务，目标导向行为映射到动作）。

Result: 该框架在设计层面通过代表性的自主无人机任务场景（自主起飞、航点导航、悬停稳定、避障、返航和紧急处理）加以展示，所生成的模型支持需求分配、接口定义、子系统职责分配以及仿真或物理部署前的验证规划。

Conclusion: 该系统化框架解决了无人机开发中早期设计阶段的歧义和可追溯性问题，为从高层需求到ROS 2实现的无缝过渡提供了桥梁，但论文中描述目前仅限于设计层面，尚未涉及仿真或物理部署验证。

Abstract: Autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are complex cyber-physical systems that require the coordinated integration of flight control, navigation, perception, communication, power management, and mission-level decision-making under safety, timing, and reliability constraints. However, many autonomous UAV development workflows still rely on document-centric requirements, separated architectural descriptions, and software implementation artifacts, which can lead to ambiguity, interface inconsistencies, and weak traceability during early design. This paper presents a Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE) design framework for the SysML-driven development of autonomous UAVs. The proposed framework uses the Systems Modeling Language (SysML) as a formal design backbone to structure UAV development across four connected layers: stakeholder requirements, functional decomposition, logical architecture, and physical/software allocation. SysML requirement diagrams, activity diagrams, block definition diagrams, internal block diagrams, state machine diagrams, and parametric diagrams are used to capture the functional, structural, behavioral, interface, and performance aspects of the UAV system. The logical architecture is then systematically mapped to a Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) software architecture by relating SysML blocks to ROS 2 nodes, flow ports and connectors to topics, request-response interactions to services, and goal-oriented behaviors to actions. The framework is illustrated at the design level using representative autonomous UAV mission scenarios, including autonomous take-off, waypoint navigation, hover stabilization, obstacle avoidance, return-to-home, and emergency handling. The resulting model supports requirement allocation, interface definition, subsystem responsibility assignment, and verification planning before simulation or physical deployment.

</details>


### [211] [TDMA Based Communications Control Co-Design for Cooperative Carrying: Delay Calibration and Sampling-Rate Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09556)
*Zahra Seifaei,Maximilian Luebke,Torsten Reissland,Danial Dehghani,Norman Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多机器人协作运输中，通过自适应采样时间调整和领导者轮换策略，在保持控制性能的同时提高无线通信负载分配的公平性。实验基于MuJoCo物理仿真和真实无线建模，对比了固定采样/静态领导、动态采样/静态领导、动态采样/轮换领导三种方案，结果表明动态采样有效降低通信开销且不影响控制性能，领导者轮换显著提升空口时间分配的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队协作运输面临核心挑战：如何在保持稳定控制的同时维持通信效率。现有方法未能同时兼顾通信负载的公平分配和控制性能的保持，亟需一种兼顾两者的策略方案。

Method: 采用MuJoCo物理仿真结合真实无线建模（包括TDMA、MAC、抖动、排队和丢包），设计三种控制方法对比实验：固定采样+静态领导、动态采样+静态领导、动态采样+轮换领导，通过测量网络延迟自适应调整采样时间，并实施领导者轮换策略。

Result: 动态采样在不牺牲控制性能的前提下有效减少通信开销；领导者轮换显著提升无线空口时间分配的公平性；两种策略叠加对团队运输能力的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该工作是首批在物理真实的协作机器人场景中联合研究动态采样、领导者轮换和无线协议交互的工作之一，为通信资源受限的现实环境中部署协调机器人团队提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Multi robot teams performing cooperative transportation face a fundamental challenge: maintaining stable control while keeping communications efficient. This paper investigates how adaptive sampling time adjustment informed by measured network delay and strategic leader rotation can distribute wireless load fairly across the team. We use physics based simulation in MuJoCo with realistic wireless modeling, including time division multiple access, medium access control, jitter, queueing, and packet loss, to evaluate three control approaches: fixed sampling with static leadership, dynamic sampling with static leadership, and dynamic sampling with rotating leadership. Our results reveal an important trade off: dynamic sampling effectively reduces communications overhead without compromising control performance, while rotating the leader role meaningfully improves how fairly airtime is distributed all with negligible impact on the team carrying ability. to the best of our knowledge, being among the first to jointly examine dynamic sampling, rotating leadership, and wireless protocol interactions in physicsrealistic multi robot cooperation, this work provides practical guidance for deploying coordinated robotic teams in real world settings where communications resources are limited.

</details>


### [212] [FactorDrive: Adaptive Multi-Step Reasoning Driven by Planning-Critical Factors for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09591)
*Guolei Huang,Tengfei She,Yuxuan Lu,Yao Huang,Yuqi Ye,Yongjun Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FactorDrive，一个端到端自动驾驶框架，通过规划关键因子（PCFs）驱动自适应多步推理，结合PCF-CoT数据集和QS-GRPO优化算法，在nuScenes和NAVSIM基准上取得SOTA规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自动驾驶方法未能充分将空间-物理证据融入规划推理，推理适应性粗粒度且无法匹配场景特定规划需求，同时推理路径优化在自动驾驶后训练中尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 大规模驾驶领域指令微调建立基础驾驶知识；2) 构建PCF-CoT数据集，将规划推理基于轨迹相关空间-物理证据，并按场景特定PCFs组织推理路径；3) 提出QS-GRPO算法，利用轨迹级规划奖励引导MCTS探索更高质量的推理路径，并通过GRPO优化策略。

Result: 在开放环基准nuScenes和闭环导向基准NAVSIM上的大量实验表明，FactorDrive取得了最先进的规划性能。

Conclusion: 通过将空间-物理证据融入推理并通过搜索优化推理路径，FactorDrive实现了自适应、场景特定的多步推理规划，有效提升了端到端自动驾驶的轨迹规划质量。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced scene understanding and enabled explicit reasoning in end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing methods insufficiently integrate spatial-physical evidence into planning reasoning, while reasoning adaptation remains coarse-grained and falls short of scene-specific planning demands. Furthermore, reasoning-path optimization for higher planning quality remains largely unexplored in autonomous-driving post-training. To address these limitations, we propose FactorDrive, an end-to-end autonomous driving framework for adaptive multi-step reasoning driven by planning-critical factors (PCFs). We first perform large-scale driving-domain instruction tuning to establish foundational driving knowledge. Building on this foundation, we construct PCF-CoT, a chain-of-thought (CoT) dataset that grounds planning reasoning in trajectory-relevant spatial-physical evidence and organizes reasoning around scene-specific PCFs, enabling the composition and depth of reasoning paths to adapt to different planning demands. We further introduce Quality Search-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization (QS-GRPO), which guides Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with trajectory-level planning rewards to discover reasoning paths with higher planning quality and uses the resulting responses to optimize the policy through GRPO, thereby improving trajectory planning performance. Extensive experiments on both open-loop (nuScenes) and closed-loop-oriented (NAVSIM) benchmarks demonstrate that FactorDrive achieves state-of-the-art planning performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [Towards an Argumentative Foundation for Evaluative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07473)
*Xiang Yin,Tim Miller,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*
